# KIP's Room Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** KIP's room becomes yours to decorate and KIP can wear things. Every piece is earned from your own badges,
recaps read and buddy cheers; nothing is ever sold. This plan ships **release 1** (B1): the 17 starter items, the 59
base items unlocked at level I–II / any family I–II / 4 recaps / 5 cheers, the Cottage set, decorate mode, worn items
everywhere KIP appears, unlock evaluation with the backfill, and the toasts.

**Architecture:**
- **Engine prep** (Task 1). Spec A's room engine (`mobile/src/lib/room/*`, built on this branch) keeps its contract.
  Its ten starter ids move to the catalogue's id scheme, `kipSeatFor` reads only a desk-slot item, `RoomView` gains a
  `paused` prop, and `drawableLoadout` draws any id without art as the starter piece.
- **Backend** (Tasks 2–6):
  - `backend/src/cosmetics/catalogue.ts`: every base item and all six sets, with `COSMETICS_RELEASE = 1` and
    `RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT`;
  - a generated `mobile/src/lib/room/catalogue.json` (release ≤ 1 only), kept current by a backend test;
  - two tables (`CosmeticUnlock`, `CosmeticLoadout`), `grantCosmetics` (pure rules over achievements, opened weekly
    recaps and cheers; backfill decided by time), its three hooks and the catch-up GET;
  - `GET/PUT /me/cosmetics`, `POST /me/cosmetics/seen`;
  - `Person.kipWorn`, the camp's `faceWorn` and the stories' `checkedInKipWorn`.
- **Mobile data** (Tasks 7–8): worn art on the KIP grid (`lib/kip/wornArt.ts`) drawn by `kipGrid`; the API client,
  `roomCopy.ts`, the cosmetics store and `useMyKip()` (colour + worn), which replaces the colour-only store.
- **Art** (Tasks 9–11): `lib/room/items/{slot}.ts` (the v2 primitives, board sprites placed at the engine's slot
  anchors), tray icons, the Cottage set, the perf test and the bundle-size script.
- **Surfaces** (Task 12): the KIP page draws your saved room and worn items; every `Kip`, `PersonKip` and `KipHead`
  passes worn items.
- **Decorate mode** (Tasks 13–14): the `DecorateRoom` modal route, a pure draft reducer, hotspots, tray, tiles, sets,
  Undo, Cancel and Done with the 403 / 409 refresh.
- **Announcements** (Task 15): the celebration line and Decorate button, the foreground toast, the backfill summary.
- **Verification** (Task 16): full suites, final review, sync with a DB backup and `prisma migrate deploy`, the
  simulator check.

**Tech Stack:**
- Backend: Express 5, Prisma 6 / Postgres, Jest with supertest, ts-node for scripts.
- Mobile: Expo 57 / React Native 0.86 with NativeWind, React Navigation 7, Reanimated 4,
  `@shopify/react-native-skia` 2.6.2, `react-native-svg`, `expo-secure-store` (all installed). Tests use Jest 30 with
  React Native Testing Library.
- New devDependency (mobile): `esbuild`, only for `scripts/room-art-size.mjs` (Task 11).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-kip-room-design.md` (Spec B, binding).
- Its sibling `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-kip-coach-design.md` (Spec A) is **built** on this branch (draft PR
  #65): the KIP page, `lib/room/*`, `RoomView`, the poses, `CoachStatusProvider`. This plan builds against that real
  code, not the spec's assumptions; where they differ, a ruling below says which way and why.
- Visual sources in `docs/design/kip-coach/project/`:
  - `K4b.dc.html` (decorate mode: hotspots, tray, info line, sets, "How new things arrive");
  - `CatWalls`, `CatWindow`, `CatDesk`, `CatFloor`, `CatOutfit` `.dc.html` (every base item, its flags and its unlock
    line; the rect lists are the item sprites);
  - `SetsV2.dc.html`, `SetCottage.dc.html` (v2 art quality), with the generators `lib.js`, `lib2.js`, `sets2.js`
    (Cottage is `sets2.js:235-298`).
- Release-2 art (levels III–V, 8 / 12 recaps, 10 / 15 cheers, the five other sets) is **out of this plan**. The data
  model, the catalogue rows, the export filter and the backfill-by-time rule all support it.

## Global Constraints

- **Branch:** `feature/kip-room`, in the worktree
  `/Users/tushar/Documents/PROJECTS/Biometrics/.claude/worktrees/social-tab`, cut from `feature/kip-coach-spec` head
  `2a65c29` (Spec A built). This plan's commit sits on top.
  - Never switch branches, never push to main, never force-push, never bare `git stash`.
- **Commits:** plain `git commit -m "…"` with **no `Co-Authored-By` trailer and no mention of Claude, Cursor or AI**
  (owner rule; it applies to every subagent). A PR body carries no attribution footer either.
- **Subagents** run on model **opus**.
- **Every task keeps both full suites green at its commit.** Run the touched tests while working. Before the commit,
  run the full mobile suite, both typechecks and, for a task that touches `backend/`, the full backend suite.
- **Mobile commands**, from `mobile/`:
  - `export PATH=/Users/tushar/.nvm/versions/node/v24.21.0/bin:$PATH`
  - Tests: `node node_modules/.bin/jest --forceExit <paths>`. With no paths it runs the full suite.
  - Typecheck: `node node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit --types jest,node --pretty false | grep -c "error TS"`.
    - The baseline, **measured on this branch on 2026-10-14, is 10**: `__tests__/api/client.test.tsx` (2),
      `FactorBar.test.tsx` (1), `ScoreRing.test.tsx` (1), `MetricDetailScreen.test.tsx` (5) and `App.tsx` (1).
    - No task may add an error. Record any change of the count in the ledger.
- **Backend commands**, from `backend/`. Never run two backend test runs at once, never point tests at the dev
  `biometrics` DB, and never print the database URL.
  - Tests go only through `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-kip-room/backend-jest.sh <paths>` (Task 0 copies it). It uses
    `TEST_DATABASE_URL` from `~/dev/biometrics-run/backend/.env` and Redis db 1.
  - A suite that fails or SIGSEGVs under full-suite load is rerun alone before it is treated as real.
  - Typecheck: `node node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit -p .` must report **0** errors. After a schema or migration
    change, run `DATABASE_URL=postgresql://x@localhost/x npx prisma generate` first (generate needs the variable but
    never connects).
  - Scripts run with `node node_modules/.bin/ts-node --project tsconfig.evals.json scripts/<name>.ts`.
- **Buttons:**
  - Every button is `<Button>` from `mobile/src/components/ui/button.tsx`, with `rounded-lg`. No pills.
  - A raw `Pressable` with `accessibilityRole="button"` or `"link"` needs an entry in
    `mobile/__tests__/conventions/buttons.test.ts`, with a count and a reason, **in the task that adds it**. Spec B adds
    exactly two kinds: the decorate hotspot tag and the tray tile (spec §6.2, §6.3), one entry each.
  - An entry whose site disappears is removed **in the task that removes it**.
- **Type:** A4 tokens only (`text-score`, `text-number`, `text-display`, `text-heading`, `text-headline`,
  `text-body`, `text-caption`, `text-fine`, `text-page-title`, `text-label`). Pixel labels use `font-pixel text-label`.
  No inline `fontSize` / `fontFamily`, no arbitrary `text-[..px]`. `__tests__/conventions/typography.test.ts` must stay
  green with its EXEMPT counts unchanged (`CelebrationModal.tsx` is exempt today; its new line uses tokens).
- **Copy:**
  - Every new user-visible string lives in `mobile/src/lib/room/roomCopy.ts` (spec §10).
  - Spoken labels never contain "·" (U+00B7) or "−" (U+2212): item names and unlock lines have spoken forms.
  - The product name is **KIP** in capitals. `__tests__/conventions/kipCopy.test.ts` stays green (ruling 14).
- **Privacy and logging (spec §2, §10):**
  - Never log user IDs together with item ids, loadouts or counter values. Cosmetics logs carry an event name and a
    count only.
  - Only worn ids are ever shared with buddies: never the room, the owned list, `fresh` or the counters.
  - Nothing cosmetic reaches the model.
- **Earn only (spec §0):** nothing is sold; no route, copy or tile mentions a price. An unlock row is never deleted
  except by account deletion and the test-fixture purge (Task 3's guard).
- **Dates:** never `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')` in the app. The backend constant uses a full ISO instant with `Z`.
- **Frozen files:** `components/social/CampScene.tsx` and `CampBanner.tsx` change only as ruling 13 allows (the worn
  prop), nothing else.
- **Shared test fixtures** live in `mobile/jest-mocks/` and `backend/tests/**/helpers.ts`, never as `__tests__` files.
- **Skia in Jest:** `@shopify/react-native-skia` is mocked by `mobile/jest-mocks/skia.js`; `RoomView` has the
  stand-in `jest-mocks/RoomView.js`. No test loads the real Skia module.

## Plan rulings (made while writing the plan)

1. **Item ids follow the catalogue scheme `{prefix}.{boardKey}`** (spec §1's examples `wall.shoji`, `floor.oakDark`,
   `out.crown`, `set.japanese.desk`). Prefixes: `wall`, `floor`, `art`, `win`, `shelf`, `desk`, `item` (desk_item),
   `plant`, `lamp`, `corner`, `rug`, `pet`, `out` (every worn slot), `set.{setId}.{slot}`. The board key is the Cat
   board's first field; the floors' `checkerF` / `concreteF` / `neonF` drop the `F`.
   - Spec A's ten starter ids (`plain_slate`, `dark_oak`, …) were never stored or sent anywhere, so Task 1 renames
     them: `wall.plain`, `floor.oakDark`, `win.plain`, `art.calendar`, `shelf.books`, `rug.slate`, `desk.monitor`,
     `item.mug`, `lamp.amber`, `plant.fern`.
2. **The whole catalogue is data now; only release 1 ships.** `catalogue.ts` holds all 134 base items (Cat boards,
   every flag and rule) and all six sets. Items unlocked at a family level I–II, `anyFamily` I–II, 4 recaps or 5 cheers
   (and the 17 starters) are `release: 1`; the rest are `release: 2`. B1 = 76 base + 11 Cottage-only pieces = 87 items.
   - Sets reuse base items where a board names one (table in Task 2). Cottage reuses `plant.fern` and `out.beanie`;
     its other eleven room pieces are set-only (`set.cottage.{slot}`). A release-1 set may not reuse a release-2 item
     (Cottage's "light oak boards" is therefore its own floor, not the release-2 `floor.oakLight`).
   - A set's "{n} pieces" is computed: 12 room pieces plus its worn pieces (Cottage 13, as on the board).
   - Flags of the five release-2 sets' own pieces are provisional; only ids are permanent, so B2 may set them.
3. **Set pieces are re-composed at the engine's slot anchors.** `sets2.js` draws each set as one scene on its own
   layout (Cottage's table is on the right, its stove mid-room). Spec A's engine places items by `SLOT_ANCHORS` with
   KIP's seat fixed, so each Cottage piece is redrawn inside its own slot's anchor with the board's palette, shapes,
   glows and loops. Specifically:
   - the Cottage **desk** piece is the farmhouse table, its chair and the sage typewriter (where the monitor stands);
     its **desk_item** is the rose teapot;
   - the Cottage **lamp** is the fairy lights along the ceiling beam (the wallpaper draws the beam). It is the one
     item allowed outside its anchor (`ANCHOR_EXEMPT`, with a reason);
   - the Cottage **corner** is a slim wood stove (14 cells wide) with the fire loop.
4. **Worn items never animate in v1.** The party hat's board ANIM flag is dropped: KIP's grid loops only for `type`,
   and a worn loop would re-record KIP's layer for every pose. The catalogue test pins `frames` to room slots.
5. **Worn drawing order on the 16 × 16 grid:** body → head → face → neck → pose arms (empty body cells only, painted
   over a neck item) → ears → held item. So headphones sit over a hat band (spec §7) and a pose's arms draw over a
   scarf; worn cells move with the body's posture (tall, squash, tilt, hop).
6. **`useMyKip()` replaces the colour-only store** (spec §7). `lib/kip/myKipColour.ts` is renamed `lib/kip/myKip.ts`
   and holds `{ colour, worn }`; `useMyKipColour()` stays as a selector of the same store, so colour-only callers
   (badges, recaps) change only their import path. `MyKipColourScope` becomes `MyKipScope`, which loads the colour
   and the cosmetics on sign-in and forgets both on sign-out.
7. **The cheer hook lives in `sendSticker`** (`buddies/stickers.ts`), not only the sticker route. Every send path
   (the sticker route, the thread, the timeline, a story reply, the inbox) goes through it, and spec §3.2 counts
   "any sticker sent to a buddy".
8. **The cheer scan is bounded at 90 rows.** At most `STICKERS_PER_BUDDY_PER_DAY` (5, a race may let a 6th through)
   stickers share a buddy-day, so the oldest 90 sent stickers always hold the 15 earliest buddy-days.
9. **`RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT[1]` is `2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z`** (the migration's date). OWNER NOTE: set it to the real
   ship date when B1 merges. A wrong date only changes whether early grants come as one summary toast or one toast
   each.
10. **The celebration line matches every level the celebration covers:** pieces whose source is `family:{F}:{l}` or
    `anyFamily:{l}` with `l ≤` the level shown. A jump from no level to II celebrates II once (one modal per family),
    and its level-I pieces would otherwise never be named.
11. **`kipToasted` stores `{ ids, backfill }`** (JSON, SecureStore, try/catch): `ids` are the toasted unseen item ids;
    `backfill` is how many unseen backfill rows the summary toast already covered, so the summary shows once, again
    only when more backfill arrives (the release-2 bump), and resets when they are seen.
12. **The unlock toast is a new `RoomToastHost`**, not `components/ui/toast.tsx`: that toast is pointer-less and pill
    shaped, and this one carries a "Decorate" `Button`. It is `rounded-lg`, slides down, stays 6 s, and is held while a
    modal route or a celebration is up.
13. **The frozen Campfire files get the worn prop.** Spec §7 requires the camp to draw what buddies wear, which
    `CampBanner.tsx` (`faceWorn`) and `CampScene.tsx` (each member's `kipWorn`, your own worn) render. Each file gets
    exactly the prop added to its `PersonKip` calls and the data it reads; nothing else in them changes. OWNER NOTE.
14. **"Cap" is an item name and a retired coach name.** The kipCopy guard flags the string `'Cap'` anywhere in `src`.
    Task 8 adds one exception to that test: the exact string `'Cap'` in `lib/room/roomCopy.ts`, with a reason.
15. **Hotspot tags sit at a fixed cell per slot** (`HOTSPOT_CELLS`, Task 13), chosen from the engine's anchors (K4b's
    tag spots are on the v1 130 × 80 layout), clamped so each tag stays inside the visible crop.
16. **The decorate room is full width and 220 points tall** (the whole 110-cell height at 2 points), cropped with
    `roomCrop` around `FOCUS_BOX`. Every hotspot's slot is then on screen vertically.
17. **Loops pause under decorate mode two ways:** the KIP tab blurs when the modal opens (`useScreenFocused`), and a
    `decorateOpen` flag store sets `RoomView`'s new `paused` prop on the KIP page's room and thumbnail, so they pause
    even if a presentation does not blur the tab.
18. **Locked previews never enter the draft.** The draft holds only owned pieces; a locked item or locked set is a
    display-only overlay. Done therefore always saves owned pieces only (spec §6.4's "reverted to what was there").
19. **Spoken unlock lines** replace the board's "·" and Roman numerals: "Steady bedtime, level 3", "Any family,
    level 4", "Read 8 weekly recaps", "Cheer a buddy 10 times", "In every room from day one". Spoken item names replace
    " · " with ", ".
20. **The Sets tab lists only exported sets** (B1: Cottage). Swatches come from the v2 palettes (`sets2.js` `pal`),
    not K4b's v1 colours: Cottage is plaster `#E9DFC9`, oak beam `#6B4A2F`, sage `#8BA77A`, rose `#E8A0A0`.
21. **Progress lines:** a family rule reads "level II now" or "No level yet"; `anyFamily` reads "highest level II now"
    or "No level yet"; recaps "5 of 8 read"; cheers "3 of 10"; a set's piece uses its set's rule.
22. **Tray icons** are each item's board sprite (or a 16 × 16 surface patch) drawn by `react-native-svg` in a square
    viewBox fitted to the sprite, 48 × 48 points (16 × 16 at 3×). Worn icons are KIP's rows 0–14 wearing the item.
23. **`esbuild` is added** (pinned, devDependency) for `scripts/room-art-size.mjs`; nothing else uses it.
24. **The starter loadout has one source.** The backend's `STARTER_ROOM` is exported in `catalogue.json`; a mobile
    test pins `STARTER_LOADOUT` to it.
25. **An item whose art is missing in this build** (a newer server, a retired piece) is drawn as its slot's starter
    piece (`drawableLoadout`), hidden from the tray, and **kept as stored**: Done sends the stored id back for every
    slot the user did not touch.

## Review Focus

1. **Two devices.** Phone A saves a new wallpaper. Phone B, still on the old version, places a rug and taps Done.
   - Expected: 409; phone B stays in decorate mode with the server's room (A's wallpaper, no rug), the line
     "Something changed on another device. Your room was refreshed." and Done disabled until something changes.
   - Pinned in Task 5 (two concurrent first saves give 200 + 409; an old version is 409) and Task 14 (the 409 path
     replaces the draft and keeps the screen open).
2. **A long-time user's first open after launch.** They have badges from before launch and open a weekly recap
   before ever visiting the KIP tab.
   - Expected: every piece earned by pre-launch rules is stored as `backfill` (the recap hook ran first); one summary
     toast "Your badges unlocked 9 things for KIP's room"; it does not come back on the next foreground; a piece earned
     after launch gets its own toast.
   - Pinned in Task 3 (backfill by time, recap hook first) and Task 15 (summary once, `kipToasted.backfill`).
3. **The server knows an item this build cannot draw** (a newer server, or a retired piece).
   - Expected: the room draws the slot's starter piece; the tray hides the item; a save of other changes keeps the
     stored id; nothing throws.
   - Pinned in Task 1 (`drawableLoadout`), Task 13 (tray filter) and Task 14 (Done keeps untouched stored ids).
4. **A buddy's worn items on a small avatar, or an id from a newer server.** A buddy wears a hat and glasses; the camp
   banner draws them at 26 points; another buddy's `kipWorn.head` is `'out.unknown'`.
   - Expected: at under 32 points only head and ears are drawn; the unknown id is dropped, the rest drawn; nothing
     crashes.
   - Pinned in Task 7 (`wornForSize`, `kipWornOf`) and Task 12 (`CampBanner`, `RequestsList` render).
5. **A grant fails** (DB hiccup) while a sticker is sent or a recap is opened.
   - Expected: the sticker or the open still succeeds (201 / 204); the log line has no user id; the next
     `GET /me/cosmetics` grants what was missed.
   - Pinned in Task 4 (a throwing `cosmeticsHooks.grant` leaves both routes green; GET catches up).

### Task 1: Engine prep: catalogue ids, the desk-slot seat guard, `drawableLoadout`, `RoomView.paused`

Rulings 1, 17 and 25, and three items from Spec A's deferred ledger (`progress.md:86`): `kipSeatFor` must read only a
desk-slot item; `RoomView` needs a `paused` prop; the KIP layer memo already includes the seat (no change, a test pins
it). Nothing visible changes.

**Files:**
- Modify: `mobile/src/lib/room/starterItems.ts`, `mobile/src/lib/room/loadout.ts`, `mobile/src/lib/room/draw.ts`,
  `mobile/src/components/room/RoomView.tsx`, `mobile/jest-mocks/RoomView.js`
- Test: `mobile/__tests__/lib/room.test.ts`, `mobile/__tests__/components/RoomView.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Spec A's `itemArt`, `registerItems`, `SEATS`, `kipSeatFor`, `RoomViewProps`, `STARTER_LOADOUT`.
- Produces:
  - starter ids `wall.plain`, `floor.oakDark`, `win.plain`, `art.calendar`, `shelf.books`, `rug.slate`, `desk.monitor`,
    `item.mug`, `lamp.amber`, `plant.fern`;
  - `kipSeatFor(loadout: RoomLoadout): KipSeat` (standard unless the desk slot holds a desk-slot item with a style);
  - `drawableLoadout(loadout: RoomLoadout): RoomLoadout` (in `draw.ts`);

- [ ] **Step 4: The seat guard and `drawableLoadout` in `src/lib/room/draw.ts`**

```ts
/** The seat from the desk item's style: standard when the desk slot is empty, unknown, or holds another slot's item. */
export function kipSeatFor(loadout: RoomLoadout): KipSeat {
  const art = itemArt(loadout.desk);
  return SEATS[art?.slot === 'desk' ? art.style ?? 'standard' : 'standard'];
}

/**
 * What the room draws for a stored loadout (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §2): an id this build has no art for, or art
 * for another slot, is drawn as the slot's starter piece. The stored loadout itself is never changed (ruling 25).
 */
export function drawableLoadout(loadout: RoomLoadout): RoomLoadout {
  const out = { ...loadout };
  for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) {
    const id = loadout[slot];
    if (id === null) continue;
    if (itemArt(id)?.slot !== slot) out[slot] = STARTER_LOADOUT[slot];
  }
  return out;
}
```

Import `ROOM_SLOTS` from `./slots` and `STARTER_LOADOUT` from `./loadout`. (An empty optional slot stays empty; a
required slot is never null in a stored loadout, the server rejects that.)

```ts
  expect(ruleOf('ANY4')).toEqual({ kind: 'anyFamily', level: 4 });

  }
});

it('never drops or renames an id: every id ever shipped is still here, and every id here is recorded', () => {

    'out.earbuds', 'plant.cactus', 'plant.fern', 'rug.slate', 'shelf.books', 'shelf.minicactus', 'wall.dots', 'wall.plain', 'win.plain',

});

it('names real families and levels, and only the counters and thresholds the boards use', () => {

    if (unlock.kind === 'counter') {

});

it('keeps flags where they belong: loops on room items only, a seat style on desk items only', () => {
  for (const item of COSMETIC_ITEMS) {

    if (item.style) expect(item.slot).toBe('desk');

});

it('gives each set exactly one piece per room slot, worn pieces in worn slots, and lists the set on every piece', () => {
  expect(COSMETIC_SETS.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['cottage', 'japanese', 'homeGym', 'gamerNight', 'stargazer', 'library']);
  for (const set of COSMETIC_SETS) {
    expect(Object.keys(set.room).sort()).toEqual([...ROOM_SLOTS].sort());
    for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) expect(cosmeticItem(set.room[slot])!.slot).toBe(slot);
    for (const [slot, id] of Object.entries(set.worn)) {
      expect(WORN_SLOTS).toContain(slot);
      expect(cosmeticItem(id!)!.slot).toBe(slot);
    }
    for (const id of [...Object.values(set.room), ...Object.values(set.worn)]) {
      const item = cosmeticItem(id!)!;
      expect(item.sets ?? []).toContain(set.id);
      // A set never ships before its pieces can be drawn.
      expect(item.release).toBeLessThanOrEqual(set.release);
    }
  }
  const cottage = COSMETIC_SETS[0]!;
  expect([Object.keys(cottage.room).length + Object.keys(cottage.worn).length, cottage.unlock, cottage.style])
    .toEqual([13, { kind: 'counter', counter: 'recaps_read', atLeast: 4 }, 'standard']);
  expect(COSMETIC_SETS.find((s) => s.id === 'japanese')!.style).toBe('low');
  expect(cosmeticItem('set.japanese.desk')!.style).toBe('low');
});

it('has a launch date for every release the app ships', () => {
  for (let r = 1; r <= COSMETICS_RELEASE; r++) expect(RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT[r as 1]).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `backend/src/cosmetics/catalogue.ts`**

```ts
// KIP's room catalogue (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §1): every item id, its slot, flags and unlock rule, and the six
// theme sets, versioned like the badge catalogue. Rules: an id is permanent (never renamed, reused or deleted; a test
// pins every id ever written here); an item may be retired (kept by owners, never granted again); a raised threshold
// never takes anything away (unlocks are rows). Only items with release <= COSMETICS_RELEASE are exported to the app
// or granted, so nobody owns an item the app cannot draw. The art lives only in the app.

import type { AchievementFamily } from '@prisma/client';

export const COSMETICS_CATALOGUE_VERSION = 1;
export type CosmeticRelease = 1 | 2;
export const COSMETICS_RELEASE: CosmeticRelease = 1;
/** When each art release shipped (§3.1): a rule met before it grants as 'backfill'. Release 2's date is set with the bump. */
export const RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT: Readonly<Partial<Record<CosmeticRelease, Date>>> = Object.freeze({
  1: new Date('2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z'),
});

export const ROOM_SLOTS = ['wallpaper', 'floor', 'art', 'window', 'shelf', 'desk', 'desk_item', 'plant', 'lamp', 'corner', 'rug', 'pet'] as const;
export type RoomSlot = (typeof ROOM_SLOTS)[number];
export const WORN_SLOTS = ['head', 'face', 'neck', 'ears'] as const;
export type WornSlot = (typeof WORN_SLOTS)[number];
export type CosmeticSlot = RoomSlot | WornSlot;
/** Room slots that may be empty (§1); every worn slot may be empty too. */
export const OPTIONAL_ROOM_SLOTS: ReadonlySet<RoomSlot> = new Set<RoomSlot>(['art', 'corner', 'pet']);

export const SET_IDS = ['cottage', 'japanese', 'homeGym', 'gamerNight', 'stargazer', 'library'] as const;
export type SetId = (typeof SET_IDS)[number];
export type Level = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
export type CounterName = 'recaps_read' | 'buddy_cheers';

export type UnlockRule =
  | { kind: 'starter' }
  | { kind: 'family'; family: AchievementFamily; level: Level }
  | { kind: 'anyFamily'; level: Level }
  | { kind: 'counter'; counter: CounterName; atLeast: number }
  | { kind: 'set'; set: SetId };
export type SetRule = Extract<UnlockRule, { kind: 'family' | 'anyFamily' | 'counter' }>;

export interface CosmeticItem {
  id: string;
  slot: CosmeticSlot;
  unlock: UnlockRule;
  release: CosmeticRelease;
  sets?: SetId[];
  tint?: true;
  emissive?: true;
  frames?: 3;
  style?: 'standard' | 'low';
  retired?: true;
}

export interface CosmeticSet {
  id: SetId;
  release: CosmeticRelease;
  unlock: SetRule;
  style: 'standard' | 'low';
  room: Record<RoomSlot, string>;
  worn: Partial<Record<WornSlot, string>>;
}

const FAMILY_CODES: Record<string, AchievementFamily> = {
  SG: 'SLEEP_GOAL', SB: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', ST: 'STEP_GOAL', CI: 'CHECK_IN', BR: 'BEST_RECOVERY_WEEK', EL: 'EVERY_DAY_LOGGED', SM: 'STEADIEST_MONTH',
};

/** A Cat board unlock code: 'S' starter, 'SB3' a family level, 'ANY4', 'RC8' recaps read, 'CB10' buddy cheers. */
export function ruleOf(code: string): UnlockRule {
  if (code === 'S') return { kind: 'starter' };
  const m = /^([A-Z]+)(\d+)$/.exec(code);
  if (!m) throw new Error(`Unknown unlock code ${code}`);
  const [, key, digits] = m;
  const n = Number(digits);
  if (key === 'ANY') return { kind: 'anyFamily', level: n as Level };
  if (key === 'RC') return { kind: 'counter', counter: 'recaps_read', atLeast: n };
  if (key === 'CB') return { kind: 'counter', counter: 'buddy_cheers', atLeast: n };
  const family = FAMILY_CODES[key!];
  if (!family || n < 1 || n > 5) throw new Error(`Unknown unlock code ${code}`);
  return { kind: 'family', family, level: n as Level };
}

// [id, slot, unlock code, flags (T tint, G glow/emissive, A 3-frame loop), release]. Copied from the Cat boards
// (CatWalls.dc.html:87-96, CatWindow.dc.html:77-99, CatDesk.dc.html:76-109, CatFloor.dc.html:78-128,
// CatOutfit.dc.html:68-90). Release 1: starters, family levels I-II, any family I-II, 4 recaps, 5 cheers.
type Row = readonly [string, CosmeticSlot, string, string, CosmeticRelease];
const BASE: readonly Row[] = [
  ['wall.plain', 'wallpaper', 'S', '', 1],
  ['wall.dots', 'wallpaper', 'S', '', 1],
  ['wall.stripes', 'wallpaper', 'SB1', 'T', 1],
  ['wall.brick', 'wallpaper', 'ST2', '', 1],
  ['wall.panel', 'wallpaper', 'RC4', '', 1],
  ['wall.shoji', 'wallpaper', 'SB3', '', 2],
  ['wall.concrete', 'wallpaper', 'ST3', '', 2],
  ['wall.florals', 'wallpaper', 'CB5', '', 1],
  ['wall.checker', 'wallpaper', 'CI2', '', 1],
  ['wall.subway', 'wallpaper', 'EL2', '', 1],
  ['wall.neon', 'wallpaper', 'CI4', 'G', 2],
  ['wall.stars', 'wallpaper', 'SM4', 'GA', 2],
  ['floor.oakDark', 'floor', 'S', '', 1],
  ['floor.oakLight', 'floor', 'RC8', '', 2],
  ['floor.tatami', 'floor', 'SB3', '', 2],
  ['floor.tiles', 'floor', 'EL1', '', 1],
  ['floor.carpet', 'floor', 'SG1', 'T', 1],
  ['floor.checker', 'floor', 'CI3', '', 2],
  ['floor.concrete', 'floor', 'ST2', '', 1],
  ['floor.rubber', 'floor', 'ST3', '', 2],
  ['floor.neon', 'floor', 'CI5', 'G', 2],
  ['art.calendar', 'art', 'S', '', 1],
  ['art.mountain', 'art', 'SG1', '', 1],
  ['art.pennant', 'art', 'CI1', 'T', 1],
  ['art.clock', 'art', 'EL1', 'A', 1],
  ['art.heart', 'art', 'SB2', 'G', 1],
  ['art.motto', 'art', 'SG2', '', 1],
  ['art.map', 'art', 'ST3', '', 2],
  ['art.game', 'art', 'CI3', '', 2],
  ['art.band', 'art', 'EL2', '', 1],
  ['art.cork', 'art', 'RC4', '', 1],
  ['art.wave', 'art', 'SM2', '', 1],
  ['art.medal', 'art', 'BR3', '', 2],
  ['win.plain', 'window', 'S', '', 1],
  ['win.curtains', 'window', 'SB1', 'T', 1],
  ['win.blinds', 'window', 'ST1', '', 1],
  ['win.sticker', 'window', 'CI2', '', 1],
  ['win.pots', 'window', 'SG3', '', 2],
  ['win.fairy', 'window', 'CB5', 'GA', 1],
  ['win.noren', 'window', 'SB3', 'T', 2],
  ['win.chime', 'window', 'BR1', 'A', 1],
  ['shelf.books', 'shelf', 'S', '', 1],
  ['shelf.minicactus', 'shelf', 'S', '', 1],
  ['shelf.trophyB', 'shelf', 'ANY1', '', 1],
  ['shelf.trophyS', 'shelf', 'ANY3', '', 2],
  ['shelf.trophyG', 'shelf', 'ANY5', 'G', 2],
  ['shelf.figurine', 'shelf', 'CB10', 'T', 2],
  ['shelf.globe', 'shelf', 'SM1', 'A', 1],
  ['shelf.luckycat', 'shelf', 'BR2', 'A', 1],
  ['shelf.vinyl', 'shelf', 'EL3', '', 2],
  ['shelf.kendama', 'shelf', 'CI4', '', 2],
  ['desk.monitor', 'desk', 'S', 'GA', 1],
  ['desk.notebook', 'desk', 'S', '', 1],
```

Continue the file:

```ts
// The six theme sets (spec §1 Theme sets; sets2.js). `reuse` maps a slot to the base item the board names for it;
// every other room slot gets a set-only piece `set.{id}.{slot}`. `flags` are those pieces' T/G/A flags.
interface SetDef {
  id: SetId; release: CosmeticRelease; code: string; style: 'standard' | 'low';
  reuse: Partial<Record<RoomSlot, string>>; worn: Partial<Record<WornSlot, string>>; flags: Partial<Record<RoomSlot, string>>;
}
const SET_DEFS: readonly SetDef[] = [
  { id: 'cottage', release: 1, code: 'RC4', style: 'standard', reuse: { plant: 'plant.fern' }, worn: { head: 'out.beanie' }, flags: { lamp: 'GA', corner: 'GA' } },
  { id: 'japanese', release: 2, code: 'SB3', style: 'low', reuse: { wallpaper: 'wall.shoji', floor: 'floor.tatami', plant: 'plant.bonsai', lamp: 'lamp.lantern' }, worn: { head: 'out.hachimaki' }, flags: {} },
  { id: 'homeGym', release: 2, code: 'ST4', style: 'standard', reuse: { floor: 'floor.rubber', window: 'win.blinds', plant: 'plant.snake', corner: 'corner.weights', pet: 'pet.dog' }, worn: { head: 'out.sweatband', neck: 'out.towel' }, flags: { lamp: 'G' } },
  { id: 'gamerNight', release: 2, code: 'ANY4', style: 'standard', reuse: { plant: 'plant.cactus', lamp: 'lamp.neonbolt', corner: 'corner.beanbag', rug: 'rug.rainbow', pet: 'pet.hamster' }, worn: { ears: 'out.phones' }, flags: { wallpaper: 'G', window: 'GA', desk_item: 'G' } },
  { id: 'stargazer', release: 2, code: 'SM5', style: 'standard', reuse: { corner: 'corner.telescope', rug: 'rug.shaggy', pet: 'pet.fish' }, worn: { face: 'out.round' }, flags: { wallpaper: 'GA', lamp: 'G', desk_item: 'G' } },
  { id: 'library', release: 2, code: 'RC12', style: 'standard', reuse: { floor: 'floor.oakDark', art: 'art.map', plant: 'plant.snake', lamp: 'lamp.reading', corner: 'corner.bookcase' }, worn: { face: 'out.round', neck: 'out.bowtie' }, flags: { window: 'A' } },
];

function withFlags(item: CosmeticItem, flags: string): CosmeticItem {
  const worn = (WORN_SLOTS as readonly string[]).includes(item.slot);
  return {
    ...item,
    ...(flags.includes('T') ? { tint: true as const } : {}),
    ...(flags.includes('G') ? { emissive: true as const } : {}),
    ...(flags.includes('A') && !worn ? { frames: 3 as const } : {}),
  };
}

function build(): { items: CosmeticItem[]; sets: CosmeticSet[] } {
  const items = new Map<string, CosmeticItem>();
  for (const [id, slot, code, flags, release] of BASE) {
    items.set(id, withFlags({ id, slot, unlock: ruleOf(code), release, ...(slot === 'desk' ? { style: 'standard' as const } : {}) }, flags));
  }
  const sets: CosmeticSet[] = [];
  for (const def of SET_DEFS) {
    const room = {} as Record<RoomSlot, string>;
    for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) {
      const reused = def.reuse[slot];
      const id = reused ?? `set.${def.id}.${slot}`;
      if (!reused) {
        items.set(id, withFlags({
          id, slot, unlock: { kind: 'set', set: def.id }, release: def.release, ...(slot === 'desk' ? { style: def.style } : {}),
        }, def.flags[slot] ?? ''));
      }
      room[slot] = id;
    }
    for (const id of [...Object.values(room), ...Object.values(def.worn)]) {
      const item = items.get(id!)!;
      items.set(id!, { ...item, sets: [...(item.sets ?? []), def.id] });
    }
    sets.push({ id: def.id, release: def.release, unlock: ruleOf(def.code) as SetRule, style: def.style, room, worn: { ...def.worn } });
  }
  return { items: [...items.values()], sets };
}

const BUILT = build();
export const COSMETIC_ITEMS: readonly CosmeticItem[] = Object.freeze(BUILT.items);
export const COSMETIC_SETS: readonly CosmeticSet[] = Object.freeze(BUILT.sets);
const ITEM_BY_ID = new Map(COSMETIC_ITEMS.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
const SET_BY_ID = new Map(COSMETIC_SETS.map((s) => [s.id, s]));

export function cosmeticItem(id: string): CosmeticItem | undefined {
  return ITEM_BY_ID.get(id);
}
export function cosmeticSet(id: string): CosmeticSet | undefined {
  return SET_BY_ID.get(id as SetId);
}

/** Spec A's starter furnishing (mobile STARTER_LOADOUT is pinned to this by a mobile test). */
export const STARTER_ROOM: Readonly<Record<RoomSlot, string | null>> = Object.freeze({
  wallpaper: 'wall.plain', floor: 'floor.oakDark', art: 'art.calendar', window: 'win.plain', shelf: 'shelf.books',
  desk: 'desk.monitor', desk_item: 'item.mug', plant: 'plant.fern', lamp: 'lamp.amber', corner: null, rug: 'rug.slate', pet: null,
});
/** The starter worn loadout is all empty: Beanie and Earbuds are owned, not worn (§1). */
export const EMPTY_WORN: Readonly<Record<WornSlot, string | null>> = Object.freeze({ head: null, face: null, neck: null, ears: null });
export const STARTER_IDS: readonly string[] = COSMETIC_ITEMS.filter((i) => i.unlock.kind === 'starter').map((i) => i.id);
```

```ts
// The app's copy of the catalogue (spec §1 "Where it lives"): ids, slots, flags, set membership and unlock rules,
// no art, release <= COSMETICS_RELEASE only. tests/cosmetics/export.test.ts fails when the file is stale.
import path from 'path';
import {
  COSMETICS_CATALOGUE_VERSION, COSMETICS_RELEASE, COSMETIC_ITEMS, COSMETIC_SETS, STARTER_ROOM,
  type CosmeticItem, type CosmeticSet, type RoomSlot,
} from './catalogue';

export interface CatalogueExport {
  version: number;
  release: number;
  starterRoom: Record<RoomSlot, string | null>;
  items: CosmeticItem[];
  sets: CosmeticSet[];
}

export const MOBILE_CATALOGUE_PATH = path.join(__dirname, '../../../mobile/src/lib/room/catalogue.json');

export function exportCatalogue(release: number = COSMETICS_RELEASE): CatalogueExport {
  return {
    version: COSMETICS_CATALOGUE_VERSION,
    release,
    starterRoom: { ...STARTER_ROOM },
    items: COSMETIC_ITEMS.filter((i) => i.release <= release),
    sets: COSMETIC_SETS.filter((s) => s.release <= release),
  };
}

export function serialiseExport(e: CatalogueExport): string {
  return `${JSON.stringify(e, null, 2)}\n`;
}
```

`mobile/src/lib/room/catalogue.ts`:

```ts
// The app's copy of the room catalogue (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §1), generated by
// backend/scripts/export-cosmetics.ts: ids, slots, flags, sets and unlock rules for release <= the shipped release.
// Never edited by hand; names and unlock lines are in roomCopy.ts, the art in items/.
import data from './catalogue.json';
import type { ItemId, RoomLoadout } from './loadout';
import { WORN_SLOTS, type RoomSlot, type WornSlot } from './slots';

export type SetId = string;
export type AchievementFamilyName =
  | 'SLEEP_GOAL' | 'STEADY_BEDTIME' | 'STEP_GOAL' | 'CHECK_IN' | 'BEST_RECOVERY_WEEK' | 'EVERY_DAY_LOGGED' | 'STEADIEST_MONTH';
export type UnlockRule =
  | { kind: 'starter' }
  | { kind: 'family'; family: AchievementFamilyName; level: number }
  | { kind: 'anyFamily'; level: number }
  | { kind: 'counter'; counter: 'recaps_read' | 'buddy_cheers'; atLeast: number }
  | { kind: 'set'; set: SetId };

export interface CatalogueItem {
  id: ItemId;
  slot: RoomSlot | WornSlot;
  unlock: UnlockRule;
  release: number;
  sets?: SetId[];
  tint?: boolean;
  emissive?: boolean;
  frames?: 3;
  style?: 'standard' | 'low';
  retired?: boolean;
}

export interface CatalogueSet {
  id: SetId;
  release: number;
  unlock: UnlockRule;
  style: 'standard' | 'low';
  room: Record<RoomSlot, ItemId>;
  worn: Partial<Record<WornSlot, ItemId>>;
}

export const CATALOGUE = data as unknown as {
  version: number;
  release: number;
  starterRoom: RoomLoadout;
  items: CatalogueItem[];
  sets: CatalogueSet[];
};

const BY_ID = new Map(CATALOGUE.items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
const SET_BY_ID = new Map(CATALOGUE.sets.map((s) => [s.id, s]));

export function catalogueItem(id: ItemId | null | undefined): CatalogueItem | undefined {
  return id ? BY_ID.get(id) : undefined;
}
export function catalogueSet(id: SetId): CatalogueSet | undefined {
  return SET_BY_ID.get(id);
}
export function catalogueItemsFor(slot: RoomSlot | WornSlot): CatalogueItem[] {
  return CATALOGUE.items.filter((i) => i.slot === slot);
}
export function isWornSlot(slot: RoomSlot | WornSlot): slot is WornSlot {
  return (WORN_SLOTS as readonly string[]).includes(slot);
}
```

matching §11 tests. Rulings 8 and 9.

- Create: `backend/prisma/migrations/20261015120000_cosmetics/migration.sql`, `backend/src/cosmetics/rules.ts`,

  `backend/tests/cosmetics/rules.test.ts`, `backend/tests/cosmetics/grant.test.ts`,
  `backend/tests/cosmetics/neverRemoved.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's catalogue (`COSMETIC_ITEMS`, `COSMETIC_SETS`, `COSMETICS_RELEASE`, `RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT`,
  `UnlockRule`, `CosmeticItem`, `CosmeticSet`, `CosmeticRelease`); `localCivilDateOrUtc(instant, timeZone: string)`.
- Produces:
  - Prisma models `CosmeticUnlock { userId, itemId, source, unlockedAt, seenAt }` (`@@id([userId, itemId])`) and

  - `facts.ts`: `MAX_RECAPS_COUNTED = 12`, `MAX_CHEERS_COUNTED = 15`, `CHEER_SCAN_ROWS = 90`,

    `countersOf(facts): { recapsRead: number; buddyCheers: number }`;
  - `unlock.ts`: `GrantOptions`, `grantCosmetics(userId, now, opts?): Promise<PlannedGrant[]>`,
    `cosmeticsHooks: { grant(userId, now): Promise<unknown> }`, `grantCosmeticsSafely(userId, now): Promise<void>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Schema and migration**

In `schema.prisma`, add to `model User` (beside `achievements Achievement[]`):

```prisma
  cosmeticUnlocks       CosmeticUnlock[]
  cosmeticLoadout       CosmeticLoadout?
```

and the two models after `Achievement`:

```prisma
/// A room or worn item a user earned (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §2). Starter items are never rows. A row is never
/// deleted except with the account (USER_OWNED_MODELS) or by the test-fixture purge.
model CosmeticUnlock {
  userId     String
  user       User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  /// A catalogue item id; never a set id.
  itemId     String
  /// 'family:STEADY_BEDTIME:3', 'anyFamily:4', 'counter:recaps_read:8', 'set:cottage' or 'backfill'.
  source     String
  unlockedAt DateTime  @default(now())
  seenAt     DateTime?

  @@id([userId, itemId])
  @@index([userId, seenAt])
}

/// What fills each slot. No row means the starter loadout.
model CosmeticLoadout {
  userId    String   @id
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  /// Record<RoomSlot, itemId | null>
  room      Json
  /// Record<WornSlot, itemId | null>
  worn      Json
  /// Optimistic concurrency: every save increments it.
  version   Int      @default(0)
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}
```

`prisma/migrations/20261015120000_cosmetics/migration.sql`:

```sql
-- KIP's room (spec 2026-10-14): earned items and the saved loadout.
CREATE TABLE "CosmeticUnlock" (
    "userId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "itemId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "source" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "unlockedAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    "seenAt" TIMESTAMP(3),

    CONSTRAINT "CosmeticUnlock_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("userId","itemId")
);

CREATE TABLE "CosmeticLoadout" (
    "userId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "room" JSONB NOT NULL,
    "worn" JSONB NOT NULL,
    "version" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    "updatedAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT "CosmeticLoadout_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("userId")
);

CREATE INDEX "CosmeticUnlock_userId_seenAt_idx" ON "CosmeticUnlock"("userId", "seenAt");

ALTER TABLE "CosmeticUnlock" ADD CONSTRAINT "CosmeticUnlock_userId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("userId") REFERENCES "User"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE "CosmeticLoadout" ADD CONSTRAINT "CosmeticLoadout_userId_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("userId") REFERENCES "User"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE;
```

Run `DATABASE_URL=postgresql://x@localhost/x npx prisma generate`. After the first test run below has migrated the test
database, check the hand-written SQL matches the schema (prints no URL):

```bash
DATABASE_URL="$URL" npx prisma migrate diff --from-url "$URL" --to-schema-datamodel prisma/schema.prisma --exit-code > /dev/null 2>&1 && echo "no drift" || echo "DRIFT"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing pure-rule tests** (`tests/cosmetics/rules.test.ts`)

```ts
import { COSMETIC_ITEMS, COSMETIC_SETS, type CosmeticItem, type CosmeticSet } from '../../src/cosmetics/catalogue';
import { BACKFILL, metAt, planGrants, sourceOf, type UnlockFacts } from '../../src/cosmetics/rules';

const t = (iso: string) => new Date(iso);
const NONE: UnlockFacts = { levels: [], recapsReadAt: [], cheersAt: [] };
const SETS = new Map(COSMETIC_SETS.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
const LAUNCH = { 1: t('2026-10-15T00:00:00Z') };
const plan = (facts: UnlockFacts, over: Partial<Parameters<typeof planGrants>[0]> = {}) =>
  planGrants({ items: COSMETIC_ITEMS, sets: COSMETIC_SETS, facts, owned: new Set(), release: 1, launchedAt: LAUNCH, ...over });
const ids = (rows: { itemId: string }[]) => rows.map((r) => r.itemId).sort();

describe('metAt', () => {
  const facts: UnlockFacts = {
    levels: [
      { family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 1, at: t('2026-10-16T08:00:00Z') },
      { family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 2, at: t('2026-10-20T08:00:00Z') },
      { family: 'STEP_GOAL', level: 1, at: t('2026-10-17T08:00:00Z') },
    ],
    recapsReadAt: [t('2026-10-16T09:00:00Z'), t('2026-10-23T09:00:00Z'), t('2026-10-30T09:00:00Z'), t('2026-11-06T09:00:00Z')],
    cheersAt: [t('2026-10-16T10:00:00Z')],
  };
  it('a family level is met when that level (or a higher one) was stored', () => {
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'family', family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 2 }, facts, SETS)).toEqual(t('2026-10-20T08:00:00Z'));
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'family', family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 3 }, facts, SETS)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('any family is met by the first family to reach the level', () => {
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'anyFamily', level: 1 }, facts, SETS)).toEqual(t('2026-10-16T08:00:00Z'));
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'anyFamily', level: 2 }, facts, SETS)).toEqual(t('2026-10-20T08:00:00Z'));
  });
  it('a counter is met at its n-th event', () => {
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'counter', counter: 'recaps_read', atLeast: 4 }, facts, SETS)).toEqual(t('2026-11-06T09:00:00Z'));
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'counter', counter: 'buddy_cheers', atLeast: 5 }, facts, SETS)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('a set piece is met when its set is; a starter is never granted', () => {
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'set', set: 'cottage' }, facts, SETS)).toEqual(t('2026-11-06T09:00:00Z'));
    expect(metAt({ kind: 'starter' }, facts, SETS)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('names each source', () => {
    expect([
      sourceOf({ kind: 'family', family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 3 }), sourceOf({ kind: 'anyFamily', level: 4 }),
      sourceOf({ kind: 'counter', counter: 'recaps_read', atLeast: 8 }), sourceOf({ kind: 'set', set: 'cottage' }),
    ]).toEqual(['family:STEADY_BEDTIME:3', 'anyFamily:4', 'counter:recaps_read:8', 'set:cottage']);
  });
});

describe('planGrants', () => {
  const after = (family: string, level: number) => ({ family, level, at: t('2026-10-20T00:00:00Z') });

  it('a family level grants its pieces and the any-family pieces of that level', () => {
    const rows = plan({ ...NONE, levels: [after('STEADY_BEDTIME', 1)] });
    expect(ids(rows)).toEqual(['lamp.kiplamp', 'out.scarf', 'shelf.trophyB', 'wall.stripes', 'win.curtains']);
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.itemId === 'wall.stripes')!.source).toBe('family:STEADY_BEDTIME:1');
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.itemId === 'shelf.trophyB')!.source).toBe('anyFamily:1');
  });

  it('4 recaps read grants the wood panel, the cork board and every Cottage piece not owned from day one', () => {
    const at = t('2026-10-20T00:00:00Z');
    const rows = plan({ ...NONE, recapsReadAt: [at, at, at, at] });
    expect(ids(rows)).toEqual([
      'art.cork', 'set.cottage.art', 'set.cottage.corner', 'set.cottage.desk', 'set.cottage.desk_item', 'set.cottage.floor',
      'set.cottage.lamp', 'set.cottage.pet', 'set.cottage.rug', 'set.cottage.shelf', 'set.cottage.wallpaper', 'set.cottage.window',
      'wall.panel',
    ]);
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.itemId === 'set.cottage.desk')!.source).toBe('set:cottage');
  });

  it('5 cheers grants florals, fairy lights, the sunflower and the party hat', () => {
    const at = t('2026-10-20T00:00:00Z');
    expect(ids(plan({ ...NONE, cheersAt: [at, at, at, at, at] }))).toEqual(['out.party', 'plant.sunflower', 'wall.florals', 'win.fairy']);
  });

  it('skips owned, retired and release-2 items, and never grants a starter', () => {
    const facts = { ...NONE, levels: [after('STEADY_BEDTIME', 1), after('STEADY_BEDTIME', 2), after('STEADY_BEDTIME', 3)] };
    const retired: CosmeticItem[] = COSMETIC_ITEMS.map((i) => (i.id === 'out.scarf' ? { ...i, retired: true } : i));
    const rows = plan(facts, { items: retired, owned: new Set(['wall.stripes']) });
    expect(ids(rows)).not.toContain('wall.stripes');
    expect(ids(rows)).not.toContain('out.scarf');
    expect(ids(rows)).not.toContain('wall.shoji'); // SB3, release 2
    expect(rows.every((r) => !COSMETIC_ITEMS.find((i) => i.id === r.itemId)!.unlock.kind.startsWith('starter'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('decides the backfill by time: a rule met before its release launched is backfill, whichever path grants it', () => {
    const before = { family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 1, at: t('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z') };
    expect(plan({ ...NONE, levels: [before] }).every((r) => r.source === BACKFILL)).toBe(true);
    const mixed = plan({ ...NONE, levels: [before, { family: 'SLEEP_GOAL', level: 1, at: t('2026-10-16T00:00:00Z') }] });
    expect(mixed.find((r) => r.itemId === 'art.mountain')!.source).toBe('family:SLEEP_GOAL:1');
    expect(mixed.find((r) => r.itemId === 'wall.stripes')!.source).toBe(BACKFILL);
  });

  it('a release-2 item whose rule was met before the bump is backfill when granted after it', () => {
    const facts = { ...NONE, levels: [1, 2, 3].map((level) => ({ family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level, at: t('2026-11-01T00:00:00Z') })) };
    const launchedAt = { 1: t('2026-10-15T00:00:00Z'), 2: t('2027-01-10T00:00:00Z') };
    const rows = plan(facts, { release: 2, launchedAt });
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.itemId === 'wall.shoji')!.source).toBe(BACKFILL);
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.itemId === 'wall.stripes')!.source).toBe('family:STEADY_BEDTIME:1');
  });

  it('takes the earliest path when an item can be earned two ways', () => {
    const sets: CosmeticSet[] = COSMETIC_SETS.map((s) => (s.id === 'cottage' ? { ...s, worn: { ...s.worn, neck: 'out.scarf' } } : s));
    const items = COSMETIC_ITEMS.map((i) => (i.id === 'out.scarf' ? { ...i, sets: ['cottage' as const] } : i));
    const early = t('2026-10-16T00:00:00Z');
    const rows = plan({ levels: [{ family: 'STEADY_BEDTIME', level: 1, at: t('2026-10-30T00:00:00Z') }], recapsReadAt: [early, early, early, early], cheersAt: [] }, { items, sets });
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.itemId === 'out.scarf')!.source).toBe('set:cottage');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `backend/src/cosmetics/rules.ts`**

```ts
// Which items a user's own data unlocks (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §3.1). Pure: the facts are read elsewhere.
// Each rule has the moment it was met; a grant is 'backfill' when that moment is before its release launched, so
// it does not matter which hook (badge, recap, cheer, GET) happens to run first.
import type { CosmeticItem, CosmeticRelease, CosmeticSet, UnlockRule } from './catalogue';

export interface UnlockFacts {
  /** Every stored badge level and when it was stored. */
  levels: ReadonlyArray<{ family: string; level: number; at: Date }>;
  /** When each opened weekly recap was opened, oldest first. */
  recapsReadAt: readonly Date[];
  /** When each counted cheer happened (one per buddy per local day), oldest first. */
  cheersAt: readonly Date[];
}

export const BACKFILL = 'backfill';

export interface PlannedGrant {
  itemId: string;
  source: string;
}

function earliest(dates: readonly Date[]): Date | null {
  let best: Date | null = null;
  for (const d of dates) if (!best || d < best) best = d;
  return best;
}

export function metAt(rule: UnlockRule, facts: UnlockFacts, setsById: ReadonlyMap<string, CosmeticSet>): Date | null {
  switch (rule.kind) {
    case 'starter':
      return null;
    case 'family':
      return earliest(facts.levels.filter((l) => l.family === rule.family && l.level >= rule.level).map((l) => l.at));
    case 'anyFamily':
      return earliest(facts.levels.filter((l) => l.level >= rule.level).map((l) => l.at));
    case 'counter':
      return (rule.counter === 'recaps_read' ? facts.recapsReadAt : facts.cheersAt)[rule.atLeast - 1] ?? null;
    case 'set': {
      const set = setsById.get(rule.set);
      return set ? metAt(set.unlock, facts, setsById) : null;
    }
  }
}

export function sourceOf(rule: UnlockRule): string {
  switch (rule.kind) {
    case 'family': return `family:${rule.family}:${rule.level}`;
    case 'anyFamily': return `anyFamily:${rule.level}`;
    case 'counter': return `counter:${rule.counter}:${rule.atLeast}`;
    case 'set': return `set:${rule.set}`;
    case 'starter': return 'starter';
  }
}

export function planGrants(input: {
  items: readonly CosmeticItem[];
  sets: readonly CosmeticSet[];
  facts: UnlockFacts;
  owned: ReadonlySet<string>;
  release: number;
  launchedAt: Readonly<Partial<Record<CosmeticRelease, Date>>>;
}): PlannedGrant[] {
  const { items, sets, facts, owned, release, launchedAt } = input;
  const setsById = new Map(sets.map((s) => [s.id as string, s]));
  const byId = new Map(items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
  const best = new Map<string, { at: Date; source: string }>();
  const offer = (item: CosmeticItem | undefined, at: Date, source: string) => {
    if (!item || item.release > release || item.retired || item.unlock.kind === 'starter' || owned.has(item.id)) return;
    const current = best.get(item.id);
    if (!current || at < current.at) best.set(item.id, { at, source });
  };
  for (const item of items) {
    if (item.unlock.kind === 'set') continue; // set-only pieces come with their set
    const at = metAt(item.unlock, facts, setsById);
    if (at) offer(item, at, sourceOf(item.unlock));
  }
  // Unlocking a set unlocks all its pieces (§1), including base pieces it reuses.
  for (const set of sets) {
    if (set.release > release) continue;
    const at = metAt(set.unlock, facts, setsById);
    if (!at) continue;
    for (const id of [...Object.values(set.room), ...Object.values(set.worn)]) offer(byId.get(id!), at, `set:${set.id}`);
  }
  return [...best]
    .map(([itemId, { at, source }]) => {
      const launched = launchedAt[byId.get(itemId)!.release];
      return { itemId, source: launched && at < launched ? BACKFILL : source };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.itemId.localeCompare(b.itemId));
}
```

```ts
import type { AchievementFamily } from '@prisma/client';
import { prisma } from '../../src/db/client';
import { civilDateToUtcMidnight } from '../../src/biometrics/civilDate';

export const day = civilDateToUtcMidnight;

/** A stored badge level, stored at `at`. */
export function level(userId: string, family: AchievementFamily, lvl: number, at: Date) {
  return prisma.achievement.create({
    data: { userId, family, level: lvl, value: lvl, earnedOn: day('2026-10-01'), weekStart: day('2026-09-28'), monthStart: day('2026-10-01'), createdAt: at },
  });
}

/** `n` built recaps of `kind`, opened at `openedAt` (null leaves them unopened). Period starts are distinct per call
 *  (the Recap key is userId + kind + periodStart): `offset` shifts them when a test makes more than one batch. */
export async function weekRecaps(userId: string, n: number, openedAt: Date | null, kind: 'WEEK' | 'MONTH' = 'WEEK', offset = 0) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const w = offset + i;
    await prisma.recap.create({
      data: {
        userId, kind, status: 'BUILT', sleepGoalMinutes: 480, openedAt,
        periodStart: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 5 + w * 7)), periodEnd: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 11 + w * 7)),
      },
    });
  }
}

/** One sticker from `from` to `to` at `at` (no pair needed: counting reads Sticker rows only). */
export function sticker(from: string, to: string, at: Date) {
  return prisma.sticker.create({ data: { fromUserId: from, toUserId: to, kind: 'CHEER', sentAt: at } });
}

export async function unlocked(userId: string) {
  return (await prisma.cosmeticUnlock.findMany({ where: { userId }, orderBy: { itemId: 'asc' } })).map((r) => ({ itemId: r.itemId, source: r.source }));
}
```

```ts
});

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-20T12:00:00Z');
const AFTER = new Date('2026-10-18T12:00:00Z');

});

  const me = await buddyUser({ timezone: 'America/Los_Angeles' });

  const b = await buddyUser();
  await sticker(me.id, a.id, new Date('2026-10-19T16:00:00Z')); // Oct 19, 09:00 LA
  await sticker(me.id, a.id, new Date('2026-10-19T20:00:00Z')); // Oct 19, 13:00 LA: the same cheer
  await sticker(me.id, a.id, new Date('2026-10-20T06:30:00Z')); // Oct 19, 23:30 LA: still Oct 19
  await sticker(me.id, a.id, new Date('2026-10-20T08:00:00Z')); // Oct 20, 01:00 LA: a new day
  await sticker(me.id, b.id, new Date('2026-10-19T16:05:00Z')); // another buddy, same day
  expect(countersOf(await loadUnlockFacts(me.id)).buddyCheers).toBe(3);
});

it('cheerTimes stops at 15, and the scan bound holds 15 buddy-days at the per-day limit', () => {

  const rows = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ toUserId: `b${i}`, sentAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 1, 0, i)) }));

  expect(first.find((r) => r.itemId === 'rug.red')!.source).toBe('family:SLEEP_GOAL:1');

  const real = cosmeticsHooks.grant;
  cosmeticsHooks.grant = async () => { throw new Error('db down'); };

  } finally {

    warn.mockRestore();
  }
});
```

```ts
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { USER_OWNED_MODELS } from '../../src/users/deletion';

// Spec §2 "Never remove owned": nothing deletes an unlock row by name. Account deletion and the fixture purge
// delete through USER_OWNED_MODELS (delegateFor), so the only path is the whole account.
function files(dir: string): string[] {
  return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((e) =>
    e.isDirectory() ? files(path.join(dir, e.name)) : /\.ts$/.test(e.name) ? [path.join(dir, e.name)] : []);
}

it('no source deletes a cosmetic unlock', () => {
  const root = path.join(__dirname, '../..');
  const hits = [...files(path.join(root, 'src')), ...files(path.join(root, 'scripts'))]
    .filter((f) => /cosmeticUnlock\s*\.\s*delete(Many)?\s*\(/.test(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')));
  expect(hits).toEqual([]);
});

it('account deletion owns both cosmetics tables', () => {
  expect(USER_OWNED_MODELS).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['CosmeticUnlock', 'CosmeticLoadout']));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `facts.ts` and `unlock.ts`**

`backend/src/cosmetics/facts.ts`:

```ts
// The facts the unlock rules read (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §3.2): badge levels, opened weekly recaps and cheers,
// all derived from existing rows; no counter is stored. Bounded reads: the highest thresholds are 12 recaps and 15
// cheers.
import { localCivilDateOrUtc } from '../biometrics/civilDate';
import { prisma } from '../db/client';
import type { UnlockFacts } from './rules';

export const MAX_RECAPS_COUNTED = 12;
export const MAX_CHEERS_COUNTED = 15;
/** 15 buddy-days × at most 6 stickers each (5 a day, a race may let a 6th through): see ruling 8. */
export const CHEER_SCAN_ROWS = 90;

/** One cheer per buddy per local day, in the sender's current zone, oldest first, at most 15. */
export function cheerTimes(rows: ReadonlyArray<{ toUserId: string; sentAt: Date }>, timezone: string): Date[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: Date[] = [];
  for (const row of [...rows].sort((a, b) => a.sentAt.getTime() - b.sentAt.getTime())) {
    const key = `${row.toUserId}|${localCivilDateOrUtc(row.sentAt, timezone)}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);
    out.push(row.sentAt);
    if (out.length === MAX_CHEERS_COUNTED) break;
  }
  return out;
}

export async function loadUnlockFacts(userId: string): Promise<UnlockFacts> {
  const [levels, recaps, user, stickers] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.achievement.findMany({ where: { userId }, select: { family: true, level: true, createdAt: true } }),
    prisma.recap.findMany({
      where: { userId, kind: 'WEEK', openedAt: { not: null } }, orderBy: { openedAt: 'asc' }, take: MAX_RECAPS_COUNTED, select: { openedAt: true },
    }),
    prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId }, select: { timezone: true } }),
    prisma.sticker.findMany({
      where: { fromUserId: userId }, orderBy: { sentAt: 'asc' }, take: CHEER_SCAN_ROWS, select: { toUserId: true, sentAt: true },
    }),
  ]);
  return {
    levels: levels.map((l) => ({ family: l.family, level: l.level, at: l.createdAt })),
    recapsReadAt: recaps.map((r) => r.openedAt!),
    cheersAt: cheerTimes(stickers, user?.timezone ?? 'UTC'),
  };
}

export function countersOf(facts: UnlockFacts): { recapsRead: number; buddyCheers: number } {
  return { recapsRead: facts.recapsReadAt.length, buddyCheers: facts.cheersAt.length };
}
```

`backend/src/cosmetics/unlock.ts`:

```ts
// grantCosmetics (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §3.1): idempotent and safe to run concurrently. createManyAndReturn +
// skipDuplicates returns only the rows THIS call inserted, so a concurrent call that lost the race announces nothing.
// Called after new badge levels, after a recap is first opened, after a sticker is sent, and by GET /me/cosmetics.
// Logs a count only: never the user id with item ids.
import { prisma } from '../db/client';
import { COSMETICS_RELEASE, COSMETIC_ITEMS, COSMETIC_SETS, RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT, type CosmeticItem, type CosmeticRelease, type CosmeticSet } from './catalogue';
import { loadUnlockFacts } from './facts';
import { planGrants, type PlannedGrant } from './rules';

export interface GrantOptions {
  release?: number;
  launchedAt?: Readonly<Partial<Record<CosmeticRelease, Date>>>;
  items?: readonly CosmeticItem[];
  sets?: readonly CosmeticSet[];
}

export async function grantCosmetics(userId: string, now: Date, opts: GrantOptions = {}): Promise<PlannedGrant[]> {
  const [facts, rows] = await Promise.all([
    loadUnlockFacts(userId),
    prisma.cosmeticUnlock.findMany({ where: { userId }, select: { itemId: true } }),
  ]);
  const planned = planGrants({
    items: opts.items ?? COSMETIC_ITEMS,
    sets: opts.sets ?? COSMETIC_SETS,
    facts,
    owned: new Set(rows.map((r) => r.itemId)),
    release: opts.release ?? COSMETICS_RELEASE,
    launchedAt: opts.launchedAt ?? RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT,
  });
  if (planned.length === 0) return [];
  const created = await prisma.cosmeticUnlock.createManyAndReturn({
    data: planned.map((p) => ({ userId, itemId: p.itemId, source: p.source, unlockedAt: now })),
    skipDuplicates: true,
    select: { itemId: true, source: true },
  });
  if (created.length > 0) console.info(JSON.stringify({ event: 'cosmetics.granted', count: created.length }));
  return created;
}

/** The grant the hooks call; tests swap it to prove a failure never fails the request that triggered it. */
export const cosmeticsHooks: { grant: (userId: string, now: Date) => Promise<unknown> } = {
  grant: (userId, now) => grantCosmetics(userId, now),
};

/** Never throws: a failed grant is logged without values and the next GET /me/cosmetics catches up. */
export async function grantCosmeticsSafely(userId: string, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await cosmeticsHooks.grant(userId, now);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(JSON.stringify({ event: 'cosmetics.grant_failed', error: err instanceof Error ? err.name : 'Error' }));
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `backend/src/cosmetics/loadout.ts`**

```ts
// Reading and saving KIP's room (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §2, §5). Owned = the starter items + unlock rows. A
// stored slot naming an item the user does not own (or no longer in the shipped catalogue) reads back as the slot's
// starter piece; the app never decides ownership. Saves are optimistic: version must match, every save increments it.
import { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { prisma } from '../db/client';
import {
  COSMETICS_CATALOGUE_VERSION, COSMETICS_RELEASE, EMPTY_WORN, OPTIONAL_ROOM_SLOTS, ROOM_SLOTS, STARTER_IDS, STARTER_ROOM, WORN_SLOTS,
  cosmeticItem, type CosmeticSlot, type RoomSlot, type WornSlot,
} from './catalogue';
import { countersOf, loadUnlockFacts } from './facts';
import { grantCosmeticsSafely } from './unlock';

type Room = Record<RoomSlot, string | null>;
type Worn = Record<WornSlot, string | null>;

export interface CosmeticsDTO {
  catalogueVersion: number;
  owned: string[];
  fresh: Array<{ itemId: string; source: string }>;
  room: Room;
  worn: Worn;
  version: number;
  counters: { recapsRead: number; buddyCheers: number };
}

export interface SaveFailure {
  status: 400 | 403 | 409;
  body: { error: 'invalid_body' | 'unknown_item' | 'wrong_slot' | 'required_slot' | 'not_owned' | 'stale'; itemIds?: string[] };
}

const shipped = (id: string) => {
  const item = cosmeticItem(id);
  return item && item.release <= COSMETICS_RELEASE ? item : undefined;
};

function ownedFrom(rows: ReadonlyArray<{ itemId: string }>): Set<string> {
  return new Set([...STARTER_IDS, ...rows.map((r) => r.itemId).filter((id) => shipped(id))]);
}

function readRoom(json: unknown, owned: ReadonlySet<string>): Room {
  const stored = (json && typeof json === 'object' ? json : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const room = { ...STARTER_ROOM } as Room;
  for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) {
    const id = stored[slot];
    if (id === null && OPTIONAL_ROOM_SLOTS.has(slot)) room[slot] = null;
    else if (typeof id === 'string' && owned.has(id) && shipped(id)?.slot === slot) room[slot] = id;
  }
  return room;
}

function readWorn(json: unknown, owned: ReadonlySet<string>): Worn {
  const stored = (json && typeof json === 'object' ? json : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const worn = { ...EMPTY_WORN } as Worn;
  for (const slot of WORN_SLOTS) {
    const id = stored[slot];
    if (typeof id === 'string' && owned.has(id) && shipped(id)?.slot === slot) worn[slot] = id;
  }
  return worn;
}

export async function readCosmetics(userId: string, now: Date, opts: { grant?: boolean } = {}): Promise<CosmeticsDTO> {
  if (opts.grant ?? true) await grantCosmeticsSafely(userId, now);
  const [rows, loadout, facts] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.cosmeticUnlock.findMany({ where: { userId }, orderBy: [{ unlockedAt: 'asc' }, { itemId: 'asc' }], select: { itemId: true, source: true, seenAt: true } }),
    prisma.cosmeticLoadout.findUnique({ where: { userId } }),
    loadUnlockFacts(userId),
  ]);
  const owned = ownedFrom(rows);
  return {
    catalogueVersion: COSMETICS_CATALOGUE_VERSION,
    owned: [...owned].sort(),
    fresh: rows.filter((r) => r.seenAt === null && owned.has(r.itemId)).map((r) => ({ itemId: r.itemId, source: r.source })),
    room: loadout ? readRoom(loadout.room, owned) : { ...STARTER_ROOM },
    worn: loadout ? readWorn(loadout.worn, owned) : { ...EMPTY_WORN },
    version: loadout?.version ?? 0,
    counters: countersOf(facts),
  };
}

const INVALID: SaveFailure = { status: 400, body: { error: 'invalid_body' } };

function exactSlots(value: unknown, slots: readonly string[]): value is Record<string, string | null> {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
  const keys = Object.keys(value);
  return keys.length === slots.length && slots.every((s) => {
    const v = (value as Record<string, unknown>)[s];
    return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, s) && (v === null || typeof v === 'string');
  });
}

export function validateBody(body: unknown): { room: Room; worn: Worn; version: number } | SaveFailure {
  const b = (body && typeof body === 'object' ? body : {}) as { room?: unknown; worn?: unknown; version?: unknown };
  if (!exactSlots(b.room, ROOM_SLOTS) || !exactSlots(b.worn, WORN_SLOTS)) return INVALID;
  if (typeof b.version !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(b.version) || b.version < 0) return INVALID;
  return { room: b.room as Room, worn: b.worn as Worn, version: b.version };
}

export function checkPieces(room: Room, worn: Worn, owned: ReadonlySet<string>): SaveFailure | null {
  const placed: Array<[CosmeticSlot, string]> = [
    ...ROOM_SLOTS.flatMap((s) => (room[s] === null ? [] : [[s, room[s]!] as [CosmeticSlot, string]])),
    ...WORN_SLOTS.flatMap((s) => (worn[s] === null ? [] : [[s, worn[s]!] as [CosmeticSlot, string]])),
  ];
  if (placed.some(([, id]) => !shipped(id))) return { status: 400, body: { error: 'unknown_item' } };
  if (placed.some(([slot, id]) => shipped(id)!.slot !== slot)) return { status: 400, body: { error: 'wrong_slot' } };
  if (ROOM_SLOTS.some((s) => room[s] === null && !OPTIONAL_ROOM_SLOTS.has(s))) return { status: 400, body: { error: 'required_slot' } };
  const notOwned = [...new Set(placed.map(([, id]) => id).filter((id) => !owned.has(id)))].sort();
  if (notOwned.length) return { status: 403, body: { error: 'not_owned', itemIds: notOwned } };
  return null;
}

const STALE: SaveFailure = { status: 409, body: { error: 'stale' } };

export async function saveCosmetics(userId: string, body: unknown, now: Date): Promise<{ ok: true; dto: CosmeticsDTO } | { ok: false; failure: SaveFailure }> {
  const parsed = validateBody(body);
  if ('status' in parsed) return { ok: false, failure: parsed };
  const rows = await prisma.cosmeticUnlock.findMany({ where: { userId }, select: { itemId: true } });
  const bad = checkPieces(parsed.room, parsed.worn, ownedFrom(rows));
  if (bad) return { ok: false, failure: bad };
  const data = { room: parsed.room, worn: parsed.worn };
  if (parsed.version === 0) {
    try {
      await prisma.cosmeticLoadout.create({ data: { userId, ...data, version: 1 } });
    } catch (err) {
      // Another first save won the race (or a row already exists): the app re-reads.
      if (err instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && err.code === 'P2002') return { ok: false, failure: STALE };
      throw err;
    }
  } else {
    const { count } = await prisma.cosmeticLoadout.updateMany({ where: { userId, version: parsed.version }, data: { ...data, version: { increment: 1 } } });
    if (count === 0) return { ok: false, failure: STALE };
  }
  return { ok: true, dto: await readCosmetics(userId, now, { grant: false }) };
}

/** Marks unseen rows seen. Ids the user has no row for (unknown, unowned, starters) are ignored. */
export async function markSeen(userId: string, body: unknown, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
  const itemIds = (body as { itemIds?: unknown } | null)?.itemIds;
  if (!Array.isArray(itemIds) || itemIds.length > 500 || !itemIds.every((i) => typeof i === 'string')) return false;
  if (itemIds.length) await prisma.cosmeticUnlock.updateMany({ where: { userId, itemId: { in: itemIds as string[] }, seenAt: null }, data: { seenAt: now } });
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `backend/src/cosmetics/routes.ts` and mount it**

```ts
// KIP's room endpoints (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §5). All need a session and answer private, no-store. Nothing
// here is health data; nothing is logged with the user's id.
import { Router, type Response } from 'express';
import { requireAuth, type AuthedRequest } from '../auth/middleware';
import { markSeen, readCosmetics, saveCosmetics } from './loadout';

export const cosmeticsRouter = Router();
const noStore = (res: Response) => res.set('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store');

cosmeticsRouter.get('/me/cosmetics', requireAuth, async (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  noStore(res);
  res.json(await readCosmetics(req.userId!, new Date()));
});

cosmeticsRouter.put('/me/cosmetics', requireAuth, async (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  noStore(res);
  const result = await saveCosmetics(req.userId!, req.body, new Date());
  if (!result.ok) {
    res.status(result.failure.status).json(result.failure.body);
    return;
  }
  res.json(result.dto);
});

cosmeticsRouter.post('/me/cosmetics/seen', requireAuth, async (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  noStore(res);
  if (!(await markSeen(req.userId!, req.body, new Date()))) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: 'invalid_body' });
    return;
  }
  res.status(204).end();
});
```

In `src/app.ts`: `import { cosmeticsRouter } from './cosmetics/routes';` and `app.use(cosmeticsRouter);` after
`app.use(kipRouter);`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `backend/src/cosmetics/worn.ts`**

```ts
// What a buddy sees of your KIP (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §7): the worn ids only, never the room or the owned list.
// An id the shipped catalogue does not know, or in the wrong slot, is dropped.
import { COSMETICS_RELEASE, EMPTY_WORN, WORN_SLOTS, cosmeticItem, type WornSlot } from './catalogue';

export type KipWornDTO = Record<WornSlot, string | null>;

export function kipWornOf(json: unknown): KipWornDTO {
  const out = { ...EMPTY_WORN } as KipWornDTO;
  if (!json || typeof json !== 'object' || Array.isArray(json)) return out;
  for (const slot of WORN_SLOTS) {
    const id = (json as Record<string, unknown>)[slot];
    const item = typeof id === 'string' ? cosmeticItem(id) : undefined;
    if (item && item.slot === slot && item.release <= COSMETICS_RELEASE) out[slot] = id as string;
  }
  return out;
}
```

```ts
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';

export const PERSON_SELECT = {
  id: true, handle: true, displayName: true, coachPersonaId: true, kipColour: true, cosmeticLoadout: { select: { worn: true } },
} as const;

export interface PersonDTO {
  id: string;
  handle: string;
  displayName: string;
  /** A character id (mobile src/components/characters/types.ts). */
  coachId: string;
  kipColour: KipColourName;
  /** What their KIP wears (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §7); never their room. */
  kipWorn: KipWornDTO;
}

export function toPerson(u: {
  id: string; handle: string | null; displayName: string | null; coachPersonaId: string | null; kipColour: string | null;
  cosmeticLoadout: { worn: Prisma.JsonValue } | null;
}): PersonDTO {
  return {
    id: u.id,
    handle: u.handle ?? '',
    displayName: u.displayName ?? u.handle ?? '',
    coachId: resolvePersona(u.coachPersonaId).id,
    kipColour: kipColourOf(u.kipColour),
    kipWorn: kipWornOf(u.cosmeticLoadout?.worn),
  };
}
```

```ts
it('has art for every worn item the app ships, in its own slot, inside the 16 × 16 grid', () => {
  const shipped = CATALOGUE.items.filter((i) => ['head', 'face', 'neck', 'ears'].includes(i.slot));
  expect(WORN_ART.map((w) => w.id).sort()).toEqual(shipped.map((i) => i.id).sort());

it('never reshapes the body: in every pose and mood, a cell that changes shows a worn piece, and nothing is erased', () => {
  const worn = { head: 'out.beanie', face: 'out.round', neck: 'out.scarf', ears: 'out.phones' };
  const colours = new Set(Object.values(worn).flatMap((id) => wornLayer(id, ramp).flat().filter(Boolean)));
  for (const pose of KIP_POSES) for (const mood of KIP_MOODS) {
    const bare = kipGrid({ pose, mood });
    const dressed = kipGrid({ pose, mood, worn });
    bare.forEach((row, y) => row.forEach((c, x) => {
      const d = dressed[y]![x];

    }));

});

  const cheer = filled(kipGrid({ pose: 'cheer', worn: { head: 'out.beanie' } }).map((r) => r.map((c) => (c === '#EF4444' ? c : null))));
```
