```ts
// What KIP can wear (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §7): each piece is a few rows of cells on KIP's 16 × 16 grid,
// ported from CatOutfit.dc.html:68-90 (offset `at`, `rows`, palette; '@b' '@h' '@s' '@d' are KIP's body, light,
// shade and deep). Worn pieces never move or reshape the body (kipArt.ts draws them, ruling 5) and never animate
// (ruling 4). Only release-1 pieces are here; the rest come with their art.
import type { KipRamp } from './kipArt';
import { EMPTY_WORN, type KipWorn } from '../room/loadout';
import { WORN_SLOTS, type WornSlot } from '../room/slots';

export interface WornArt {
  id: string;
  slot: WornSlot;
  /** The top-left of `rows` on the 16 × 16 grid. */
  at: readonly [number, number];
  rows: readonly string[];
  pal: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
}

export const WORN_ART: readonly WornArt[] = [
  { id: 'out.beanie', slot: 'head', at: [4, 2], rows: ['...wW...', '.LLrrrR.', 'LrrrrrrR', 'ddddddDD'], pal: { w: '#F8FAFC', W: '#CBD5E1', r: '#EF4444', L: '#F87171', R: '#B91C1C', d: '#B91C1C', D: '#7F1D1D' } },
  { id: 'out.cap', slot: 'head', at: [4, 4], rows: ['.LbbbbR....', 'bbbbbbbbkkk'], pal: { b: '#2563EB', L: '#60A5FA', R: '#1E40AF', k: '#1E3A8A' } },
  { id: 'out.sweatband', slot: 'head', at: [4, 6], rows: ['wwwwwwww', 'rrrrrrrr'], pal: { w: '#F8FAFC', r: '#DC2626' } },
  { id: 'out.bucket', slot: 'head', at: [3, 3], rows: ['..cccccc..', '..dddddd..', 'bbbbbbbbbb'], pal: { c: '@h', d: '@d', b: '@s' } },
  { id: 'out.hachimaki', slot: 'head', at: [4, 6], rows: ['wwwrrwwwwW', '.........W'], pal: { w: '#F8FAFC', r: '#DC2626', W: '#E5E7EB' } },
  { id: 'out.beret', slot: 'head', at: [4, 3], rows: ['...k.....', '.bbbbbb..', 'bbbbbbbbb'], pal: { b: '#B91C1C', k: '#111827' } },
  { id: 'out.party', slot: 'head', at: [6, 0], rows: ['..y..', '..p..', '.ppp.', '.pyp.', 'ppppp', 'pypyp'], pal: { p: '@h', y: '#FDE047' } },
  { id: 'out.round', slot: 'face', at: [5, 7], rows: ['FFFFFf', 'f.ff.f', 'ffffff'], pal: { F: '#FCD34D', f: '#D97706' } },
  { id: 'out.scarf', slot: 'neck', at: [3, 10], rows: ['LssssssssS', '.ddddddLs.', '.......Ls.', '.......dd.'], pal: { L: '#F87171', s: '#DC2626', S: '#991B1B', d: '#991B1B' } },
  { id: 'out.bowtie', slot: 'neck', at: [5, 10], rows: ['bb..bb', 'bbkkbb', 'bb..bb'], pal: { b: '#2563EB', k: '#1E3A8A' } },
  { id: 'out.earbuds', slot: 'ears', at: [3, 8], rows: ['w........w', 'w........w'], pal: { w: '#F8FAFC' } },
  { id: 'out.phones', slot: 'ears', at: [3, 5], rows: ['..kkkkkk..', '.k......k.', 'cc......cc', 'cC......cC', 'cc......cc'], pal: { k: '#1F2937', c: '#38BDF8', C: '#0369A1' } },
];

const REGISTRY = new Map<string, WornArt>(WORN_ART.map((w) => [w.id, w]));

/** Tests and later art batches add pieces here. */
export function registerWornArt(arts: readonly WornArt[]): void {
  for (const art of arts) REGISTRY.set(art.id, art);
}

export function wornArt(id: string | null | undefined): WornArt | undefined {
  return id ? REGISTRY.get(id) : undefined;
}

const tint = (c: string, ramp: KipRamp) =>
  c === '@b' ? ramp.body : c === '@h' ? ramp.light : c === '@s' ? ramp.shade : c === '@d' ? ramp.deep : c;

/** The piece as a 16 × 16 fill grid (null is empty), at its place on an upright, resting KIP. */
export function wornLayer(id: string, ramp: KipRamp): (string | null)[][] {
  const grid = Array.from({ length: 16 }, () => Array<string | null>(16).fill(null));
  const art = wornArt(id);
  if (!art) return grid;
  art.rows.forEach((row, j) => [...row].forEach((key, i) => {
    const x = art.at[0] + i, y = art.at[1] + j;
    if (key !== '.' && art.pal[key] && x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < 16 && y < 16) grid[y]![x] = tint(art.pal[key]!, ramp);
  }));
  return grid;
}

/** A worn record from the server or a buddy: ids this build cannot draw, or in the wrong slot, are dropped. */
export function kipWornOf(value: unknown): KipWorn {
  const out: KipWorn = { ...EMPTY_WORN };
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return out;
  for (const slot of WORN_SLOTS) {
    const id = (value as Record<string, unknown>)[slot];
    if (typeof id === 'string' && wornArt(id)?.slot === slot) out[slot] = id;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Below this size a KIP draws only head and ears pieces (§7 "At small sizes"). */
export const SMALL_KIP_PT = 32;

export function wornForSize(worn: Partial<KipWorn> | undefined, sizePt: number): Partial<KipWorn> {
  if (!worn) return {};
  if (sizePt >= SMALL_KIP_PT) return worn;
  const out: Partial<KipWorn> = {};
  if (worn.head !== undefined) out.head = worn.head;
  if (worn.ears !== undefined) out.ears = worn.ears;
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Draw worn pieces in `kipGrid`** (`lib/kip/kipArt.ts`, ruling 5)

1. `KipVariant` gains `worn?: Partial<Record<WornSlot, string | null>>` (import `WornSlot` as a type from
   `../room/slots`, `wornLayer` from `./wornArt`).
2. Add a posture function for worn layers that repeats the body's remaps without the ground shadow:

```ts
/** Moves a worn layer exactly as the body's posture moves the body: tall, squash, tilt, hop (no shadow). */
function postureLayer(layer: (string | null)[][], p: PoseDef, m: MoodDef, hop: number): (string | null)[][] {
  const move = (g: (string | null)[][], f: (x: number, y: number) => [number, number] | null) => {
    const out = blank<string>();
    for (let y = 0; y < KIP_GRID; y++) for (let x = 0; x < KIP_GRID; x++) {
      const c = g[y]![x];
      if (!c) continue;
      const to = f(x, y);
      if (to) put(out, to[0], to[1], c);
    }
    return out;
  };
  let g = layer;
  if (p.tall || m.tall) g = move(g, (x, y) => [x, y <= 12 ? y - 1 : y]);
  if (m.squash) g = move(g, (x, y) => (y === 7 ? null : [x, y < 7 ? y + 1 : y]));
  if (m.tilt) { const t = m.tilt; g = move(g, (x, y) => [x + (y < 9 ? t : 0), y]); }
  if (hop) g = move(g, (x, y) => [x, y - hop]);
  return g;
}
```

3. In `kipGrid`, record which cells the arms paint (they are placed only in empty body cells), and compose the fills in
   the ruling's order. Replace the arms loop and the `out` line with:

```ts
  const armCells = new Set<string>();
  const arms = p.frames ? p.frames[Math.abs(Math.floor(frame)) % p.frames.length]! : p.arms ?? [];
  for (const [ax, ay, role] of arms) {
    const y = ay - hop;
    if (y >= 0 && !g[y]![ax]) { g[y]![ax] = role; armCells.add(`${ax},${y}`); }
  }

  const fills: Record<Role, string> = {
    b: ramp.body, h: ramp.light, s: ramp.shade, d: ramp.deep, e: ramp.eyes, w: ramp.white, z: ramp.shadow,
  };
  const out: KipGrid = g.map((row, y) => row.map((c, x) => (c && !armCells.has(`${x},${y}`) ? fills[c] : null)));
  const paint = (layer: (string | null)[][]) => layer.forEach((row, y) => row.forEach((c, x) => { if (c) out[y]![x] = c; }));
  const layerOf = (slot: WornSlot) => {
    const id = variant.worn?.[slot];
    return id ? postureLayer(wornLayer(id, ramp), p, m, hop) : null;
  };
  // Ruling 5: body, head, face, neck, the pose's arms (over a neck piece), ears, then a held item.
  for (const slot of ['head', 'face', 'neck'] as const) { const l = layerOf(slot); if (l) paint(l); }
  armCells.forEach((k) => { const [x, y] = k.split(',').map(Number); out[y!]![x!] = fills[g[y!]![x!]!]; });
  const ears = layerOf('ears');
  if (ears) paint(ears);
```

   `const { colour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR, pose = 'rest', mood = 'idle', frame = 0 } = variant;`.) The held-item block
   stays last, unchanged.
4. `kipArt` and `kipBounds` already go through `kipGrid`, so worn pieces are in the rects and the crop. Add:

```ts
/** A worn piece's tray icon (spec §8 Icons): rows 0–14 of a resting KIP wearing only that piece. */
export function wornIconRects(id: string, colour: KipColour): PixelRect[] {
  const slot = wornArt(id)?.slot;
  return slot ? kipArt({ colour, pose: 'rest', mood: 'idle', worn: { [slot]: id } }).filter((r) => r.y < 15) : [];
}
```

```ts
import { apiFetch } from './client';
import { send } from './buddies';
import type { KipWorn, RoomLoadout } from '../lib/room/loadout';

// KIP's room (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §5). The server decides ownership; the app only shows it.
export interface CosmeticsDTO {
  catalogueVersion: number;
  owned: string[];
  fresh: Array<{ itemId: string; source: string }>;
  room: Record<string, string | null>;
  worn: Record<string, string | null>;
  version: number;
  counters: { recapsRead: number; buddyCheers: number };
}

export const fetchCosmetics = () => apiFetch<CosmeticsDTO>('/me/cosmetics');
export const putCosmetics = (body: { room: RoomLoadout; worn: KipWorn; version: number }) =>
  apiFetch<CosmeticsDTO>('/me/cosmetics', send('PUT', body));
export const postCosmeticsSeen = (itemIds: string[]) => apiFetch<void>('/me/cosmetics/seen', send('POST', { itemIds }));
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `src/lib/kip/myKip.ts`** (ruling 6)

Keep every colour function as it is and change the state from a colour to `{ colour, worn }`:

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { fetchIdentity } from '../../api/buddies';
import { saveKipColour } from '../../api/kip';
import { DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR, type KipColour } from './kipArt';
import { kipColourOf } from './kipColour';
import { kipWornOf } from './wornArt';
import { EMPTY_WORN, type KipWorn } from '../room/loadout';

// Your KIP (spec 2026-10-13 KIP colour §3; spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §7): its colour (from the identity read) and
// what it wears (from GET /me/cosmetics, via the cosmetics store), loaded once per sign-in by MyKipScope, coral and
// bare until then or when a read fails, updated by a save, and forgotten on sign-out.

export interface MyKip {
  colour: KipColour;
  worn: KipWorn;
}

let me: MyKip = { colour: DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR, worn: EMPTY_WORN };
let epoch = 0;
let loadFailed = false;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function publish(next: Partial<MyKip>): void {
  me = { ...me, ...next };
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}

export function getMyKip(): MyKip {
  return me;
}
export function getMyKipColour(): KipColour {
  return me.colour;
}
```

Then the existing `myKipColourLoadFailed`, `loadMyKipColour` (publishes `{ colour: kipColourOf(...) }`),
`setMyKipColour` (`publish({ colour: next })`), `saveMyKipColour`, and:

```ts
/** What KIP wears, from the server's worn record; pieces this build cannot draw are dropped (§7). */
export function setMyKipWorn(raw: unknown): void {
  publish({ worn: kipWornOf(raw) });
}

export function resetMyKip(): void {
  epoch++;
  loadFailed = false;
  publish({ colour: DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR, worn: EMPTY_WORN });
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => listeners.delete(listener);
}

export function useMyKip(): MyKip {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getMyKip, getMyKip);
}

export function useMyKipColour(): KipColour {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getMyKipColour, getMyKipColour);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `src/lib/room/cosmeticsStore.ts`**

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { fetchCosmetics, postCosmeticsSeen, putCosmetics, type CosmeticsDTO } from '../../api/cosmetics';
import { setMyKipWorn } from '../kip/myKip';
import { EMPTY_WORN, STARTER_LOADOUT, type KipWorn, type RoomLoadout } from './loadout';
import { ROOM_SLOTS, WORN_SLOTS } from './slots';

// One shared copy of GET /me/cosmetics (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §4, §5): what you own, what is new, your room and
// what KIP wears, as stored (ruling 25: ids this build cannot draw are kept here and drawn through drawableLoadout).
// Loaded per sign-in by MyKipScope and again on foreground and KIP tab focus (RoomToastHost); a save publishes the
// server's answer. A read that lands after sign-out is dropped.

export interface Cosmetics {
  owned: ReadonlySet<string>;
  fresh: ReadonlyArray<{ itemId: string; source: string }>;
  room: RoomLoadout;
  worn: KipWorn;
  version: number;
  counters: { recapsRead: number; buddyCheers: number };
}

let cosmetics: Cosmetics | null = null;
let epoch = 0;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function publish(next: Cosmetics | null): void {
  cosmetics = next;
  if (next) setMyKipWorn(next.worn);
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}

const idOrNull = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : null);

function fromDTO(dto: CosmeticsDTO): Cosmetics {
  const room = { ...STARTER_LOADOUT };
  for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) if (slot in dto.room) room[slot] = idOrNull(dto.room[slot]);
  const worn = { ...EMPTY_WORN };
  for (const slot of WORN_SLOTS) worn[slot] = idOrNull(dto.worn[slot]);
  return { owned: new Set(dto.owned), fresh: dto.fresh, room, worn, version: dto.version, counters: dto.counters };
}

export function getCosmetics(): Cosmetics | null {
  return cosmetics;
}

/** Reads the server (which also catches up on grants). Keeps what is shown when the read fails. */
export async function loadCosmetics(): Promise<Cosmetics | null> {
  const at = epoch;
  try {
    const dto = await fetchCosmetics();
    if (at === epoch) publish(fromDTO(dto));
  } catch {
    // The next sign-in, foreground or KIP tab focus tries again.
  }
  return cosmetics;
}

/** PUT /me/cosmetics. Throws the ApiError (403 not_owned, 409 stale, …) for decorate mode to handle. */
export async function saveCosmeticsLoadout(input: { room: RoomLoadout; worn: KipWorn; version: number }): Promise<Cosmetics> {
  const at = epoch;
  const next = fromDTO(await putCosmetics(input));
  if (at === epoch) publish(next);
  return next;
}

/** Clears these from "new" now and tells the server; a failed post is retried by the next open. */
export async function markCosmeticsSeen(itemIds: readonly string[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!itemIds.length) return;
  const ids = new Set(itemIds);
  if (cosmetics) publish({ ...cosmetics, fresh: cosmetics.fresh.filter((f) => !ids.has(f.itemId)) });
  try {
    await postCosmeticsSeen([...ids]);
  } catch {
    // Still unseen on the server: the next open marks it again.
  }
}

export function resetCosmetics(): void {
  epoch++;
  cosmetics = null;
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}

export function hasUnseen(c: Cosmetics | null): boolean {
  return !!c && c.fresh.length > 0;
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => listeners.delete(listener);
}

export function useCosmetics(): Cosmetics | null {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getCosmetics, getCosmetics);
}

const roomOf = () => cosmetics?.room ?? STARTER_LOADOUT;
/** Your stored room (the starter room until the first read). Draw it through drawableLoadout. */
export function useMyRoom(): RoomLoadout {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, roomOf, roomOf);
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { loadMyKipColour, resetMyKip } from '../../lib/kip/myKip';
import { loadCosmetics, resetCosmetics } from '../../lib/room/cosmeticsStore';

// Mounted once inside the signed-in navigator: loads your KIP's colour and its room and worn pieces; signing out
// unmounts it and forgets them.
export function MyKipScope() {
  useEffect(() => {
    void loadMyKipColour();
    void loadCosmetics();
    return () => {
      resetMyKip();
      resetCosmetics();
    };
  }, []);
  return null;
}
```

```ts
import type { ItemId } from './loadout';
import type { RoomSlot, WornSlot } from './slots';

export const ITEM_NAMES: Readonly<Record<ItemId, string>> = {

  'plant.succulents': 'Succulent trio', 'plant.bamboo': 'Lucky bamboo', 'plant.sunflower': 'Sunflower',

  'corner.floorlamp': 'Floor lamp', 'corner.yogamat': 'Yoga mat roll', 'corner.bag': 'Punching bag', 'corner.beanbag': 'Bean bag',
  'rug.slate': 'Slate rug', 'rug.red': 'Red rug', 'rug.gymmat': 'Gym mat', 'rug.checker': 'Checker rug',
  'rug.woven': 'Round woven', 'rug.tatami': 'Tatami mat',

  'set.cottage.wallpaper': 'Plaster and oak beams', 'set.cottage.floor': 'Light oak boards',

  'set.cottage.corner': 'Wood stove', 'set.cottage.rug': 'Patchwork quilt rug', 'set.cottage.pet': 'Dog in a pet bed',
};

export const SET_NAMES: Readonly<Record<SetId, string>> = { cottage: 'Cottage' };

/** Lower-case slot names for sentences ("in the wallpaper spot", "Decorate the desk item"). */
export const SLOT_NAMES: Readonly<Record<RoomSlot | WornSlot, string>> = {
  wallpaper: 'wallpaper', floor: 'floor', art: 'art', window: 'window', shelf: 'shelf', desk: 'desk', desk_item: 'desk item',
  plant: 'plant', lamp: 'lamp', corner: 'corner', rug: 'rug', pet: 'pet', head: 'head', face: 'face', neck: 'neck', ears: 'ears',
};

/** Category chips and the KIP tab's group headers. */
export const SLOT_TITLES: Readonly<Record<RoomSlot | WornSlot, string>> = {
  wallpaper: 'Wallpaper', floor: 'Floor', art: 'Art', window: 'Window', shelf: 'Shelf', desk: 'Desk', desk_item: 'Desk item',
  plant: 'Plant', lamp: 'Lamp', corner: 'Corner', rug: 'Rug', pet: 'Pet', head: 'Head', face: 'Face', neck: 'Neck', ears: 'Ears',
};

const FAMILY_NAMES: Record<AchievementFamilyName, string> = {
  SLEEP_GOAL: 'Sleep goal', STEADY_BEDTIME: 'Steady bedtime', STEP_GOAL: 'Step goal', CHECK_IN: 'Check-in',
  BEST_RECOVERY_WEEK: 'Best recovery week', EVERY_DAY_LOGGED: 'Every day logged', STEADIEST_MONTH: 'Steadiest month',
};
const ROMAN = ['', 'I', 'II', 'III', 'IV', 'V'];

export function itemName(id: ItemId): string {
  return ITEM_NAMES[id] ?? id;
}
export function spokenName(id: ItemId): string {
  return itemName(id).split(' · ').join(', ');
}

function setRule(rule: UnlockRule): UnlockRule {
  return rule.kind === 'set' ? catalogueSet(rule.set)?.unlock ?? rule : rule;
}

export function unlockLine(rule: UnlockRule): string {
  const r = setRule(rule);
  switch (r.kind) {
    case 'starter': return 'In every room from day one';
    case 'family': return `${FAMILY_NAMES[r.family]} · ${ROMAN[r.level]}`;
    case 'anyFamily': return `Any family · level ${ROMAN[r.level]}`;
    case 'counter': return r.counter === 'recaps_read' ? `Read ${r.atLeast} weekly recaps` : `Cheer a buddy ${r.atLeast} times`;
    case 'set': return '';
  }
}

export function unlockSpoken(rule: UnlockRule): string {
  const r = setRule(rule);
  if (r.kind === 'family') return `${FAMILY_NAMES[r.family]}, level ${r.level}`;
  if (r.kind === 'anyFamily') return `Any family, level ${r.level}`;
  return unlockLine(r);
}

export interface ProgressFacts {
  /** Highest earned level per family (0 or missing: none). */
  levels: Partial<Record<AchievementFamilyName, number>>;
  recapsRead: number;
  buddyCheers: number;
}

export function progressLine(rule: UnlockRule, facts: ProgressFacts): string {
  const r = setRule(rule);
  switch (r.kind) {
    case 'family': {
      const lvl = facts.levels[r.family] ?? 0;
      return lvl ? `level ${ROMAN[lvl]} now` : 'No level yet';
    }
    case 'anyFamily': {
      const lvl = Math.max(0, ...Object.values(facts.levels).map((l) => l ?? 0));
      return lvl ? `highest level ${ROMAN[lvl]} now` : 'No level yet';
    }
    case 'counter':
      return r.counter === 'recaps_read' ? `${facts.recapsRead} of ${r.atLeast} read` : `${facts.buddyCheers} of ${r.atLeast}`;
    default:
      return '';
  }
}
```

Add `progressFromAchievements` beside `progressLine`:

```ts
import type { AchievementsSnapshot } from '../achievementsStore';

/** Progress facts from the badge store the app already has, plus the server's two counters. */
export function progressFromAchievements(snapshot: AchievementsSnapshot, counters: { recapsRead: number; buddyCheers: number }): ProgressFacts {
  const levels: Partial<Record<AchievementFamilyName, number>> = {};
  if (snapshot.state.status === 'ready') for (const f of snapshot.state.data.families) levels[f.family] = f.level;
  return { levels, ...counters };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the shared art checks and the failing tests**

`mobile/jest-mocks/roomArtChecks.ts` (a fixture, not a suite):

```ts
import { KIP_COLOURS, kipRamp } from '../src/lib/kip/kipArt';
import { catalogueItem, catalogueItemsFor } from '../src/lib/room/catalogue';
import { boundsOf, boxContains } from '../src/lib/room/grid';
import { itemIcon } from '../src/lib/room/icons';
import { itemArt, type ItemContext } from '../src/lib/room/items';
import { ANCHOR_EXEMPT } from '../src/lib/room/items/shared';
import { SLOT_ANCHORS, type RoomSlot } from '../src/lib/room/slots';
import '../src/lib/room/art';

export const CTX_DAY: ItemContext = { weather: 'clear', night: false, colour: 'coral', ramp: kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.coral), screen: 'lines' };
export const CTX_NIGHT: ItemContext = { ...CTX_DAY, weather: 'fallback', night: true };
const LIME: ItemContext = { ...CTX_DAY, colour: 'lime', ramp: kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.lime) };

/** Spec §8 / §11 "Item art", for every shipped item of one room slot (set pieces only once their art exists, Task 11). */
export function checkSlotArt(slot: RoomSlot, { sets = true }: { sets?: boolean } = {}): void {
  for (const item of catalogueItemsFor(slot).filter((i) => sets || !i.id.startsWith('set.'))) {
    const art = itemArt(item.id);
    const tag = { id: item.id };
    expect({ ...tag, art: !!art, icon: (itemIcon(item.id)?.length ?? 0) > 0 }).toEqual({ ...tag, art: true, icon: true });
    if (!art) continue;
    expect({ ...tag, slot: art.slot }).toEqual({ ...tag, slot });
    // The flags match the catalogue.
    expect({ ...tag, tint: !!art.tint, emissive: !!art.emissive, frames: art.frames ?? null, style: art.style ?? null })
      .toEqual({ ...tag, tint: !!item.tint, emissive: !!item.emissive, frames: item.frames ?? null, style: item.style ?? null });
    for (const ctx of [CTX_DAY, CTX_NIGHT]) {
      const layers = art.draw(ctx);
      expect({ ...tag, base: (layers.base?.length ?? 0) > 0 }).toEqual({ ...tag, base: true });
      // Inside its anchor, unless exempt with a reason (ruling 3).
      if (!ANCHOR_EXEMPT.has(item.id)) {
        expect({ ...tag, inside: boxContains(SLOT_ANCHORS[slot], boundsOf(layers.base!)!) }).toEqual({ ...tag, inside: true });
      }
      // Emissive items light only in the glow layer; others have none.
      expect({ ...tag, glow: (layers.emissive?.length ?? 0) > 0 }).toEqual({ ...tag, glow: !!item.emissive });
      // Loops have exactly three frames, and they differ.
      if (item.frames) {
        expect({ ...tag, frames: layers.frames?.length }).toEqual({ ...tag, frames: 3 });
        expect({ ...tag, moves: JSON.stringify(layers.frames![0]) !== JSON.stringify(layers.frames![1]) || JSON.stringify(layers.frames![1]) !== JSON.stringify(layers.frames![2]) || ctx.night })
          .toEqual({ ...tag, moves: true });
      } else {
        expect({ ...tag, frames: layers.frames ?? null }).toEqual({ ...tag, frames: null });
      }
      // Deterministic.
      expect(art.draw(ctx)).toEqual(layers);
    }
    // Tinted items follow KIP's colour; others never do.
    const coral = JSON.stringify(art.draw(CTX_DAY));
    const lime = JSON.stringify(art.draw(LIME));
    expect({ ...tag, follows: coral !== lime }).toEqual({ ...tag, follows: !!item.tint || (slot === 'desk' && !!item.frames) });
  }
  expect(catalogueItem('no.such')).toBeUndefined();
}
```

(The monitor's and the laptop's screens use KIP's colour as the accent of their loop frames, Spec A, so a looping desk
"follows" KIP's colour though it is not tinted; that is why the last check allows a desk with frames.)

`mobile/__tests__/lib/roomArtWalls.test.ts`:

```ts
import { checkSlotArt, CTX_DAY } from '../../jest-mocks/roomArtChecks';
import { drawRoomStatic } from '../../src/lib/room/draw';
import { itemArt } from '../../src/lib/room/items';
import { clipFloor, floorSpan, FLOOR_POLY } from '../../src/lib/room/items/shared';
import { STARTER_LOADOUT } from '../../src/lib/room/loadout';
import { FLOOR_Y, ROOM_H, ROOM_W, WALL_X0 } from '../../src/lib/room/grid';

it.each(['wallpaper', 'floor', 'art', 'window'] as const)('every shipped %s piece has art, an icon and the right flags', (slot) => {
  checkSlotArt(slot, { sets: false });
});

it('clips floor patterns to the floor, which widens to the full grid 12 rows below the wall line', () => {
  expect(floorSpan(FLOOR_Y)).toEqual([WALL_X0, ROOM_W - WALL_X0]);
  expect(floorSpan(FLOOR_Y + 12)).toEqual([0, ROOM_W]);
  expect(floorSpan(FLOOR_Y - 1)).toEqual([0, 0]);
  const clipped = clipFloor([[0, FLOOR_Y - 4, ROOM_W, 8, '#000000']]);
  expect(clipped.every(([x, y, w]) => y >= FLOOR_Y && x >= floorSpan(y)[0] && x + w <= floorSpan(y)[1])).toBe(true);
  expect(FLOOR_POLY.at(-2)).toEqual([0, ROOM_H]);
});

it('every window piece keeps the weather sky: the glass shows the same sky as the plain frame', () => {
  const sky = (id: string) => itemArt(id)!.draw(CTX_DAY).base!.filter(([x, y]) => x >= 22 && x < 34 && y >= 16 && y < 20);
  expect(sky('win.blinds').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(drawRoomStatic({ loadout: { ...STARTER_LOADOUT, window: 'win.curtains' }, weather: 'clear', night: false, colour: 'coral' }).light.length)
    .toEqual(drawRoomStatic({ loadout: STARTER_LOADOUT, weather: 'clear', night: false, colour: 'coral' }).light.length);
});
```

`mobile/__tests__/components/ItemIcon.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from 'react';

import { Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { ItemIcon } from '../../src/components/room/ItemIcon';
import { KIP_COLOURS, kipRamp } from '../../src/lib/kip/kipArt';
import '../../src/lib/room/art';

const fills = (s: ReturnType<typeof render>) => s.UNSAFE_getAllByType(Rect).map((r) => r.props.fill);

it('draws a room piece\'s sprite at 48 points, decorative, in KIP\'s colour when tinted', () => {
  const s = render(<ItemIcon id="art.pennant" colour="lime" testID="icon" />);
  expect(s.getByTestId('icon', { includeHiddenElements: true }).props.style).toMatchObject({ width: 48, height: 48 });
  expect(fills(s)).toContain(kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.lime).body);
});

it('draws a worn piece on KIP\'s head', () => {
  expect(fills(render(<ItemIcon id="out.round" colour="coral" />))).toContain('#FCD34D');
});

it('draws an empty box for an id it has no icon for', () => {
  expect(render(<ItemIcon id="no.such" colour="coral" />).UNSAFE_queryAllByType(Rect)).toHaveLength(0);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `src/lib/room/icons.ts`**

```ts
// Tray icons (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §8 Icons): each room piece registers its board sprite (or a 16 × 16 surface
// patch); worn pieces are KIP's head wearing them. Icons are drawn with react-native-svg (ItemIcon) and never build a
// room picture.
import { kipRamp, KIP_COLOURS, wornIconRects, type KipColour } from '../kip/kipArt';
import { wornArt } from '../kip/wornArt';
import type { RoomRect } from './grid';
import type { ItemId } from './loadout';

/** A board sprite rect: x, y, w, h, colour; '@b' '@h' '@s' '@d' are KIP's body, light, shade and deep. */
export type SpriteRect = readonly [number, number, number, number, string];

const ICONS = new Map<ItemId, readonly SpriteRect[]>();

export function registerIcons(map: Readonly<Record<ItemId, readonly SpriteRect[]>>): void {
  for (const [id, rects] of Object.entries(map)) ICONS.set(id, rects);
}

export function itemIcon(id: ItemId): readonly SpriteRect[] | undefined {
  return ICONS.get(id) ?? (wornArt(id) ? wornIconRects(id, 'coral').map((r) => [r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, r.fill] as const) : undefined);
}

/** The icon's rects in this KIP colour (tinted cells resolved). */
export function iconRectsFor(id: ItemId, colour: KipColour): RoomRect[] {
  if (wornArt(id)) return wornIconRects(id, colour).map((r) => [r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, r.fill]);
  const ramp = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
  const tint = (c: string) => (c === '@b' ? ramp.body : c === '@h' ? ramp.light : c === '@s' ? ramp.shade : c === '@d' ? ramp.deep : c);
  return (ICONS.get(id) ?? []).map(([x, y, w, h, c]) => [x, y, w, h, tint(c)]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `src/components/room/ItemIcon.tsx`**

```tsx
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import type { KipColour } from '../../lib/kip/kipArt';
import { boundsOf } from '../../lib/room/grid';
import { iconRectsFor } from '../../lib/room/icons';

// A tray tile's picture (spec §6.3, §8 Icons; ruling 22): the piece's sprite in a square viewBox fitted to it, 48 × 48
// points by default (16 × 16 at 3×). Decorative: the tile carries the label.
export function ItemIcon({ id, colour, size = 48, testID }: { id: string; colour: KipColour; size?: number; testID?: string }) {
  const rects = useMemo(() => iconRectsFor(id, colour), [id, colour]);
  const box = boundsOf(rects);
  const side = box ? Math.max(box.w, box.h, 16) : 16;
  const viewBox = box ? `${box.x - (side - box.w) / 2} ${box.y - (side - box.h) / 2} ${side} ${side}` : '0 0 16 16';
  return (
    <View testID={testID} accessible={false} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants" style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
      {box ? (
        <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox={viewBox}>
          {rects.map(([x, y, w, h, fill], i) => (
            <Rect key={i} x={x} y={y} width={w} height={h} fill={fill} />
          ))}
        </Svg>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `src/lib/room/items/shared.ts`**

```ts
// The v2 art helpers every item file shares (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §8; docs/design/kip-coach/project/lib2.js).
// Items are drawn on the 170 × 110 room grid at the engine's slot anchors (slots.ts), from the Cat boards' sprites
// (one board pixel = one room cell) with the v2 finish: contact shadows, a lit edge, glows in the emissive layer.
import { darkenHex, mixHex } from '../../colour';
import type { KipRamp } from '../../kip/kipArt';
import { FLOOR_Y, ROOM_H, ROOM_W, WALL_X0, WALL_X1, type Box, type RoomRect } from '../grid';
import type { SpriteRect } from '../icons';
import type { ItemContext } from '../items';
import type { ItemId } from '../loadout';
import { clipB, poly, R, type Rects } from '../paint';
import { windowSky } from '../weather';

export type { SpriteRect } from '../icons';

export function tintOf(c: string, ramp: KipRamp): string {
  return c === '@b' ? ramp.body : c === '@h' ? ramp.light : c === '@s' ? ramp.shade : c === '@d' ? ramp.deep : c;
}

/** A board sprite placed with its own (0, 0) at (x, y). */
export function sprite(r: Rects, rects: readonly SpriteRect[], x: number, y: number, ramp: KipRamp): void {
  for (const [sx, sy, w, h, c] of rects) R(r, x + sx, y + sy, w, h, tintOf(c, ramp));
}

export function spriteBox(rects: readonly SpriteRect[]): Box {
  let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y, w, h] of rects) { x0 = Math.min(x0, x); y0 = Math.min(y0, y); x1 = Math.max(x1, x + w); y1 = Math.max(y1, y + h); }
  return { x: x0, y: y0, w: x1 - x0, h: y1 - y0 };
}

export const three = (f: (k: 0 | 1 | 2) => Rects): [Rects, Rects, Rects] => [f(0), f(1), f(2)];

/** '#FDE047' → '253,224,71', for glow(). */
export function rgbOf(hex: string): string {
  const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
  return `${(n >> 16) & 255},${(n >> 8) & 255},${n & 255}`;
}

/** The back wall, as a clip box. */
export const BACK_WALL: readonly [number, number, number, number] = [WALL_X0, 0, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y];

/** A wallpaper: the pattern over the back wall, plain side walls in `side`, and the skirting (as Plain slate). */
export function wallSurface(r: Rects, base: string, side: string, pattern: (p: Rects, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number) => void): void {
  R(r, BACK_WALL[0], BACK_WALL[1], BACK_WALL[2], BACK_WALL[3], base);
  const p: Rects = [];
  pattern(p, BACK_WALL[0], BACK_WALL[1], BACK_WALL[2], BACK_WALL[3]);
  r.push(...clipB(p, BACK_WALL));
  poly(r, [[0, 0], [WALL_X0, 0], [WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12]], side);
  poly(r, [[WALL_X1, 0], [ROOM_W, 0], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y]], side);
  R(r, WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y - 2, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 2, darkenHex(base, 0.7));
}

export const FLOOR_POLY: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [
  [WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [ROOM_W, ROOM_H], [0, ROOM_H], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12],
];

/** The floor's span [x0, x1) at row y: the trapezoid under the back wall, then the full grid. */
export function floorSpan(y: number): [number, number] {
  if (y < FLOOR_Y) return [0, 0];
  if (y >= FLOOR_Y + 12) return [0, ROOM_W];
  const xl = WALL_X0 - ((y - FLOOR_Y) * WALL_X0) / 12;
  return [Math.ceil(xl), Math.floor(ROOM_W - xl)];
}

/** Rects clipped to the floor, row by row. */
export function clipFloor(rects: readonly RoomRect[]): Rects {
  const out: Rects = [];
  for (const [x, y, w, h, c] of rects) {
    for (let row = Math.max(y, FLOOR_Y); row < Math.min(y + h, ROOM_H); row++) {
      const [x0, x1] = floorSpan(row);
      const a = Math.max(x, x0), b = Math.min(x + w, x1);
      if (b > a) out.push([a, row, b - a, 1, c]);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** A floor: its base colour on the floor polygon, then the pattern clipped to it. */
export function floorSurface(r: Rects, base: string, pattern: (p: Rects) => void): void {
  poly(r, FLOOR_POLY, base);
  const p: Rects = [];
  pattern(p);
  r.push(...clipFloor(p));
}

/** Art hangs centred in its anchor, below a 2-cell pin, with a soft shadow on the wall. */
export function artFrame(r: Rects, rects: readonly SpriteRect[], ramp: KipRamp, anchor: Box): void {
  const b = spriteBox(rects);
  const x = anchor.x + Math.floor((anchor.w - b.w) / 2) - b.x;
  const y = anchor.y + 3 - b.y;
  R(r, x + b.x + 1, y + b.y + 1, b.w, b.h, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.18)');
  R(r, x + b.x + Math.floor(b.w / 2) - 1, anchor.y, 2, 2, '#94A3B8');
  sprite(r, rects, x, y, ramp);
}

/** Spec A's plain window (starterItems `win.plain`): frame, sky and sill. Every window piece draws it first. */
export function windowFrame(r: Rects, { weather, night }: Pick<ItemContext, 'weather' | 'night'>): void {
  R(r, 18, 12, 36, 40, '#1E2430');
  windowSky(r, { x: 20, y: 14, w: 32, h: 36 }, weather, night);
  R(r, 35, 14, 2, 36, '#1E2430');
  R(r, 20, 31, 32, 1, '#1E2430');
  R(r, 16, 52, 40, 3, '#191E28');
  R(r, 16, 52, 40, 1, '#2A3140');
}

/** Spec A's shelf plank (starterItems `shelf.books`). Every shelf piece stands on it. */
export const SHELF_TOP = 30;
export function shelfPlank(r: Rects): void {
  R(r, 96, SHELF_TOP, 34, 2, '#5B4636');
  R(r, 96, SHELF_TOP, 34, 1, '#7C5A3E');
  R(r, 99, SHELF_TOP + 2, 1, 2, '#3A2D23');
  R(r, 126, SHELF_TOP + 2, 1, 2, '#3A2D23');
}

/** Items allowed to draw outside their slot's anchor, and why (ruling 3). */
export const ANCHOR_EXEMPT: ReadonlyMap<ItemId, string> = new Map([
  ['set.cottage.lamp', 'fairy lights strung along the ceiling beam, across the whole back wall'],
]);

export { mixHex, darkenHex };
```

In `starterItems.ts`, make `plainFrame.draw` call `windowFrame(r, ctx)` and `books.draw` call `shelfPlank(r)` in place
of the identical rects (the room test "removing an item removes exactly its rects" proves nothing moved).

- [ ] **Step 6: `items/wallpaper.ts`** (CatWalls.dc.html:65-91 `patch`, the same code for the wall and the icon)

```ts
// Wallpapers (CatWalls.dc.html "slot · wallpaper"). One pattern function fills the room's back wall and the 16 × 16
// tray icon, as on the board. Side walls are the base colour, darker.
import { registerItems, type ItemArt } from '../items';
import { registerIcons, type SpriteRect } from '../icons';
import { R, type Rects } from '../paint';
import type { KipRamp } from '../../kip/kipArt';
import { darkenHex, tintOf, wallSurface } from './shared';

type Pattern = { base: string; draw: (p: Rects, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, ramp: KipRamp) => void };

const PATTERNS: Record<string, Pattern> = {
  'wall.plain': { base: '#2A3140', draw: () => undefined },
  'wall.dots': { base: '#2A3140', draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h) => { for (let y = 1; y < h; y += 4) for (let x = y % 8 === 1 ? 1 : 3; x < w; x += 4) R(p, x0 + x, y0 + y, 1, 1, '#4B5568'); } },
  'wall.stripes': { base: '@d', draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h, ramp) => { for (let x = 1; x < w; x += 6) R(p, x0 + x, y0, 2, h, ramp.shade); } },
  'wall.brick': { base: '#5A2A22', draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h) => { for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 4) for (let x = y % 8 ? -4 : 0; x < w; x += 8) R(p, x0 + x + 1, y0 + y, 7, 3, '#8A4434'); } },
  'wall.panel': { base: '#6B4F3A', draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h) => { for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 6) R(p, x0 + x, y0, 1, h, '#5A4130'); R(p, x0, y0, w, 1, '#8A6A4E'); R(p, x0, y0 + Math.floor(h / 2), w, 1, '#5A4130'); } },
  'wall.florals': {
    base: '#3B2F4A',
    draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h) => {
      for (let y = 2; y < h; y += 6) for (let x = y % 12 === 2 ? 2 : 5; x < w; x += 6) {
        R(p, x0 + x, y0 + y - 1, 1, 1, '#F9A8D4'); R(p, x0 + x - 1, y0 + y, 3, 1, '#F9A8D4'); R(p, x0 + x, y0 + y + 1, 1, 1, '#F9A8D4');
        R(p, x0 + x, y0 + y, 1, 1, '#FDE047'); R(p, x0 + x + 1, y0 + y + 2, 1, 1, '#4ADE80');
      }
    },
  },
  'wall.checker': { base: '#E5E7EB', draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h) => { for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 4) for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 4) if (((x + y) / 4) % 2) R(p, x0 + x, y0 + y, 4, 4, '#1F2937'); } },
  'wall.subway': { base: '#E5E7EB', draw: (p, x0, y0, w, h) => { for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 3) { R(p, x0, y0 + y, w, 1, '#CBD5E1'); for (let x = y % 6 ? 3 : 0; x < w; x += 6) R(p, x0 + x, y0 + y, 1, 3, '#CBD5E1'); } } },
};

const TINTED = new Set(['wall.stripes']);
const NEW_IDS = ['wall.dots', 'wall.stripes', 'wall.brick', 'wall.panel', 'wall.florals', 'wall.checker', 'wall.subway'];

const art: ItemArt[] = NEW_IDS.map((id) => ({
  id,
  slot: 'wallpaper',
  ...(TINTED.has(id) ? { tint: true } : {}),
  draw: ({ ramp }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    const { base, draw } = PATTERNS[id]!;
    const b = tintOf(base, ramp);
    wallSurface(r, b, darkenHex(b, 0.82), (p, x, y, w, h) => draw(p, x, y, w, h, ramp));
    return { base: r };
  },
}));

/** The board's 16 × 16 patch for the tray. */
function patch16(id: string): SpriteRect[] {
  const p: Rects = [];
  const { base, draw } = PATTERNS[id]!;
  R(p, 0, 0, 16, 16, base);
  // Icons keep '@' colours: ItemIcon resolves them in the viewer's KIP colour.
  draw(p, 0, 0, 16, 16, { body: '@b', light: '@h', shade: '@s', deep: '@d', eyes: '', white: '', shadow: '' });
  return p as SpriteRect[];
}

registerItems(art);
registerIcons(Object.fromEntries(['wall.plain', ...NEW_IDS].map((id) => [id, patch16(id)])));
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `items/floor.ts`**

```ts
// Floors (CatWalls.dc.html "slot · floor"): v2 perspective boards and tiles on the floor polygon (shared.floorSurface).
import { registerItems, type ItemArt } from '../items';
import { registerIcons, type SpriteRect } from '../icons';
import { FLOOR_Y, ROOM_H, ROOM_W } from '../grid';
import { floorSeams, R, type Rects } from '../paint';
import { floorSurface } from './shared';

const FLOOR_BOX = { x: 0, y: FLOOR_Y, w: ROOM_W, h: ROOM_H - FLOOR_Y };

/** Speckles as on the board (CatWalls patch 'carpet' / 'concreteF'), over a box. */
function speckle(p: Rects, x0: number, y0: number, w: number, h: number, n: number, a: string, b: string): void {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) R(p, x0 + ((i * 5 + 1) % w), y0 + ((i * 3 + 2) % h), 1, 1, i % 2 ? a : b);
}

const art: ItemArt[] = [
  {
    id: 'floor.tiles', slot: 'floor',
    draw: () => {
      const r: Rects = [];
      floorSurface(r, '#E5E7EB', (p) => floorSeams(p, 10, '#CBD5E1', [79, 85, 93, 103]));
      return { base: r };
    },
  },
  {
    id: 'floor.carpet', slot: 'floor', tint: true,
    draw: ({ ramp }) => {
      const r: Rects = [];
      floorSurface(r, ramp.shade, (p) => speckle(p, FLOOR_BOX.x, FLOOR_BOX.y, FLOOR_BOX.w, FLOOR_BOX.h, (FLOOR_BOX.w * FLOOR_BOX.h) / 4, ramp.deep, ramp.body));
      return { base: r };
    },
  },
  {
    id: 'floor.concrete', slot: 'floor',
    draw: () => {
      const r: Rects = [];
      floorSurface(r, '#57534E', (p) => {
        speckle(p, FLOOR_BOX.x, FLOOR_BOX.y, FLOOR_BOX.w, FLOOR_BOX.h, (FLOOR_BOX.w * FLOOR_BOX.h) / 6, '#48443F', '#6B665F');
        floorSeams(p, 30, '#4A4642', [92]);
      });
      return { base: r };
    },
  },
];

const icon = (base: string, rest: (p: Rects) => void): SpriteRect[] => {
  const p: Rects = [];
  R(p, 0, 0, 16, 16, base);
  rest(p);
  return p as SpriteRect[];
};

registerItems(art);
registerIcons({
  'floor.oakDark': icon('#5B4636', (p) => { for (let y = 4; y < 16; y += 5) R(p, 0, y, 16, 1, '#4A382B'); R(p, 8, 0, 1, 4, '#4A382B'); R(p, 4, 5, 1, 4, '#4A382B'); }),
  'floor.tiles': icon('#E5E7EB', (p) => { for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 6) { R(p, i, 0, 1, 16, '#CBD5E1'); R(p, 0, i, 16, 1, '#CBD5E1'); } }),
  'floor.carpet': icon('@s', (p) => speckle(p, 0, 0, 16, 16, 64, '@d', '@b')),
  'floor.concrete': icon('#57534E', (p) => speckle(p, 0, 0, 16, 16, 42, '#48443F', '#6B665F')),
});
```

Each piece is `artFrame(r, SPRITE, ramp, SLOT_ANCHORS.art)` from its board sprite, copied verbatim (the rect array in
the board line, `@b`/`@h`/`@s`/`@d` kept):

| id | board line | flags | extra |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `art.mountain` | `CatWindow.dc.html:79` | — | — |
| `art.pennant` | `:80` | tint | — |
| `art.clock` | `:81` | frames | the minute hand moves (below) |
| `art.heart` | `:82` | emissive | the tube glows (below) |
| `art.motto` | `:83` | — | — |
| `art.band` | `:86` | — | — |
| `art.cork` | `:87` | — | — |
| `art.wave` | `:88` | — | — |

Icons: `registerIcons({ 'art.calendar': CALENDAR /* :78 */, 'art.mountain': MOUNTAIN, … })` with the same arrays.

Declare each board sprite as a constant first, verbatim from its line, for example
`const CLOCK: readonly SpriteRect[] = [[3, 0, 8, 14, '#334155'], [0, 3, 14, 8, '#334155'], …];` (`CatWindow.dc.html:81`),
and likewise `MOUNTAIN`, `PENNANT`, `HEART`, `MOTTO`, `BAND`, `CORK`, `WAVE`, `CALENDAR`. Write the two special pieces
in full:

```ts
// The clock: the board's face without its hands; the hour hand is static, the minute hand turns a quarter a frame.
const CLOCK_FACE: readonly SpriteRect[] = CLOCK.filter(([, , , , c]) => c !== '#1F2937' && c !== '#DC2626');
const clock: ItemArt = {
  id: 'art.clock', slot: 'art', frames: 3,
  draw: ({ ramp }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    artFrame(r, CLOCK_FACE, ramp, SLOT_ANCHORS.art);
    const b = spriteBox(CLOCK);
    const ox = SLOT_ANCHORS.art.x + Math.floor((SLOT_ANCHORS.art.w - b.w) / 2), oy = SLOT_ANCHORS.art.y + 3;
    R(r, ox + 7, oy + 4, 1, 4, '#1F2937'); // the hour hand
    const HANDS: Array<[number, number, number, number]> = [[7, 3, 1, 5], [7, 7, 4, 1], [7, 7, 1, 4]];
    return { base: r, frames: three((k) => { const f: Rects = []; const [x, y, w, h] = HANDS[k]!; R(f, ox + x, oy + y, w, h, '#1F2937'); R(f, ox + 7, oy + 7, 1, 1, '#DC2626'); return f; }) };
  },
};

// The neon heart: the unlit tube in the base (darkened by the night veil), the lit tube and its halo in the glow layer.
const heart: ItemArt = {
  id: 'art.heart', slot: 'art', emissive: true,
  draw: ({ ramp, night }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    artFrame(r, HEART.map(([x, y, w, h, c]) => [x, y, w, h, darkenHex(c, 0.55)] as const), ramp, SLOT_ANCHORS.art);
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    const b = spriteBox(HEART);
    const ox = SLOT_ANCHORS.art.x + Math.floor((SLOT_ANCHORS.art.w - b.w) / 2), oy = SLOT_ANCHORS.art.y + 3;
    sprite(emissive, HEART, ox, oy, ramp);
    glow(emissive, ox + b.w / 2, oy + b.h / 2, night ? 14 : 9, night ? 11 : 7, '244,114,182', night ? 0.12 : 0.05);
    return { base: r, emissive };
  },
};
```

Every other piece is the plain form:

```ts
const plain = (id: string, rects: readonly SpriteRect[], tint?: true): ItemArt => ({
  id, slot: 'art', ...(tint ? { tint } : {}),
  draw: ({ ramp }) => { const r: Rects = []; artFrame(r, rects, ramp, SLOT_ANCHORS.art); return { base: r }; },
});
registerItems([plain('art.mountain', MOUNTAIN), plain('art.pennant', PENNANT, true), clock, heart, plain('art.motto', MOTTO), plain('art.band', BAND), plain('art.cork', CORK), plain('art.wave', WAVE)]);
```

(`artFrame` places a sprite at `anchor.x + floor((anchor.w - w) / 2)`, `anchor.y + 3`; the clock and heart compute the
same origin for their extra layers.)

Every window piece draws `windowFrame(r, ctx)` first (the glass always shows today's weather, spec §1), then its decor
sprite (a verbatim constant: `CURTAINS`, `BLINDS`, `STICKER`, `FAIRY`, `CHIME`) with the board's origin at `(19, 15)` (the board's frame interior maps onto Spec A's frame there; the curtains'
`x -3, y -3` then land at `x 16, y 12`, the anchor's corner):

| id | board line | flags | extra |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `win.curtains` | `CatWindow.dc.html:93` | tint | — |
| `win.blinds` | `:94` | — | — |
| `win.sticker` | `:95` | — | — |
| `win.fairy` | `:97` | emissive, frames | the bulbs chase (below) |
| `win.chime` | `:99` | frames | the tubes sway (below) |

```ts
const O = [19, 15] as const;

// Fairy lights: the wire in the base; each bulb in the glow layer, every third one brighter in turn.
const FAIRY_BULBS = FAIRY.filter(([, , , , c]) => c !== '#475569');
const fairy: ItemArt = {
  id: 'win.fairy', slot: 'window', emissive: true, frames: 3,
  draw: (ctx) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    windowFrame(r, ctx);
    sprite(r, FAIRY.filter(([, , , , c]) => c === '#475569'), O[0], O[1], ctx.ramp);
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    sprite(emissive, FAIRY_BULBS, O[0], O[1], ctx.ramp);
    if (ctx.night) for (const [x, y, , , c] of FAIRY_BULBS) glow(emissive, O[0] + x + 0.5, O[1] + y + 0.5, 2.5, 2.5, rgbOf(c), 0.1);
    const frames = three((k) => {
      const f: Rects = [];
      FAIRY_BULBS.forEach(([x, y], i) => { if ((i + k) % 3 === 0) R(f, O[0] + x, O[1] + y, 1, 1, '#FFFFFF'); });
      return f;
    });
    return { base: r, emissive, frames };
  },
};

// Wind chime: the bar in the base; the tubes and the clapper swing one cell and back.
const CHIME_BAR = CHIME.filter(([, y]) => y < 7);
const CHIME_TUBES = CHIME.filter(([, y]) => y >= 7);
const chime: ItemArt = {
  id: 'win.chime', slot: 'window', frames: 3,
  draw: (ctx) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    windowFrame(r, ctx);
    sprite(r, CHIME_BAR, O[0], O[1], ctx.ramp);
    return { base: r, frames: three((k) => { const f: Rects = []; sprite(f, CHIME_TUBES, O[0] + [0, 1, -1][k]!, O[1], ctx.ramp); return f; }) };
  },
};
```

The chime's base must still be non-empty and inside the anchor (the frame is). Icons: the board decor sprite over a
small window (`[[0, 0, 34, 30, '#1E2430'], [2, 2, 30, 26, '#8592A6']]` then the decor), `win.plain` the board's `WIN`
(`CatWindow.dc.html:76`).

- [ ] **Step 2: `deskFurniture` and the desks** (`items/desk.ts`)

Move the desk-and-chair part of Spec A's monitor (`starterItems.ts`, the lines under `// The desk` and `// KIP's chair`)

Then:

```ts
// Desks (CatDesk.dc.html "DESK"): every desk piece is Spec A's desk and chair plus what stands on it, where the
// monitor stands (x 72-100, top at y 72). A screen shows the KIP stage in its loop frames (Spec A plan ruling 5).
import { registerIcons, type SpriteRect } from '../icons';
import { registerItems, type ItemArt, type MonitorScreen } from '../items';
import type { Box } from '../grid';
import { glow, gradV, R, type Rects } from '../paint';
import { deskFurniture, mixHex, sprite, three } from './shared';

const NOTEBOOK: readonly SpriteRect[] = /* CatDesk.dc.html:90, verbatim */ [[0, 0, 22, 4, '#F1F5F9'], [10, 0, 2, 4, '#CBD5E1'], [2, 1, 6, 1, '#94A3B8'], [13, 1, 7, 1, '#94A3B8'], [2, 2, 5, 1, '#94A3B8'], [15, 3, 6, 1, '#FACC15']];
const LAPTOP: readonly SpriteRect[] = /* CatDesk.dc.html:91, verbatim */ [[2, 0, 16, 10, '#334155'], [3, 1, 14, 8, '#0F1A2A'], [5, 3, 6, 1, '#7DD3FC'], [5, 5, 9, 1, '#7DD3FC'], [0, 10, 20, 2, '#94A3B8']];

const LAPTOP_AT = [76, 60] as const; // its 12 rows end on the desk top at y 72
/** The laptop's glass, where its loop frames draw the stage. */
export const LAPTOP_GLASS: Box = { x: LAPTOP_AT[0] + 3, y: LAPTOP_AT[1] + 1, w: 14, h: 8 };

const accentOn = (body: string) => {
  const n = parseInt(body.slice(1), 16);
  const lum = (0.299 * ((n >> 16) & 255) + 0.587 * ((n >> 8) & 255) + 0.114 * (n & 255)) / 255;
  return lum < 0.42 ? '#F5F5F4' : body;
};

/** The stage on a 14 × 8 glass: three lines and a cursor, three dots, or five bars. */
function laptopScreen(screen: MonitorScreen, k: 0 | 1 | 2, accent: string): Rects {
  const r: Rects = [];
  const { x, y } = LAPTOP_GLASS;
  const L = '#7DD3FC';
  if (screen === 'dots') [3, 6, 9].forEach((dx, i) => R(r, x + dx, y + 3, 2, 2, i === k ? accent : mixHex(accent, '#0F1A2A', 0.55)));
  else if (screen === 'chart') ([[2, 2], [4, 4], [6, 2], [8, 5], [10, 3]] as const).forEach(([bx, bh]) => R(r, x + bx, y + 7 - bh, 1, bh, bx === 8 ? accent : L));
  else { R(r, x + 2, y + 2, 6, 1, L); R(r, x + 2, y + 4, 9, 1, L); if (k !== 2) R(r, x + 9, y + 4, 1, 1, L); }
  return r;
}

const notebook: ItemArt = {
  id: 'desk.notebook', slot: 'desk', style: 'standard',
  draw: ({ ramp }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    deskFurniture(r);
    sprite(r, NOTEBOOK, 75, 68, ramp);
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const laptop: ItemArt = {
  id: 'desk.laptop', slot: 'desk', style: 'standard', emissive: true, frames: 3,
  draw: ({ ramp, night, screen }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    deskFurniture(r);
    // The board's laptop, with Spec A's v2 glass (no static lines: the loop frames carry the screen).
    sprite(r, LAPTOP.filter(([, , , , c]) => c !== '#7DD3FC' && c !== '#0F1A2A'), LAPTOP_AT[0], LAPTOP_AT[1], ramp);
    gradV(r, LAPTOP_GLASS.x, LAPTOP_GLASS.y, LAPTOP_GLASS.w, LAPTOP_GLASS.h, ['#0B1422', '#0F1A2A', '#132036']);
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    glow(emissive, LAPTOP_GLASS.x + 7, LAPTOP_GLASS.y + 4, 12, 8, '125,211,252', night ? 0.08 : 0.035);
    return { base: r, emissive, frames: three((k) => laptopScreen(screen, k, accentOn(ramp.body))) };
  },
};

registerItems([notebook, laptop]);
registerIcons({ 'desk.monitor': /* CatDesk.dc.html:89 */ MONITOR, 'desk.notebook': NOTEBOOK, 'desk.laptop': LAPTOP });
```

(`MONITOR` is the board's monitor sprite array, `CatDesk.dc.html:89`, copied verbatim.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Glows and loops**

```ts
// Desk lamps (CatFloor.dc.html:76 `lamp`): the board's lamp at (62, 59), a pool of light in the glow layer.
function deskLamp(id: string, head: string, shadeC: string, tint?: true): ItemArt {
  return {
    id, slot: 'lamp', emissive: true, ...(tint ? { tint } : {}),
    draw: ({ ramp, night }) => {
      const r: Rects = [];
      const h = tintOf(head, ramp), s = tintOf(shadeC, ramp);
      sprite(r, [[3, 11, 5, 2, '#334155'], [5, 3, 1, 8, '#334155'], [0, 0, 7, 3, h], [0, 2, 7, 1, s]], 62, 59, ramp);
      const emissive: Rects = [];
      glow(emissive, 65, 66, night ? 16 : 9, night ? 10 : 6, rgbOf(h), night ? 0.12 : 0.06);
      return { base: r, emissive };
    },
  };
}
const mint = deskLamp('lamp.mint', '#6EE7B7', '#34D399');
const kiplamp = deskLamp('lamp.kiplamp', '@b', '@s', true);
```

`lamp.salt` and `corner.floorlamp` add the same kind of glow (`glow(emissive, cx, cy, …, rgbOf(head), …)`) centred on
their lit part: the salt lamp's body `(66, 66)` in `#F97316`; the floor lamp's shade `(6, 50)` in `#FDE68A`, radii
`night ? 18 : 8` by `night ? 14 : 6`.

Loops (each returns `frames: three(...)`; the moving rects leave the base):

```ts
// Snow globe (shelf, CatDesk.dc.html:83): three flakes fall a cell a frame inside the glass.
const GLOBE_AT = [110, 21] as const;
const globeFrames = three((k) => {
  const f: Rects = [];
  [[2, 2], [5, 1], [4, 4]].forEach(([x, y], i) => px(f, GLOBE_AT[0] + x!, GLOBE_AT[1] + 1 + ((y! + k + i) % 5), '#F8FAFC'));
  return f;
});
// (the board's three '#F8FAFC' flakes are left out of the base)

// Lucky cat (CatDesk.dc.html:84): the raised paw (the rect at x 7, y -1, 1 × 3) waves down and up.
const CAT_AT = [110, 22] as const;
const catFrames = three((k) => { const f: Rects = []; R(f, CAT_AT[0] + 7, CAT_AT[1] - 1 + [0, 1, 0][k]!, 1, k === 1 ? 2 : 3, '#F8FAFC'); return f; });

// Punching bag (corner, CatFloor.dc.html:104): the bag's three red rects swing one cell right and back; the chain
// and the stand stay in the base. At (2, 52).
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Rugs** (`items/rug.ts`): v2 rugs lie in perspective like Spec A's slate rug

```ts
// Rugs (CatFloor.dc.html:113-121) on the floor in perspective: the board's flat 60 × 7 patterns become trapezoids
// with Spec A's slate rug outline (top edge x 40-134 at y 92, bottom x 30-144 at y 106).
import { registerIcons } from '../icons';
import { registerItems, type ItemArt } from '../items';
import { E, poly, type Rects } from '../paint';

const TOP = 92, BOT = 106;
const xl = (y: number) => 40 - ((y - TOP) * 10) / 14;
const xr = (y: number) => 134 + ((y - TOP) * 10) / 14;
const band = (r: Rects, y0: number, y1: number, c: string) => poly(r, [[xl(y0), y0], [xr(y0), y0], [xr(y1), y1], [xl(y1), y1]], c);
/** A column of the rug from fraction t0 to t1, rows y0 to y1. */
const cell = (r: Rects, t0: number, t1: number, y0: number, y1: number, c: string) => {
  const at = (t: number, y: number) => xl(y) + (xr(y) - xl(y)) * t;
  poly(r, [[at(t0, y0), y0], [at(t1, y0), y0], [at(t1, y1), y1], [at(t0, y1), y1]], c);
};

const rugs: ItemArt[] = [
  { id: 'rug.red', slot: 'rug', draw: () => { const r: Rects = []; band(r, TOP, BOT, '#9A3B3B'); band(r, TOP + 2, TOP + 3, '#7F2D2D'); band(r, BOT - 3, BOT - 2, '#7F2D2D'); return { base: r }; } },
  { id: 'rug.gymmat', slot: 'rug', draw: () => { const r: Rects = []; band(r, TOP, BOT, '#1F2937'); for (const t of [0.25, 0.5, 0.75]) cell(r, t, t + 0.01, TOP, BOT, '#111827'); return { base: r }; } },
  { id: 'rug.checker', slot: 'rug', draw: () => { const r: Rects = []; band(r, TOP, BOT, '#1F2937'); for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) if ((i + j) % 2 === 0) cell(r, i / 12, (i + 1) / 12, TOP + j * 7, TOP + (j + 1) * 7, '#F1F5F9'); return { base: r }; } },
  { id: 'rug.woven', slot: 'rug', draw: () => { const r: Rects = []; E(r, 87, 99, 50, 7, '#C08A5A'); E(r, 87, 99, 44, 5, '#A16207'); E(r, 87, 99, 38, 4, '#C08A5A'); E(r, 87, 99, 24, 2, '#92400E'); return { base: r }; } },
  { id: 'rug.tatami', slot: 'rug', draw: () => { const r: Rects = []; band(r, TOP, BOT, '#C9C08A'); band(r, TOP, TOP + 1, '#3F6212'); band(r, BOT - 1, BOT, '#3F6212'); for (let i = 1; i < 8; i++) cell(r, i / 8, i / 8 + 0.008, TOP + 1, BOT - 1, '#B5AB72'); return { base: r }; } },
];
registerItems(rugs);
```

Icons: the board's flat sprites (`rugStripe('#475569', '#334155')` for `rug.slate`, `:115`-`:119` for the rest).

  `lamp.amber` `lamp('#F59E0B', '#D97706')`, `rug.slate`).

```ts
import { checkSlotArt } from '../../jest-mocks/roomArtChecks';
import { CATALOGUE, catalogueSet } from '../../src/lib/room/catalogue';
import { drawRoom } from '../../src/lib/room/draw';
import { itemIcon } from '../../src/lib/room/icons';
import { itemArt } from '../../src/lib/room/items';

import { ROOM_SLOTS } from '../../src/lib/room/slots';

it('every catalogue id the app ships has art and an icon (a release can never offer an undrawable piece)', () => {
  const missing = CATALOGUE.items.filter((i) => !(itemArt(i.id) ?? wornArt(i.id)) || !itemIcon(i.id)?.length).map((i) => i.id);
  expect(missing).toEqual([]);
  for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) checkSlotArt(slot);
});

it.each(CATALOGUE.sets.map((s) => s.id))('the %s set draws deterministically, day and night, with four swatches', (id) => {
  const set = catalogueSet(id)!;
  expect(SET_SWATCHES[id]).toHaveLength(4);
  for (const night of [false, true]) {
    const input = { loadout: set.room, weather: 'mostly' as const, night, kip: { colour: 'lime' as const, pose: 'type' as const, mood: 'glance' as const, worn: { ...EMPTY_WORN, ...set.worn } }, screen: 'lines' as const };
    const d = drawRoom(input);
    expect(drawRoom(input)).toEqual(d);
    expect({ base: d.base.length, light: d.light.length, emissive: d.emissive.length, frames: d.frames.map((f) => f.length), kip: d.kip.length })
      .toMatchSnapshot(`${id} ${night ? 'night' : 'day'}`);
  }
});
```

`__tests__/perf/roomBuild.test.ts` (dev only, never gates CI):

```ts
import { drawRoomStatic } from '../../src/lib/room/draw';
import { CATALOGUE } from '../../src/lib/room/catalogue';
import { STARTER_LOADOUT } from '../../src/lib/room/loadout';

// Spec §8 rendering budget: the static room builds in under 8 ms on an iPhone 12 / Pixel 6 class device. This measures
// the pure build in Node for every set at night (the heaviest case) and logs it; run with ROOM_PERF=1. Never logs user data.
const run = process.env.ROOM_PERF === '1' ? describe : describe.skip;

run('room build time', () => {
  it.each([['starter', STARTER_LOADOUT] as const, ...CATALOGUE.sets.map((s) => [s.id, s.room] as const)])('%s at night', (name, loadout) => {
    const times: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      const t = performance.now();
      drawRoomStatic({ loadout, weather: 'stormy', night: true, colour: 'coral' });
      times.push(performance.now() - t);
    }
    times.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    console.info(JSON.stringify({ event: 'room.build', set: name, medianMs: Number(times[20]!.toFixed(2)) }));
    expect(times.length).toBe(40);
  });
});
```

```ts
// The Cottage set (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §1; SetCottage.dc.html, sets2.js:235-298): plaster and oak beams,
// light oak boards, gingham curtains with a window box, an embroidery hoop, jars and plates, a farmhouse table with
// the sage typewriter, a rose teapot, fairy lights on the beam, a wood stove with a flickering fire, a patchwork rug
// and a dog asleep in its bed. Each piece is redrawn inside its own slot's anchor (the board composes one scene on
// its own layout); the fern is the starter plant.
import { registerIcons, type SpriteRect } from '../../icons';
import { registerItems, type ItemArt } from '../../items';
import { FLOOR_Y, ROOM_H, ROOM_W, WALL_X0, WALL_X1 } from '../../grid';
import { clipB, E, floorSeams, glow, gradV, leaf, poly, px, R, shade, type Rects } from '../../paint';
import { windowSky } from '../../weather';
import { darkenHex, mixHex, three } from '../shared';

export const COTTAGE_SWATCHES = ['#E9DFC9', '#6B4A2F', '#8BA77A', '#E8A0A0'] as const;

const wallpaper: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.wallpaper', slot: 'wallpaper',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    gradV(r, WALL_X0, 0, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y, ['#EFE6D2', '#E9DFC9', '#E2D7BE', '#D8CBAE']);
    poly(r, [[0, 0], [WALL_X0, 0], [WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12]], '#C9BB9B');
    poly(r, [[WALL_X1, 0], [ROOM_W, 0], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y]], '#C9BB9B');
    for (let x = 12; x < WALL_X1; x += 6) for (let y = 6 + (x % 4) * 3; y < FLOOR_Y; y += 13) px(r, x, y, '#DDD1B6');
    R(r, WALL_X0, 4, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 5, '#6B4A2F'); R(r, WALL_X0, 4, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 1, '#8A6542'); R(r, WALL_X0, 8, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 1, '#523822');
    for (const x of [60, 104]) { R(r, x, 9, 4, FLOOR_Y - 9, '#6B4A2F'); R(r, x, 9, 1, FLOOR_Y - 9, '#8A6542'); }
    R(r, WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y - 2, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 2, '#B8A88A');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const floor: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.floor', slot: 'floor',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    poly(r, [[WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [ROOM_W, ROOM_H], [0, ROOM_H], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12]], '#C19A6B');
    floorSeams(r, 12, '#A57F52', [82, 93]);
    for (let x = 20; x < WALL_X1; x += 24) poly(r, [[x + 6, FLOOR_Y + 4], [x + 7, FLOOR_Y + 4], [x + 7, FLOOR_Y + 6], [x + 6, FLOOR_Y + 6]], '#A57F52');
    return { base: clipB(r, [0, 0, ROOM_W, ROOM_H]) };
  },
};

const window: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.window', slot: 'window',
  draw: ({ weather, night }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    R(r, 18, 12, 36, 38, '#FFFFFF'); R(r, 19, 13, 34, 36, '#6B4A2F');
    windowSky(r, { x: 21, y: 15, w: 30, h: 32 }, weather, night);
    R(r, 35, 15, 2, 32, '#F5F1E6'); R(r, 21, 30, 30, 2, '#F5F1E6');
    R(r, 16, 48, 40, 2, '#8A6542'); R(r, 18, 50, 36, 5, '#8B5A3C'); R(r, 18, 50, 36, 1, '#A87350');
    ([[21, '#E8A0A0'], [26, '#F472B6'], [31, '#FDE047'], [36, '#E8A0A0'], [41, '#FB7185'], [46, '#F5F1E6'], [50, '#F472B6']] as const).forEach(([x, c], k) => {
      E(r, x + 1, 47, 2.2, 2.2, '#6E9A4A'); E(r, x + 1, 45.5 - (k % 2), 1.6, 1.6, c); px(r, x + 1, 45 - (k % 2), '#FDE68A');
    });
    poly(r, [[16, 12], [25, 12], [23, 30], [19, 54], [16, 54]], '#E8A0A0');
    poly(r, [[47, 12], [56, 12], [56, 54], [53, 54], [49, 30]], '#E8A0A0');
    for (let y = 14; y < 54; y += 4) { R(r, 16, y, 4, 1, '#F5C6C6'); R(r, 52, y, 4, 1, '#F5C6C6'); }
    R(r, 18, 30, 4, 2, '#8BA77A'); R(r, 50, 30, 4, 2, '#8BA77A'); R(r, 16, 12, 40, 1, '#523822');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const art: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.art', slot: 'art',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    const cx = 141, cy = 25;
    R(r, cx - 1, 14, 2, 3, '#B88A5A');
    E(r, cx, cy, 9, 9, '#B88A5A'); E(r, cx, cy, 8, 8, '#F5F1E6'); E(r, cx, cy, 7, 7, '#EFE8D8');
    leaf(r, cx, cy + 6, cx - 4, cy - 2, 1.4, '#6E9A4A'); leaf(r, cx, cy + 6, cx + 5, cy - 1, 1.4, '#6E9A4A');
    E(r, cx, cy - 3, 2.6, 2.6, '#E8A0A0'); E(r, cx, cy - 3, 1, 1, '#FDE047'); E(r, cx - 4.5, cy + 2, 1.6, 1.6, '#F472B6'); E(r, cx + 5, cy + 3, 1.6, 1.6, '#93C5FD');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const shelf: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.shelf', slot: 'shelf',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    R(r, 96, 30, 34, 2, '#6B4A2F'); R(r, 96, 30, 34, 1, '#8A6542');
    poly(r, [[98, 32], [102, 32], [98, 34]], '#6B4A2F'); poly(r, [[124, 32], [128, 32], [128, 34]], '#6B4A2F');
    for (const [x, c] of [[102, '#93C5FD'], [112, '#E8A0A0']] as const) { E(r, x, 24, 5, 5, '#F5F1E6'); E(r, x, 24, 3.6, 3.6, c); E(r, x, 24, 2.4, 2.4, '#F5F1E6'); }
    R(r, 97, 28, 20, 2, '#8A6542');
    for (const [x, c] of [[118, '#F59E0B'], [123, '#C2410C'], [127, '#8BA77A']] as const) {
      const w = x === 127 ? 3 : 4;
      R(r, x, 23, w, 7, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'); R(r, x, 25, w, 5, c); R(r, x, 22, w, 2, '#D9C79E');
    }
    return { base: r };
  },
};

// The farmhouse table and chair where Spec A's desk stands, and the sage typewriter where the monitor stands
// (sets2.js:276-279 moved by (-36, +9)). No screen: the stage's dots and chart are not shown (as the notebook).
const desk: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.desk', slot: 'desk', style: 'standard',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 88, 98, 30, 3, 0.12);
    R(r, 64, 76, 3, 23, darkenHex('#8A6542', 0.85)); R(r, 109, 76, 3, 23, darkenHex('#8A6542', 0.85));
    E(r, 65.5, 86, 2.6, 1.4, '#A87350'); E(r, 110.5, 86, 2.6, 1.4, '#A87350');
    R(r, 62, 72, 52, 4, '#C19A6B'); R(r, 62, 72, 52, 1, mixHex('#C19A6B', '#FFFFFF', 0.22)); R(r, 62, 76, 52, 2, '#8A6542');
    // The chair, Spec A's geometry in oak.
    shade(r, 123, 96, 18, 2);
    R(r, 136, 58, 3, 29, '#8A6542'); R(r, 138, 58, 1, 29, '#B88A5A');
    R(r, 108, 84, 31, 3, '#8A6542'); R(r, 108, 84, 31, 1, '#B88A5A');
    R(r, 110, 87, 2, 9, darkenHex('#8A6542', 0.8)); R(r, 134, 87, 2, 9, darkenHex('#8A6542', 0.8));
    // The sage typewriter.
    R(r, 80, 54, 14, 7, '#F5F1E6'); R(r, 81, 56, 10, 1, '#9CA3AF'); R(r, 81, 58, 8, 1, '#9CA3AF');
    R(r, 76, 61, 22, 9, '#8BA77A'); R(r, 76, 61, 22, 1, '#A9C39A'); R(r, 74, 67, 26, 5, '#6E8A5E');
    for (let x = 77; x < 97; x += 3) { px(r, x, 68, '#F5F1E6'); px(r, x + 1, 70, '#F5F1E6'); }
    R(r, 75, 62, 1, 4, '#4A4340'); R(r, 98, 62, 1, 4, '#4A4340');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const deskItem: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.desk_item', slot: 'desk_item',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 106, 72, 4, 1, 0.2);
    E(r, 107, 69, 3.4, 3, '#E8A0A0'); E(r, 105.5, 67.5, 1.4, 1, '#F5C6C6');
    R(r, 105, 65, 4, 1, '#E8A0A0'); px(r, 106, 64, '#C27878');
    poly(r, [[104, 68], [102, 65], [102.6, 64.6], [104.6, 67]], '#E8A0A0');
    R(r, 109, 67, 1, 3, '#C27878');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

// Fairy lights along the beam (sets2.js:289-295): the wire in the base, the bulbs twinkle in the frames, a warm glow
// at night. Outside the lamp anchor by design (ANCHOR_EXEMPT).
const BULBS = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => 14 + i * 6).map((x) => ({ x, y: 10 + Math.round(3 * Math.sin(((x - 14) / 144) * Math.PI * 3) ** 2) }));
const lamp: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.lamp', slot: 'lamp', emissive: true, frames: 3,
  draw: ({ night }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    for (const { x, y } of BULBS) px(r, x, y - 1, '#4A4340');
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    if (night) glow(emissive, 85, 10, 70, 10, '253,224,150', 0.05);
    else glow(emissive, 85, 10, 40, 6, '253,224,150', 0.03);
    const frames = three((k) => {
      const f: Rects = [];
      BULBS.forEach(({ x, y }, i) => {
        const lit = (i + k) % 4 !== 0;
        px(f, x, y, lit ? '#FDE68A' : '#B59A55');
        if (lit && night) R(f, x - 1, y - 1, 3, 3, 'rgba(253,230,138,0.18)');
      });
      return f;
    });
    return { base: r, emissive, frames };
  },
};

// A slim wood stove in the corner, its pipe up the side wall; the fire flickers in the door (sets2.js:290-292).
const corner: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.corner', slot: 'corner', emissive: true, frames: 3,
  draw: ({ night }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 7, 95, 8, 1.5);
    R(r, 5, 30, 4, 30, '#2F2A28'); R(r, 5, 30, 1, 30, '#4A4340');
    R(r, 0, 60, 14, 3, '#3A3431'); R(r, 1, 63, 12, 29, '#2F2A28'); R(r, 1, 63, 12, 1, '#4A4340');
    R(r, 3, 70, 8, 10, '#1A1513'); R(r, 4, 71, 6, 8, '#3A1A0C');
    R(r, 2, 92, 2, 3, '#2F2A28'); R(r, 10, 92, 2, 3, '#2F2A28');
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    if (night) glow(emissive, 7, 76, 22, 14, '251,146,60', 0.07);
    else glow(emissive, 7, 76, 8, 6, '251,146,60', 0.05);
    const frames = three((k) => {
      const f: Rects = [];
      const sh = [0, 1, -1][k]!;
      E(f, 7 + sh * 0.5, 77, 3, 2, '#C2410C'); E(f, 7 - sh, 76, 2.2, 2, '#F97316'); E(f, 7 + sh, 75, 1.2, 1.6, '#FDE047');
      return f;
    });
    return { base: r, emissive, frames };
  },
};

const rug: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.rug', slot: 'rug',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    const xl = (y: number) => 40 - ((y - 92) * 10) / 14, xr = (y: number) => 134 + ((y - 92) * 10) / 14;
    poly(r, [[40, 92], [134, 92], [144, 106], [30, 106]], '#F5F1E6');
    const qc = ['#E8A0A0', '#8BA77A', '#F5D58A', '#93C5FD'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
      const ya = 93 + j * 7, yb = ya + 6, t0 = i / 8, t1 = (i + 1) / 8;
      const at = (t: number, y: number) => xl(y) + (xr(y) - xl(y)) * t;
      poly(r, [[at(t0, ya) + 0.5, ya], [at(t1, ya) - 0.5, ya], [at(t1, yb) - 0.5, yb], [at(t0, yb) + 0.5, yb]], qc[(i + j * 2) % 4]!);
    }
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const pet: ItemArt = {
  id: 'set.cottage.pet', slot: 'pet',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 42, 105, 12, 1.2);
    E(r, 42, 101, 12, 4, '#8BA77A'); E(r, 42, 100, 9.5, 2.6, '#6E8A5E');
    E(r, 41, 98.5, 7.5, 2.4, '#D9A066'); E(r, 46, 97, 3.2, 2.2, '#D9A066');
    poly(r, [[44, 96], [45, 93], [47, 96]], '#B07A44'); px(r, 47, 97, '#1F1A17');
    E(r, 38, 99, 3, 1.2, '#E8BB85'); R(r, 31, 103, 22, 1, '#5E7A50');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

registerItems([wallpaper, floor, window, art, shelf, desk, deskItem, lamp, corner, rug, pet]);
```

Icons: for each piece, its own base rects cropped to their bounds (`registerIcons` with

coral `ItemContext`). In `art.ts` add `import './items/sets/cottage';` and

- [ ] **Step 3: The bundle-size script** (spec §8; ruling 23)

`cd mobile && npm install --save-dev --save-exact esbuild@0.25` (record the installed version in the ledger).

`mobile/scripts/room-art-size.mjs`:

```js
// Spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §8: the room's item art must add at most 150 KB (minified) to the JS bundle. Bundles
// src/lib/room/art.ts (every item file and what they import) with esbuild and prints the size. Run by hand before each
// art batch:  node scripts/room-art-size.mjs
import { build } from 'esbuild';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';

const root = path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const LIMIT = 150 * 1024;
const result = await build({
  entryPoints: [path.join(root, 'src/lib/room/art.ts')],
  bundle: true, minify: true, write: false, format: 'esm', platform: 'neutral', logLevel: 'silent',
  loader: { '.json': 'json' },
});
const bytes = result.outputFiles[0].contents.length;
console.log(`room item art: ${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB minified (limit ${LIMIT / 1024} KB)`);
process.exit(bytes > LIMIT ? 1 : 0);
```

Run: `node scripts/room-art-size.mjs`. Expected: under 150 KB, exit 0. Record the number in the ledger. (`art.ts`
imports no React Native module; if esbuild reports one, mark it `external`.)

```ts
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { sourceFiles } from '../../jest-mocks/sourceScan';

// Spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §7: every <Kip> and <PersonKip> passes what that KIP wears. The only exceptions draw a
// KIP that is nobody's (signed-out screens, the dev gallery) or a colour preview.
const SRC = path.join(__dirname, '../../src');
const EXEMPT: Record<string, string> = {
  'screens/SignUpScreen.tsx': 'signed out: a coral KIP that is nobody yet',
  'screens/ForgotPasswordScreen.tsx': 'signed out',
  'screens/ResetPasswordScreen.tsx': 'signed out',
  'components/onboarding-hero.tsx': 'the onboarding mascot before any account data loads',
  'screens/dev/KipGalleryScreen.tsx': 'the dev gallery draws every pose and mood bare',
  'components/kip/Kip.tsx': 'the component itself',
  'components/kip/PersonKip.tsx': 'the component itself',
};

it('every Kip and PersonKip outside the exemptions passes worn', () => {
  const hits = sourceFiles(SRC).flatMap((file) => {
    const rel = path.relative(SRC, file);
    if (EXEMPT[rel]) return [];
    const text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    const tags = text.match(/<(Kip|PersonKip)\b[^>]*>/gs) ?? [];
    return tags.filter((t) => !/\bworn=/.test(t)).map((t) => `${rel}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
  });
  expect(hits).toEqual([]);
});
```
