- [ ] **Step 2: `src/lib/room/decorate.ts`**

```ts
// Decorate mode's draft (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §6.4), pure. The draft holds only owned pieces; a locked item or
// a locked set is a display-only preview (ruling 18), so Done can only ever save what you own. Undo steps back one
// change of the draft; Apply set is one change.
import { catalogueSet } from './catalogue';
import type { ItemId, KipWorn, RoomLoadout } from './loadout';
import { ROOM_SLOTS, WORN_SLOTS, type RoomSlot, type WornSlot } from './slots';

export interface Look {
  room: RoomLoadout;
  worn: KipWorn;
}
export type DecorateTab = 'room' | 'kip' | 'sets';
export type DecorateSlot = RoomSlot | WornSlot;
export type Preview = { kind: 'item'; slot: DecorateSlot; itemId: ItemId } | { kind: 'set'; setId: string };

export interface DecorateState {
  saved: Look;
  draft: Look;
  history: Look[];
  preview: Preview | null;
  tab: DecorateTab;
  roomSlot: RoomSlot;
  wornSlot: WornSlot;
  /** The tile tapped last (null itemId: the None tile). */
  picked: { slot: DecorateSlot; itemId: ItemId | null } | null;
  notice: 'refreshed' | 'saveFailed' | null;
}

export type DecorateAction =
  | { type: 'tab'; tab: DecorateTab }
  | { type: 'slot'; slot: DecorateSlot }
  | { type: 'place'; slot: DecorateSlot; itemId: ItemId | null }
  | { type: 'preview'; slot: DecorateSlot; itemId: ItemId }
  | { type: 'applySet'; setId: string }
  | { type: 'previewSet'; setId: string }
  | { type: 'undo' }
  | { type: 'refreshed'; look: Look }
  | { type: 'saveFailed' }
  | { type: 'saved'; look: Look };

export const isWornSlot = (slot: DecorateSlot): slot is WornSlot => (WORN_SLOTS as readonly string[]).includes(slot);

export function slotValue(look: Look, slot: DecorateSlot): ItemId | null {
  return isWornSlot(slot) ? look.worn[slot] : look.room[slot];
}

function withSlot(look: Look, slot: DecorateSlot, id: ItemId | null): Look {
  return isWornSlot(slot) ? { ...look, worn: { ...look.worn, [slot]: id } } : { ...look, room: { ...look.room, [slot]: id } };
}

export function lookEquals(a: Look, b: Look): boolean {
  return ROOM_SLOTS.every((s) => a.room[s] === b.room[s]) && WORN_SLOTS.every((s) => a.worn[s] === b.worn[s]);
}

export function initDecorate(saved: Look, open: { tab?: DecorateTab; slot?: DecorateSlot } = {}): DecorateState {
  const { slot } = open;
  const worn = slot !== undefined && isWornSlot(slot);
  return {
    saved, draft: saved, history: [], preview: null,
    tab: open.tab ?? (worn ? 'kip' : 'room'),
    roomSlot: slot !== undefined && !worn ? (slot as RoomSlot) : 'wallpaper',
    wornSlot: worn ? (slot as WornSlot) : 'head',
    picked: null, notice: null,
  };
}

/** A tile tapped in a slot also makes it the slot the info line talks about. */
const focusOn = (slot: DecorateSlot): Partial<DecorateState> => (isWornSlot(slot) ? { wornSlot: slot } : { roomSlot: slot });

function commit(s: DecorateState, next: Look): DecorateState {
  if (lookEquals(next, s.draft)) return { ...s, preview: null };
  return { ...s, history: [...s.history, s.draft], draft: next, preview: null, notice: null };
}

export function decorateReducer(s: DecorateState, a: DecorateAction): DecorateState {
  switch (a.type) {
    case 'tab':
      return { ...s, tab: a.tab, preview: null };
    case 'slot':
      return isWornSlot(a.slot) ? { ...s, tab: 'kip', wornSlot: a.slot, preview: null } : { ...s, tab: 'room', roomSlot: a.slot, preview: null };
    case 'place':
      return { ...commit(s, withSlot(s.draft, a.slot, a.itemId)), ...focusOn(a.slot), picked: { slot: a.slot, itemId: a.itemId } };
    case 'preview':
      return { ...s, ...focusOn(a.slot), preview: { kind: 'item', slot: a.slot, itemId: a.itemId }, picked: { slot: a.slot, itemId: a.itemId }, notice: null };
    case 'applySet': {
      const set = catalogueSet(a.setId);
      return set ? commit(s, { room: { ...set.room }, worn: { ...s.draft.worn, ...set.worn } }) : s;
    }
    case 'previewSet':
      return { ...s, preview: { kind: 'set', setId: a.setId }, notice: null };
    case 'undo': {
      const last = s.history[s.history.length - 1];
      return last ? { ...s, draft: last, history: s.history.slice(0, -1), preview: null, picked: null } : { ...s, preview: null };
    }
    case 'refreshed':
      return { ...s, saved: a.look, draft: a.look, history: [], preview: null, picked: null, notice: 'refreshed' };
    case 'saveFailed':
      return { ...s, notice: 'saveFailed' };
    case 'saved':
      return { ...s, saved: a.look, draft: a.look, history: [], preview: null, notice: null };
  }
}

export function isChanged(s: DecorateState): boolean {
  return !lookEquals(s.draft, s.saved);
}

/** What the room and KIP show: the draft with any locked preview on top. */
export function displayLook(s: DecorateState): Look {
  const p = s.preview;
  if (!p) return s.draft;
  if (p.kind === 'item') return withSlot(s.draft, p.slot, p.itemId);
  const set = catalogueSet(p.setId);
  return set ? { room: { ...set.room }, worn: { ...s.draft.worn, ...set.worn } } : s.draft;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `src/lib/room/tray.ts`**

```ts
// The tray's content (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §6.2, §6.3), pure: which pieces a slot offers, each tile's tag and
// spoken label, the info line, the set cards. A piece is offered only when this build can draw it (§1).
import { wornArt } from '../kip/wornArt';
import { CATALOGUE, catalogueItem, catalogueItemsFor, catalogueSet, type CatalogueItem } from './catalogue';
import { isWornSlot, slotValue, type DecorateSlot, type DecorateState } from './decorate';
import { itemArt } from './items';
import type { ItemId } from './loadout';
import { ROOM_COPY, SET_NAMES, itemName, progressLine, spokenName, unlockLine, unlockSpoken, type ProgressFacts } from './roomCopy';
import { spokenLine } from '../kipStage';
import { SET_SWATCHES } from './art';

export interface TrayContext {
  owned: ReadonlySet<string>;
  facts: ProgressFacts;
}

export interface TileModel {
  key: string;
  itemId: ItemId | null;
  name: string;
  state: 'placed' | 'owned' | 'locked';
  tag: string;
  label: string;
  flags: string[];
  selected: boolean;
}

const OPTIONAL = new Set<DecorateSlot>(['art', 'corner', 'pet', 'head', 'face', 'neck', 'ears']);

export function offeredItems(slot: DecorateSlot, owned: ReadonlySet<string>): CatalogueItem[] {
  const drawable = (i: CatalogueItem) => (isWornSlot(slot) ? !!wornArt(i.id) : !!itemArt(i.id));
  const rank = (i: CatalogueItem) => (i.unlock.kind === 'starter' ? 0 : owned.has(i.id) ? 1 : 2);
  return catalogueItemsFor(slot)
    .filter((i) => drawable(i) && (!i.retired || owned.has(i.id)))
    .map((i, n) => ({ i, n }))
    .sort((a, b) => rank(a.i) - rank(b.i) || a.n - b.n)
    .map(({ i }) => i);
}

export function tilesFor(state: DecorateState, slot: DecorateSlot, ctx: TrayContext): TileModel[] {
  const current = slotValue(state.draft, slot);
  const picked = (id: ItemId | null) => state.picked?.slot === slot && state.picked.itemId === id;
  const tiles: TileModel[] = [];
  if (OPTIONAL.has(slot)) {
    const name = slot === 'art' ? ROOM_COPY.noArt : ROOM_COPY.none;
    const placed = current === null;
    tiles.push({
      key: `${slot}-none`, itemId: null, name, state: placed ? 'placed' : 'owned', tag: placed ? ROOM_COPY.tags.placed : ROOM_COPY.tags.owned,
      label: placed ? ROOM_COPY.tile.placed(name) : ROOM_COPY.tile.owned(name), flags: [], selected: picked(null),
    });
  }
  for (const item of offeredItems(slot, ctx.owned)) {
    const placed = current === item.id;
    const owned = ctx.owned.has(item.id) || item.unlock.kind === 'starter';
    const spoken = spokenName(item.id);
    tiles.push({
      key: item.id,
      itemId: item.id,
      name: itemName(item.id),
      state: placed ? 'placed' : owned ? 'owned' : 'locked',
      tag: placed ? ROOM_COPY.tags.placed : owned ? ROOM_COPY.tags.owned : unlockLine(item.unlock),
      label: placed ? ROOM_COPY.tile.placed(spoken) : owned ? ROOM_COPY.tile.owned(spoken) : ROOM_COPY.tile.locked(spoken, unlockSpoken(item.unlock), progressLine(item.unlock, ctx.facts)),
      flags: [item.tint && ROOM_COPY.flags.tint, item.emissive && ROOM_COPY.flags.glow, item.frames && ROOM_COPY.flags.anim].filter((f): f is string => !!f),
      selected: picked(item.id),
    });
  }
  return tiles;
}

export interface InfoLine {
  label: string;
  text: string;
  muted: boolean;
  spoken: string;
}

const line = (label: string, text: string, muted = false): InfoLine => ({ label, text, muted, spoken: spokenLine(`${label} · ${text}`) });

export function infoLine(state: DecorateState, ctx: TrayContext): InfoLine {
  const I = ROOM_COPY.info;
  const p = state.preview;
  if (p?.kind === 'set') return line(I.locked, I.lockedText(SET_NAMES[p.setId] ?? p.setId, unlockLine(catalogueSet(p.setId)!.unlock)), true);
  if (p?.kind === 'item') return line(I.locked, I.lockedText(itemName(p.itemId), unlockLine(catalogueItem(p.itemId)!.unlock)), true);
  if (state.tab === 'sets') return line(I.sets, I.setsText);
  const slot: DecorateSlot = state.tab === 'kip' ? state.wornSlot : state.roomSlot;
  const current = slotValue(state.draft, slot);
  if (state.tab === 'kip') return current ? line(I.wearing, itemName(current)) : line(I.dress, I.dressText, true);
  if (state.picked?.slot === slot && state.picked.itemId !== null && current === state.picked.itemId && slotValue(state.saved, slot) !== current) {
    return line(I.previewing, I.previewText(itemName(current), slot));
  }
  return current ? line(I.inRoom, I.placedText(itemName(current))) : line(I.inRoom, I.emptyText(slot));
}

export interface SetCardModel {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  swatches: readonly string[];
  pieces: number;
  line: string;
  spoken: string;
  owned: boolean;
}

export function setCards(ctx: TrayContext): SetCardModel[] {
  return CATALOGUE.sets.map((set) => {
    const ids = [...Object.values(set.room), ...Object.values(set.worn)] as ItemId[];
    const owned = ids.every((id) => ctx.owned.has(id) || catalogueItem(id)?.unlock.kind === 'starter');
    return {
      id: set.id, name: SET_NAMES[set.id] ?? set.id, swatches: SET_SWATCHES[set.id] ?? [], pieces: ids.length,
      line: ROOM_COPY.set.pieces(ids.length, unlockLine(set.unlock)), spoken: ROOM_COPY.set.piecesSpoken(ids.length, unlockSpoken(set.unlock)), owned,
    };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `hotspots.ts` and `decorateOpen.ts`**

```ts
// Where each slot's "+ ART" tag sits over the decorate room (spec §6.2; ruling 15): a cell per slot near its anchor
// (the engine's layout, not K4b's v1 one), turned into points by the crop and clamped inside the view.
import { roomCrop } from '../../components/room/roomCrop';
import { FOCUS_BOX } from './grid';
import type { RoomSlot } from './slots';

export const HOTSPOT_CELLS: Readonly<Record<RoomSlot, { x: number; y: number }>> = {
  wallpaper: { x: 66, y: 1 }, floor: { x: 132, y: 100 }, window: { x: 18, y: 13 }, art: { x: 128, y: 38 },
  shelf: { x: 94, y: 9 }, desk: { x: 76, y: 78 }, desk_item: { x: 96, y: 50 }, plant: { x: 136, y: 70 },
  lamp: { x: 46, y: 46 }, corner: { x: 1, y: 30 }, rug: { x: 60, y: 92 }, pet: { x: 20, y: 102 },
};
export const TAG_W = 64;
export const TAG_H = 18;

export function hotspotPositions(crop: { width: number; height: number }, cellPt: number): Record<RoomSlot, { left: number; top: number }> {
  const off = roomCrop({ width: crop.width, height: crop.height, cellPt, box: FOCUS_BOX });
  const clamp = (v: number, max: number) => Math.max(2, Math.min(max - 2, Math.round(v)));
  return Object.fromEntries(
    (Object.keys(HOTSPOT_CELLS) as RoomSlot[]).map((slot) => {
      const c = HOTSPOT_CELLS[slot];
      return [slot, { left: clamp(off.x + c.x * cellPt, crop.width - TAG_W), top: clamp(off.y + c.y * cellPt, crop.height - TAG_H) }];
    }),
  ) as Record<RoomSlot, { left: number; top: number }>;
}
```

If the test finds two tags at the same spot at some width (the clamp can push two edge tags together), move one of the
cells inward; check the 12 tags by eye on the simulator in Task 16.

```ts
// Whether decorate mode is open (ruling 17): the KIP page's room and thumbnail pause their loops while it is.
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

let open = false;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
export function setDecorateOpen(next: boolean): void {
  open = next;
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}
const subscribe = (l: () => void) => { listeners.add(l); return () => listeners.delete(l); };
export function useDecorateOpen(): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, () => open, () => open);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The components and the screen**

`components/decorate/HotspotLayer.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { hotspotPositions, TAG_H, TAG_W } from '../../lib/room/hotspots';
import { ROOM_COPY } from '../../lib/room/roomCopy';
import { ROOM_SLOTS, type RoomSlot } from '../../lib/room/slots';

// The 12 hotspot tags over the decorate room (spec §6.2, K4b), and the dashed LOCKED tag on a locked preview's slot.
// Each tag is a raw Pressable: a label placed on the picture (one buttons-guard entry for the kind).
export function HotspotLayer({ crop, lockedSlot, onSelect }: {
  crop: { width: number; height: number }; lockedSlot: RoomSlot | null; onSelect: (slot: RoomSlot) => void;
}) {
  const at = hotspotPositions(crop, 2);
  return (
    <View pointerEvents="box-none" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, width: crop.width, height: crop.height }}>
      {ROOM_SLOTS.map((slot) =>
        slot === lockedSlot ? (
          <View key={slot} testID={`decorate-locked-${slot}`} accessible={false} style={{ position: 'absolute', ...at[slot], minWidth: TAG_W, height: TAG_H, borderWidth: 1, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: '#F5F5F4', borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(10,11,14,0.75)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            <Text className="font-pixel text-label text-[#F5F5F4]">{ROOM_COPY.locked}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <Pressable
            key={slot}
            testID={`decorate-hotspot-${slot}`}
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel={ROOM_COPY.hotspotLabel(slot)}
            onPress={() => onSelect(slot)}
            hitSlop={4}
            style={{ position: 'absolute', ...at[slot], minWidth: TAG_W, height: TAG_H, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(10,11,14,0.75)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 4 }}
          >
            <Text className="font-pixel text-label text-[#F5F5F4]" numberOfLines={1} maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.2}>{ROOM_COPY.hotspot(slot)}</Text>
          </Pressable>
        ),
      )}
    </View>
  );
}
```

(The tags keep the room's dark palette in both themes, as Home's hot tags do. If `text-[#F5F5F4]` trips the typography
guard, it does not: it is a colour, not a size.)

`components/decorate/DecorateTile.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { ItemIcon } from '../room/ItemIcon';
import type { KipColour } from '../../lib/kip/kipArt';
import type { TileModel } from '../../lib/room/tray';

const PLACED = '#4ADE80';
const FLAG: Record<string, string> = { TINT: '#C4B5FD', GLOW: '#FDE047', ANIM: '#67E8F9' };

// A tray tile (spec §6.3): the piece's icon at 3×, its name, a tag (Placed / Owned / the unlock line) and its flags.
// Locked tiles are at 45%. A raw Pressable (one buttons-guard entry): a picture you tap to place or preview.
export function DecorateTile({ tile, colour, onPress, testID }: { tile: TileModel; colour: KipColour; onPress: () => void; testID: string }) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      testID={testID}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={tile.label}
      accessibilityState={{ selected: tile.selected }}
      onPress={onPress}
      className="w-[31%] gap-1 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-2"
      style={{ opacity: tile.state === 'locked' ? 0.45 : 1, borderWidth: tile.selected ? 2 : 1, borderColor: tile.selected ? '#F5F5F4' : undefined }}
    >
      <View className="items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-background py-1">
        {tile.itemId ? <ItemIcon id={tile.itemId} colour={colour} /> : <View style={{ width: 48, height: 48 }} />}
      </View>
      <Text className="text-fine" numberOfLines={2}>{tile.name}</Text>
      <Text className="text-fine text-muted-foreground" style={tile.state === 'placed' ? { color: PLACED } : undefined} numberOfLines={2}>{tile.tag}</Text>
      {tile.flags.length ? (
        <View className="flex-row flex-wrap gap-1">
          {tile.flags.map((f) => (
            <Text key={f} className="rounded border px-1 font-pixel text-label" style={{ color: FLAG[f], borderColor: FLAG[f] }}>{f}</Text>
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

`components/decorate/DecorateTray.tsx` renders, by `state.tab`:
- **Room:** a horizontal `ScrollView` of 12 category chips — `Button` `size="sm"`, `variant={selected ? 'default' : 'outline'}`,
  `testID={`decorate-chip-${slot}`}`, `accessibilityState={{ selected }}`, label `SLOT_TITLES[slot]`, `onPress` →
  `dispatch({ type: 'slot', slot })` — then a `flex-row flex-wrap gap-2` grid of `DecorateTile`s from
  `tilesFor(state, state.roomSlot, ctx)` with `testID={`decorate-tile-${tile.key}`}` and
  `onPress={() => tile.state === 'locked' ? dispatch({ type: 'preview', slot, itemId: tile.itemId! }) : dispatch({ type: 'place', slot, itemId: tile.itemId })}`.

`screens/DecorateRoomScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, ScrollView, View, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useNavigation, useRoute } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/button';
import { PageTitle } from '../components/ui/page-title';
import { Skeleton } from '../components/ui/skeleton';
import { Text } from '../components/ui/text';
import { RoomView } from '../components/room/RoomView';
import { HotspotLayer } from '../components/decorate/HotspotLayer';
import { DecorateTray } from '../components/decorate/DecorateTray';
import { useAchievements } from '../lib/achievementsStore';
import { useMyKip } from '../lib/kip/myKip';
import { useTodayScene } from '../lib/home/useTodayScene';
import { useReducedMotion } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { decorateReducer, displayLook, initDecorate, isChanged, isWornSlot, type DecorateSlot, type DecorateTab } from '../lib/room/decorate';
import type { RoomSlot } from '../lib/room/slots';
import { setDecorateOpen } from '../lib/room/decorateOpen';
import { drawableLoadout } from '../lib/room/draw';
import { loadCosmetics, markCosmeticsSeen, useCosmetics } from '../lib/room/cosmeticsStore';
import { progressFromAchievements, ROOM_COPY } from '../lib/room/roomCopy';
import { infoLine, type TrayContext } from '../lib/room/tray';

const ROOM_HEIGHT = 220; // the whole 110-cell height at 2 points (ruling 16)

// Decorate mode (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §6; K4b): Cancel / DECORATE / Done, the room with hotspot tags, the info
// line with Undo, the Room / KIP / Sets tabs and the tray. A full-screen modal route; the KIP page's loops pause
// while it is open (ruling 17).
export function DecorateRoomScreen() {
  const navigation = useNavigation<any>();
  const params = (useRoute().params ?? {}) as { tab?: DecorateTab; slot?: DecorateSlot };
  const cosmetics = useCosmetics();
  useEffect(() => {
    setDecorateOpen(true);
    if (!cosmetics) void loadCosmetics();
    return () => setDecorateOpen(false);
  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  if (!cosmetics) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView className="flex-1 bg-background">
        <Skeleton testID="decorate-loading" className="m-4 h-56 rounded-lg" />
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }
  return <Decorate params={params} navigation={navigation} />;
}

function Decorate({ params, navigation }: { params: { tab?: DecorateTab; slot?: DecorateSlot }; navigation: any }) {
  const cosmetics = useCosmetics()!;
  const me = useMyKip();
  const achievements = useAchievements();
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const day = useTodayScene(navigation);
  const { width } = useWindowDimensions();
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(decorateReducer, undefined, () => initDecorate({ room: cosmetics.room, worn: cosmetics.worn }, params));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const leaving = useRef(false);

  // Opening decorate mode marks everything shown as seen (spec §4).
  useEffect(() => {
    void markCosmeticsSeen(cosmetics.fresh.map((f) => f.itemId));
  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const ctx: TrayContext = useMemo(
    () => ({ owned: cosmetics.owned, facts: progressFromAchievements(achievements, cosmetics.counters) }),
    [cosmetics.owned, cosmetics.counters, achievements],
  );
  const look = displayLook(state);
  const loadout = useMemo(() => drawableLoadout(look.room), [look.room]);
  const changed = isChanged(state);
  const info = infoLine(state, ctx);
  const lockedSlot: RoomSlot | null = state.preview?.kind === 'item' && !isWornSlot(state.preview.slot) ? state.preview.slot : null;

  // Cancel, the Android back button and a dismiss gesture all ask first when something changed.
  useEffect(() => navigation.addListener?.('beforeRemove', (e: { preventDefault: () => void; data: { action: unknown } }) => {
    if (leaving.current || !changed) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    Alert.alert(ROOM_COPY.discard.title, undefined, [
      { text: ROOM_COPY.discard.keep, style: 'cancel' },
      { text: ROOM_COPY.discard.discard, style: 'destructive', onPress: () => { leaving.current = true; navigation.dispatch(e.data.action); } },
    ]);
  }), [navigation, changed]);

  const cancel = () => {
    if (!changed) { leaving.current = true; navigation.goBack(); return; }
    Alert.alert(ROOM_COPY.discard.title, undefined, [
      { text: ROOM_COPY.discard.keep, style: 'cancel' },
      { text: ROOM_COPY.discard.discard, style: 'destructive', onPress: () => { leaving.current = true; navigation.goBack(); } },
    ]);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView className="flex-1 bg-background">
      <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between px-4 pb-2 pt-1">
        <Button testID="decorate-cancel" variant="ghost" onPress={cancel}>{ROOM_COPY.cancel}</Button>
        <PageTitle>{ROOM_COPY.title}</PageTitle>
        <Button testID="decorate-done" disabled={!changed} loading={saving} onPress={() => undefined /* Task 14 */}>{ROOM_COPY.done}</Button>
      </View>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 32 }}>
        <View style={{ width, height: ROOM_HEIGHT }}>
          <RoomView
            testID="decorate-room"
            loadout={loadout}
            worn={look.worn}
            weather={day.window.weather}
            night={day.window.night}
            colour={me.colour}
            pose="rest"
            mood="glance"
            screen="lines"
            bubble={null}
            crop={{ width, height: ROOM_HEIGHT }}
            cellPt={2}
            reduceMotion={reduceMotion}
            accessibilityLabel={info.spoken}
          />
          {state.tab === 'room' ? <HotspotLayer crop={{ width, height: ROOM_HEIGHT }} lockedSlot={lockedSlot} onSelect={(slot) => dispatch({ type: 'slot', slot })} /> : null}
        </View>
        <View className="flex-row items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3">
          <View testID="decorate-info" accessible accessibilityLabel={info.spoken} className="shrink grow gap-0.5">
            <Text className="font-pixel text-label text-muted-foreground">{info.label}</Text>
            <Text className={info.muted ? 'text-caption text-muted-foreground' : 'text-caption'}>{info.text}</Text>
          </View>
          <Button testID="decorate-undo" variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={state.history.length === 0} accessibilityLabel={ROOM_COPY.undoLabel} onPress={() => dispatch({ type: 'undo' })}>
            {ROOM_COPY.undo}
          </Button>
        </View>
        <View testID="decorate-tabs" accessibilityRole="tablist" accessibilityLabel={ROOM_COPY.tabsLabel} className="flex-row gap-2 px-4">
          {(['room', 'kip', 'sets'] as const).map((tab) => (
            <Button
              key={tab}
              testID={`decorate-tab-${tab}`}
              accessibilityRole="tab"
              accessibilityState={{ selected: state.tab === tab }}
              variant={state.tab === tab ? 'default' : 'outline'}
              className="flex-1"
              onPress={() => dispatch({ type: 'tab', tab })}
            >
              {ROOM_COPY.tabs[tab]}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </View>
        <DecorateTray state={state} dispatch={dispatch} ctx={ctx} colour={me.colour} />
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}
```

Reduce Motion comes from `useReducedMotion` (`react-native-reanimated`), as `CelebrationModal` reads it; the window's
weather from `useTodayScene` (`lib/home/useTodayScene.ts`, Spec A Task 7), as on the KIP page. The info line's spoken
label is `info.spoken` (`spokenLine` turns each " · " into ", ").

- [ ] **Step 7: The route, the entry points, the dot and the pause**

`navigation/RootNavigator.tsx`: `DecorateRoom: { tab?: DecorateTab; slot?: DecorateSlot } | undefined;` in
`RootStackParamList`, and

```tsx
<Stack.Screen name="DecorateRoom" component={DecorateRoomScreen} options={{ headerShown: false, presentation: 'fullScreenModal', gestureEnabled: false }} />
```

```tsx
  const save = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      // The draft holds only owned pieces and keeps every stored id you did not touch (rulings 18, 25).
      const next = await saveCosmeticsLoadout({ room: state.draft.room, worn: state.draft.worn, version: cosmetics.version });
      dispatch({ type: 'saved', look: { room: next.room, worn: next.worn } });
      leaving.current = true;
      navigation.goBack();
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && (err.status === 403 || err.status === 409)) {
        // Another device saved, or something you placed is no longer yours: show the server's room and stay.
        const fresh = await loadCosmetics();
        if (fresh) dispatch({ type: 'refreshed', look: { room: fresh.room, worn: fresh.worn } });
      } else {
        dispatch({ type: 'saveFailed' });
      }
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The Sets tab and `SetCard`**

```tsx
// A theme set's card (spec §6.2 Sets; K4b): four swatches, the name, "{n} pieces · {unlock line}", and Apply set
// (owned) or Preview (locked).
export function SetCard({ card, onApply, onPreview }: { card: SetCardModel; onApply: () => void; onPreview: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Card testID={`decorate-set-${card.id}`} className="gap-2 p-3">
      <View className="flex-row gap-1" accessible={false}>
        {card.swatches.map((c) => <View key={c} className="h-6 flex-1 rounded" style={{ backgroundColor: c }} />)}
      </View>
      <Text className="text-headline">{card.name}</Text>
      <Text className="text-caption text-muted-foreground" accessibilityLabel={card.spoken}>{card.line}</Text>
      <Button
        testID={`decorate-set-${card.id}-action`}
        variant={card.owned ? 'default' : 'outline'}
        accessibilityLabel={card.owned ? ROOM_COPY.set.applyLabel(card.name) : ROOM_COPY.set.previewLabel(card.name)}
        onPress={card.owned ? onApply : onPreview}
      >
        {card.owned ? ROOM_COPY.set.apply : ROOM_COPY.set.preview}
      </Button>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

The Sets tab renders `setCards(ctx).map((card) => <SetCard … onApply={() => dispatch({ type: 'applySet', setId: card.id })} onPreview={() => dispatch({ type: 'previewSet', setId: card.id })} />)`.

```ts
import { EMPTY_TOASTED, decorateTarget, nextToast, piecesForBadge } from '../../src/lib/room/announce';

const f = (itemId: string, source: string) => ({ itemId, source });

it('toasts a recap or cheer piece once, naming the first and counting the rest', () => {
  const fresh = [f('wall.panel', 'counter:recaps_read:4'), f('art.cork', 'counter:recaps_read:4'), f('set.cottage.desk', 'set:cottage')];
  const first = nextToast(fresh, EMPTY_TOASTED);
  expect(first.toast).toEqual({ kind: 'items', itemIds: ['wall.panel', 'art.cork', 'set.cottage.desk'], text: "New for KIP's room: Wood panel and 2 more" });
  expect(nextToast(fresh, first.toasted).toast).toBeNull();
});

it('never toasts a badge piece (the celebration names it)', () => {
  expect(nextToast([f('rug.red', 'family:SLEEP_GOAL:1'), f('shelf.trophyB', 'anyFamily:1')], EMPTY_TOASTED).toast).toBeNull();
});

it('summarises the backfill once, again only when more arrives, and forgets it once seen', () => {
  const backfill = [f('pet.bed', 'backfill'), f('rug.red', 'backfill'), f('wall.stripes', 'backfill')];
  const one = nextToast(backfill, EMPTY_TOASTED);
  expect(one.toast).toEqual({ kind: 'backfill', count: 3, text: "Your badges unlocked 3 things for KIP's room" });
  expect(nextToast(backfill, one.toasted).toast).toBeNull();
  const more = nextToast([...backfill, f('out.scarf', 'backfill')], one.toasted);
  expect(more.toast).toMatchObject({ kind: 'backfill', count: 4 });
  expect(nextToast([], more.toasted).toasted).toEqual(EMPTY_TOASTED);
});

it('drops ids from the device list once they are seen, so it stays small', () => {
  const { toasted } = nextToast([f('wall.panel', 'counter:recaps_read:4')], EMPTY_TOASTED);
  expect(nextToast([], toasted).toasted.ids).toEqual([]);
});

it('finds a celebration\'s pieces: its family at its level or below, and any family at those levels', () => {
  const fresh = [
    f('rug.red', 'family:SLEEP_GOAL:1'), f('art.motto', 'family:SLEEP_GOAL:2'), f('wall.brick', 'family:STEP_GOAL:2'),
    f('shelf.trophyB', 'anyFamily:1'), f('wall.panel', 'counter:recaps_read:4'),
  ];
  expect(piecesForBadge(fresh, 'SLEEP_GOAL', 2)).toEqual(['rug.red', 'art.motto', 'shelf.trophyB']);
  expect(piecesForBadge(fresh, 'SLEEP_GOAL', 1)).toEqual(['rug.red', 'shelf.trophyB']);
});

it('opens decorate mode on the piece\'s tab and slot', () => {
  expect(decorateTarget('rug.red')).toEqual({ tab: 'room', slot: 'rug' });
  expect(decorateTarget('out.scarf')).toEqual({ tab: 'kip', slot: 'neck' });
});
```

```tsx
jest.mock('../../src/navigation/navigationRef', () => ({
  MODAL_ROUTES: new Set(['RecapStory', 'DecorateRoom']),
  navigationRef: { isReady: () => true, getCurrentRoute: jest.fn(() => ({ name: 'Home' })), addListener: jest.fn(() => () => undefined), navigate: jest.fn() },
}));
const load = loadCosmetics as jest.Mock;
const store: Record<string, string> = {};
jest.spyOn(SecureStore, 'getItemAsync').mockImplementation(async (k) => store[k] ?? null);
jest.spyOn(SecureStore, 'setItemAsync').mockImplementation(async (k, v) => { store[k] = v; });

const cosmetics = (fresh: Array<{ itemId: string; source: string }>) => ({ fresh, owned: new Set(), room: {}, worn: {}, version: 1, counters: { recapsRead: 0, buddyCheers: 0 } });

beforeEach(() => { for (const k of Object.keys(store)) delete store[k]; jest.useFakeTimers(); });
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

it('shows a new piece once with a Decorate button that opens decorate mode on its slot, and hides after 6 s', async () => {
  load.mockResolvedValue(cosmetics([{ itemId: 'wall.panel', source: 'counter:recaps_read:4' }]));
  const s = render(<RoomToastHost />);
  await act(async () => undefined);
  expect(s.getByText("New for KIP's room: Wood panel")).toBeTruthy();
  fireEvent.press(s.getByTestId('room-toast-decorate'));
  expect(navigationRef.navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('DecorateRoom', { tab: 'room', slot: 'wallpaper' });
  s.unmount();
  const again = render(<RoomToastHost />);
  await act(async () => undefined);
  expect(again.queryByTestId('room-toast')).toBeNull(); // the device list remembers it
});

it('still toasts when the keychain throws (it may repeat later, it never crashes)', async () => {
  (SecureStore.getItemAsync as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('locked'));

  act(() => { jest.advanceTimersByTime(ROOM_TOAST_MS); });

});

it('waits while a modal route is up, and checks again on the next foreground', async () => {

  const s = render(<RoomToastHost />);

  expect(s.queryByTestId('room-toast')).toBeNull();
  (navigationRef.getCurrentRoute as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ name: 'Coach' });
  await act(async () => { listeners.forEach((l) => l('active')); });
  expect(s.getByTestId('room-toast')).toBeTruthy();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `src/lib/room/announce.ts`**

```ts
// Telling you something unlocked (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §4). Badge pieces are named by the celebration; recap,
// cheer and set pieces get one toast each time new ones arrive; the backfill gets one summary toast (ruling 11).
import { catalogueItem } from './catalogue';
import { isWornSlot, type DecorateSlot, type DecorateTab } from './decorate';
import { itemName, ROOM_COPY } from './roomCopy';

export interface Toasted {
  /** Unseen piece ids already toasted on this device. */
  ids: string[];
  /** How many unseen backfill rows the summary already covered. */
  backfill: number;
}
export const EMPTY_TOASTED: Toasted = { ids: [], backfill: 0 };

export type RoomToast =
  | { kind: 'items'; itemIds: string[]; text: string }
  | { kind: 'backfill'; count: number; text: string };

type Fresh = ReadonlyArray<{ itemId: string; source: string }>;
const MAX_IDS = 40;
const toastable = (source: string) => source.startsWith('counter:') || source.startsWith('set:');

export function nextToast(fresh: Fresh, toasted: Toasted): { toast: RoomToast | null; toasted: Toasted } {
  const unseen = new Set(fresh.map((f) => f.itemId));
  const ids = toasted.ids.filter((id) => unseen.has(id));
  const backfill = fresh.filter((f) => f.source === 'backfill').length;
  const covered = Math.min(toasted.backfill, backfill);
  if (backfill > covered) {
    return { toast: { kind: 'backfill', count: backfill, text: ROOM_COPY.backfill(backfill) }, toasted: { ids, backfill } };
  }
  const items = fresh.filter((f) => toastable(f.source) && !ids.includes(f.itemId)).map((f) => f.itemId);
  if (!items.length) return { toast: null, toasted: { ids, backfill: covered } };
  return {
    toast: { kind: 'items', itemIds: items, text: ROOM_COPY.newFor(itemName(items[0]!), items.length - 1) },
    toasted: { ids: [...ids, ...items].slice(-MAX_IDS), backfill: covered },
  };
}

/** The unseen pieces a celebration names: its family at its level or below, and any family at those levels (ruling 10). */
export function piecesForBadge(fresh: Fresh, family: string, level: number): string[] {
  return fresh
    .filter(({ source }) => {
      const fam = /^family:([A-Z_]+):(\d)$/.exec(source);
      if (fam) return fam[1] === family && Number(fam[2]) <= level;
      const any = /^anyFamily:(\d)$/.exec(source);
      return !!any && Number(any[1]) <= level;
    })
    .map((f) => f.itemId);
}

export function decorateTarget(itemId: string): { tab: DecorateTab; slot: DecorateSlot } {
  const slot = (catalogueItem(itemId)?.slot ?? 'wallpaper') as DecorateSlot;
  return { tab: isWornSlot(slot) ? 'kip' : 'room', slot };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `src/lib/room/toasted.ts`**

```ts
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { EMPTY_TOASTED, type Toasted } from './announce';

// The pieces this device has toasted (spec §4): SecureStore key `kipToasted`, a few ids and a count, far under the
// ~2 KB value limit. A keychain failure reads as nothing toasted: a toast may repeat, nothing breaks.
const KEY = 'kipToasted';

export async function readToasted(): Promise<Toasted> {
  try {
    const raw = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY);
    const parsed = raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<Toasted>) : null;
    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.ids) || typeof parsed.backfill !== 'number') return EMPTY_TOASTED;
    return { ids: parsed.ids.filter((i): i is string => typeof i === 'string'), backfill: parsed.backfill };
  } catch {
    return EMPTY_TOASTED;
  }
}

export async function writeToasted(t: Toasted): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY, JSON.stringify(t));
  } catch {
    // Held for this session only.
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `src/components/room/RoomToastHost.tsx`** (ruling 12)

```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AccessibilityInfo, AppState, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import Animated, { FadeOut, SlideInUp } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { Button } from '../ui/button';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { getAchievementsSnapshot, pendingLevels } from '../../lib/achievementsStore';
import { decorateTarget, nextToast, type RoomToast } from '../../lib/room/announce';
import { loadCosmetics } from '../../lib/room/cosmeticsStore';
import { ROOM_COPY } from '../../lib/room/roomCopy';
import { readToasted, writeToasted } from '../../lib/room/toasted';
import { MODAL_ROUTES, navigationRef } from '../../navigation/navigationRef';

export const ROOM_TOAST_MS = 6000;

/** A modal route is up, or a badge celebration is waiting (it names its own pieces). */
function held(): boolean {
  const route = navigationRef.isReady() ? navigationRef.getCurrentRoute()?.name : undefined;
  return (route !== undefined && MODAL_ROUTES.has(route)) || pendingLevels(getAchievementsSnapshot()).length > 0;
}

// "New for KIP's room: Light oak" with Decorate (spec 2026-10-14 KIP's room §4): checked on mount (sign-in), on every
// return to the foreground and whenever the KIP tab gains focus. Each piece once (kipToasted). Mounted inside the
// signed-in navigator.
export function RoomToastHost() {
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<RoomToast | null>(null);
  const busy = useRef(false);

  const check = useCallback(async () => {
    if (busy.current) return;
    busy.current = true;
    try {
      const cosmetics = await loadCosmetics();
      if (!cosmetics || held()) return;
      const { toast: next, toasted } = nextToast(cosmetics.fresh, await readToasted());
      await writeToasted(toasted);
      if (next) {
        setToast(next);
        AccessibilityInfo.announceForAccessibility(next.text);
      }
    } finally {
      busy.current = false;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void check();
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener('change', (state) => { if (state === 'active') void check(); });
    let last = navigationRef.isReady() ? navigationRef.getCurrentRoute()?.name : undefined;
    const off = navigationRef.addListener('state', () => {
      const name = navigationRef.getCurrentRoute()?.name;
      if (name === 'Coach' && last !== 'Coach') void check();
      last = name;
    });
    return () => { sub.remove(); off(); };
  }, [check]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return undefined;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), ROOM_TOAST_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  if (!toast) return null;
  const target = toast.kind === 'items' ? decorateTarget(toast.itemIds[0]!) : undefined;
  return (
    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} pointerEvents="box-none" style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}>
      <Animated.View entering={SlideInUp.duration(250)} exiting={FadeOut.duration(250)} testID="room-toast"
        className="mx-4 mt-2 flex-row items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card px-4 py-3">
        <Text className="shrink grow text-body">{toast.text}</Text>
        <Button testID="room-toast-decorate" variant="outline" size="sm" onPress={() => { setToast(null); navigationRef.navigate('DecorateRoom', target); }}>
          {ROOM_COPY.decorate}
        </Button>
      </Animated.View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}
```

Mount it in `RootNavigator.tsx` beside `<CelebrationHost />` (inside the signed-in tree, inside `ToastProvider`). If
`navigationRef.navigate` is typed strictly, pass `target ?? undefined` as the params of `DecorateRoom`.

## Self-review (2026-10-14, against the spec and the real code on `feature/kip-room`)

**Spec coverage, section by section:**

| Spec | Task |
| --- | --- |
| §0 owner decisions: v2 quality, 12 + 4 slots, ~190 items and 6 sets, earn only, unlock sources, own room only | 2 (catalogue), 9–11 (art), 3 (rules), Global Constraints |
| §0 defaults: a cheer per buddy per local day, the pre-launch backfill toast, a buddy's room later | 3, 15, not built |
| §1 where the catalogue lives, versioning, retired, `COSMETICS_RELEASE`, the export and its staleness test | 2 |
| §1 item shape, what is in it, unlock lines, counters used, no multi-slot items, empty slots, the window's sky | 2, 8 (lines), 9 (sky kept) |
| §1 theme sets (one item per room slot, a set unlocks all its pieces, colour never changes) | 2, 3, 11, 14 |
| §2 the two tables, owned = starters ∪ rows, never remove, account deletion, no row = starter, undrawable = starter in the app | 3, 5, 1 / 12 |
| §3.1 `grantCosmetics`, backfill by time, the three hooks and the GET catch-up, failures never fail a request | 3, 4, 5 |
| §3.2 the counters | 3 |
| §3.3 progress lines | 5 (counters), 8 (`progressLine`), 13 (tiles) |
| §4 the celebration line, the toast, the device list, "fresh" and seen, the dot, no push | 15, 5, 8, 13 |
| §5 the three endpoints, every error, concurrency, Apply set through PUT | 5, 14 |
| §6.1 entry points and the route | 13 |
| §6.2 layout, hotspots, info line, tabs, tray, "How new things arrive" | 13, 14 |
| §6.3 tiles | 13 |
| §6.4 behaviour (owned, locked, Apply set, Preview, Done with 403 / 409 / other, Cancel, paused loops) | 13, 14, 1 / 13 (paused) |
| §7 worn art and order, `Kip` / `PersonKip`, `useMyKip`, buddies' `kipWorn`, the camp's `faceWorn`, stories, small sizes, no icon / splash | 7, 8, 6, 12 |
| §8 code-authored art, layers, tint, loops, icons, the rendering budget, the size script, night | 9, 10, 11 |
| §9 B1 contents and `release: 2` handling | 2 (catalogue and export), 9–11 (art for B1 only) |
| §10 rules | Global Constraints |
| §11 tests | each task's Step 1; the guards in 3 (never removed), 8 (copy), 12 (worn), 13 (buttons) |
| §12 out of scope | not built |

**Where the spec cannot be followed literally against the real code, and the ruling taken:**
1. **The ids in Spec A's code are not the catalogue's.** Spec A shipped `plain_slate`, `dark_oak`, … Nothing was ever
   stored, so Task 1 renames them to the spec's `{prefix}.{key}` scheme (ruling 1).
2. **The v2 sets are whole scenes, not slot items.** `sets2.js` draws each set on its own layout (Cottage's table on the
   right, its stove mid-room, its fairy lights across the ceiling). Spec A's engine places items at fixed
   `SLOT_ANCHORS` with KIP's seat fixed, so each piece is redrawn inside its anchor and the fairy lights are the one
   anchor exemption (ruling 3). The Cottage room therefore matches the board's palette, pieces and loops, not its exact
   composition.
3. **Spec B §8 names the tint ramp `b, h, s, d`;** the real `KipRamp` is `body, light, shade, deep`. The art uses the
   real names; the boards' `@b @h @s @d` sprite codes map onto them (`tintOf`).
4. **Spec §4's toast "Decorate" button** cannot live in the existing `components/ui/toast.tsx` (pointer-less, pill
   shaped), so a separate host draws it (ruling 12).
5. **The frozen Campfire files** (Spec A's constraint) must change for spec §7's camp; only the worn prop changes
   (ruling 13, an owner note).
6. **The kipCopy guard flags "Cap"**, a release-1 item name; one exception is added (ruling 14).
7. **K4b's hotspot positions and v1 swatches are on the v1 130 × 80 layout and palettes;** the plan uses cells on the
   engine's layout and the v2 palettes (rulings 15, 20).
8. **A worn piece's ANIM flag (the party hat)** has no loop on KIP's grid without re-recording KIP's layer for every
   pose; dropped in v1 (ruling 4).
9. **The board piece counts** (Japanese 14, Home gym 12, …) do not equal 12 room pieces plus the worn pieces the
   boards list; the card computes its count (ruling 2). Only Cottage (13, matching) ships in release 1.
10. **Some release-1 sprites are wider than their anchor** (the pill organiser, the controller, the bean bag); they are
    redrawn narrower in the board's colours (Task 10).

**Placeholder scan:** no "TBD" / "TODO" / "similar to". Board sprites are referenced by file and line and copied
verbatim (they are committed beside the plan's spec); every non-sprite piece of art, every rule, route, store,
reducer, component and test is written out.

**Type consistency:** these names are used identically across tasks:
- backend: `COSMETIC_ITEMS`, `COSMETIC_SETS`, `cosmeticItem`, `STARTER_ROOM`, `EMPTY_WORN`, `STARTER_IDS`,
  `RELEASE_LAUNCHED_AT`, `UnlockFacts`, `planGrants`, `PlannedGrant`, `BACKFILL`, `loadUnlockFacts`, `countersOf`,
  `grantCosmetics`, `cosmeticsHooks`, `grantCosmeticsSafely`, `CosmeticsDTO`, `readCosmetics`, `saveCosmetics`,
  `markSeen`, `kipWornOf` (backend), `KipWornDTO`, `PersonDTO.kipWorn`, `checkedInKipWorn`, `faceWorn`;
- mobile: `CATALOGUE`, `catalogueItem`, `catalogueSet`, `catalogueItemsFor`, `drawableLoadout`, `RoomViewProps.paused`,
  `WornArt`, `wornArt`, `kipWornOf` (mobile), `wornForSize`, `SMALL_KIP_PT`, `wornIconRects`, `useMyKip`,
  `setMyKipWorn`, `resetMyKip`, `Cosmetics`, `loadCosmetics`, `saveCosmeticsLoadout`, `markCosmeticsSeen`,
  `useMyRoom`, `hasUnseen`, `ROOM_COPY`, `itemName`, `spokenName`, `unlockLine`, `unlockSpoken`, `progressLine`,
  `ProgressFacts`, `Look`, `DecorateState`, `decorateReducer`, `displayLook`, `isChanged`, `TileModel`, `tilesFor`,
  `infoLine`, `setCards`, `HOTSPOT_CELLS`, `hotspotPositions`, `useDecorateOpen`, `Toasted`, `nextToast`,
  `piecesForBadge`, `decorateTarget`, `SET_SWATCHES`, `ANCHOR_EXEMPT`, `checkSlotArt`.

**Review Focus:** each of the five lines has its pinning test in the owning task (Tasks 1, 3, 4, 5, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15).
The hotspot overlap and the largest-text decorate screen are also checked by eye in Task 16.
